### [1] [VideoKR: Towards Knowledge- and Reasoning-Intensive Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05259)
*Lin Fu,Zheyuan Yang,Yang Wang,Tingyu Song,Arman Cohan,Yilun Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了VideoKR，首个面向知识密集型和推理密集型视频理解的大规模训练语料库，包含31.5万视频推理样本和14.5万个专家领域视频，通过人机协同的技能导向生成流程构建，实验表明基于该数据的后训练方法在知识密集型视频推理上显著优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频理解数据集和模型在需要深层知识和复杂推理的视频任务上表现不足，缺乏专门针对知识推理密集型视频理解的大规模训练数据。

Method: 构建了VideoKR语料库（31.5万推理样本、14.5万个CC许可专家领域视频），设计人机协同的技能导向示例生成流程以逐步增强视频推理能力；同时构建了专家标注的评估基准VideoKR-Eval；采用标准SFT→GRPO训练流程进行后训练。

Result: 在标准SFT→GRPO流程下，基于VideoKR后训练的模型在知识密集型视频推理上优于现有后训练方法，且在通用视频推理上保持竞争力；消融实验验证了VideoKR各组成部分的贡献。

Conclusion: 数据设计是推动视频推理进步的关键驱动力，VideoKR为知识密集型视频推理提供了有效的数据支撑和 actionable insights。

Original Abstract: We introduce VideoKR, the first large-scale training corpus specifically designed to strengthen knowledge- and reasoning-intensive video understanding. It comprises 315K video reasoning examples over 145K newly collected, CC-licensed, expert-domain videos. We develop a human-in-the-loop, skill-oriented example generation pipeline that targets progressively deeper video reasoning capabilities while ensuring the difficulty, diversity, and reliability of both the examples and their CoT rationales. We also curate VideoKR-Eval, a new expert-annotated benchmark where questions require genuine video understanding and knowledge-intensive reasoning rather than textual shortcuts. Our experiments show that, under a standard SFT$\rightarrow$GRPO pipeline, models post-trained on VideoKR outperform prior post-training approaches on knowledge-intensive video reasoning while remaining competitive on general video reasoning, highlighting data design as a key driver of progress in video reasoning. We further conduct comprehensive ablations to isolate the contributions of VideoKR, providing actionable insights for future work.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了VideoKR，这是首个专门用于增强知识密集型和推理密集型视频理解的大规模训练语料库。它包含31.5万个视频推理示例，覆盖14.5万个新收集的、采用CC许可的专家领域视频。我们开发了一种人机协同的、面向技能导向的示例生成流程，该流程针对逐步深化的视频推理能力，同时确保示例及其思维链推理过程的难度、多样性和可靠性。我们还构建了VideoKR-Eval，一个新的专家标注基准，其中的问题要求真正的视频理解和知识密集型推理，而非文本捷径。我们的实验表明，在标准的SFT→GRPO流程下，基于VideoKR后训练的模型在知识密集型视频推理上优于先前的后训练方法，同时在通用视频推理上保持竞争力，这突出了数据设计是推动视频推理进步的关键驱动力。我们进一步进行了全面的消融实验以分离VideoKR各部分的贡献，为未来工作提供了可操作的见解。

</details>


### [2] [NIV: Neural Axis Variations for Variable Font Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05261)
*Nadav Benedek,Ariel Shamir,Ohad Fried*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出NIV方法，利用神经网络将静态字体自动转换为支持多轴连续插值的可变字体，仅需输入字形轮廓和设计轴即可预测逐点位移。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 构建可变字体需要大量人工专业排版设计，过程繁琐耗时；现有方法难以自动完成从静态字体到可变字体的转换。

Method: 提出NIV（Neural Axis Variations），直接在矢量字形几何上运行，通过新颖的Property Embedding机制捕获多轴交互，预测每个顶点的位移，实现统一框架下的多轴连续变化。

Result: 在Google可变字体构建的百万级数据集上训练，模型可泛化至未见过的码点、字体风格、高复杂度CJK字形，甚至手写输入；输出为标准可变字体文件。

Conclusion: NIV实现了从静态字体到可变字体的全自动转换，且展示了利用神经变形合成具有连续参数变化的结构化几何对象的通用方法。

Original Abstract: Variable fonts enable continuous variation of glyph geometry along semantic design axes such as weight, width, slant, and optical size. However, constructing a variable font from a static font remains a labor-intensive process requiring expert typographic design and manual specification of glyph variation data. We introduce NIV (Neural Axis Variations), a method that automatically converts a static font into a fully functional variable font. Given glyph outlines and a set of desired design axes, NIV predicts per-point displacements. The model operates directly on vector glyph geometry and employs a novel Property Embedding mechanism that captures interactions between multiple axes, enabling consistent multi-axis variation within a unified framework. We train NIV on a newly constructed dataset derived from variable Google Fonts, comprising over one million variation tuples. The resulting model generalizes across unseen code points, unseen font styles, high-complexity CJK glyphs, and even out-of-distribution handwriting inputs. The generated outputs are standard variable font files supporting continuous interpolation via existing rendering engines. To facilitate research, we release the dataset, the complete training and inference implementation, and trained models at https://github.com/ndvbd/NIV. Beyond typography, our approach demonstrates how structured geometric objects with continuous parametric variation can be synthesized using neural deformations.

中文摘要翻译: 可变字体能够沿重量、宽度、倾斜和光学尺寸等语义设计轴实现字形几何的连续变化。然而，从静态字体构建可变字体仍然是一个劳动密集型过程，需要专业的排版设计和字形变化数据的手动标注。我们提出了NIV（Neural Axis Variations），一种将静态字体自动转换为全功能可变字体的方法。给定字形轮廓和一组期望的设计轴，NIV预测每个顶点的位移。该模型直接在矢量字形几何上运行，并采用一种新颖的Property Embedding机制来捕获多轴之间的交互，从而在统一框架内实现一致的多轴变化。我们在从Google可变字体构建的新数据集上训练NIV，该数据集包含超过一百万个变化元组。所得模型能够泛化到未见过的码点、未见过的字体风格、高复杂度的CJK字形，甚至分布外的手写输入。生成的输出为标准可变字体文件，支持通过现有渲染引擎进行连续插值。为促进研究，我们在https://github.com/ndvbd/NIV上发布了数据集、完整的训练和推理实现以及训练好的模型。超越排版领域，我们的方法展示了如何利用神经变形来合成具有连续参数变化的结构化几何对象。

</details>


### [3] [A Vision-language Framework for Comparative Reasoning in Radiology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06407)
*Tengfei Zhang,Ziheng Zhao,Lisong Dai,Xiaoman Zhang,Pengcheng Qiu,Ya Zhang,Yanfeng Wang,Weidi Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出面向放射学实践的实体感知跨图像比较框架MedReCo和MedReCo-VLM，构建大规模多中心、多模态比较影像数据集MedReCo-DB，在检索和时序比较生成任务上显著优于现有方法，推动医学影像AI与临床实践更好对齐。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有医学影像AI主要聚焦于单图像孤立解读，缺乏对临床实践中跨时间、跨患者比较推理能力的支持，导致AI与真实放射学工作流程脱节。

Method: 首先构建MedReCo-DB大规模比较影像数据集（来自8个机构、4个国家、7种模态的69万+图像），将报告分解为解剖结构、异常发现和病理条件以提供实体级监督。基于此开发MedReCo（实体感知视觉编码器）用于可控临床类比病例检索，以及MedReCo-VLM（视觉-语言扩展模型）用于间隔变化的生成式解读。

Result: MedReCo在全部12个内部检索设置中取得Recall@1最优，外部检索平均提升6.0个百分点；在临床易混淆鉴别组中一致优于最强基线。MedReCo-VLM在所有比较生成评估中取得最佳性能，在胸片和CT上分别将纵向随访准确率提升14.5-46.5和13.0-27.9个百分点。

Conclusion: 实体感知的比较推理可以从常规临床数据中大规模学习，为医学影像AI提供更符合临床实践的基础能力。

Original Abstract: Medical imaging artificial intelligence has achieved strong performance in isolated image interpretation, but remains poorly aligned with radiological practice, where diagnosis and follow-up rely on comparison across prior studies and analogous reference cases. Here we formulate radiological comparison as an entity-aware cross-image reasoning problem and introduce a framework that supports both reference-case retrieval and temporal comparative interpretation. We construct MedReCo-DB, a large-scale comparative imaging resource derived from routine image-report pairs, comprising more than 690,000 images from over 160,000 patients across eight institutions, four countries and seven imaging modalities. Reports are decomposed into anatomical structures, abnormal findings and pathological conditions to provide supervision for entity-conditioned retrieval and comparative visual question answering. Using this resource, we develop MedReCo, an entity-aware visual encoder for controllable retrieval of clinically analogous cases, and MedReCo-VLM, a vision--language extension for generative interpretation of interval change. Across internal, external and cross-center evaluations, MedReCo achieved the highest Recall@1 in all 12 internal retrieval settings and improved external retrieval by a mean of 6.0 percentage points. In clinically confusable differential groups, it consistently outperformed the strongest baselines. MedReCo-VLM achieved the best performance across all comparative generation evaluations and improved longitudinal follow-up accuracy by 14.5-46.5 percentage points on chest radiographs and 13.0-27.9 percentage points on CT. These findings suggest that entity-aware comparative reasoning can be learned from routine clinical data at scale and may provide a more clinically aligned foundation for medical imaging AI.

中文摘要翻译: 医学影像人工智能在孤立图像解读方面已取得强劲性能，但仍与依赖跨既往研究和类比参考病例进行诊断和随访的放射学实践存在较大差距。本文将放射学比较形式化为实体感知的跨图像推理问题，并提出一个同时支持参考病例检索和时间比较解读的框架。我们构建了MedReCo-DB，一个大规模比较影像资源，来源于常规影像-报告配对数据，包含来自8个机构、4个国家和7种成像模态的超过69万张图像。报告被分解为解剖结构、异常发现和病理条件，为实体条件检索和比较视觉问答提供监督。利用这一资源，我们开发了MedReCo——一种用于可控检索临床类比病例的实体感知视觉编码器，以及MedReCo-VLM——一种用于间隔变化生成式解读的视觉-语言扩展模型。在内部、外部和跨中心评估中，MedReCo在所有12个内部检索设置中均取得最高的Recall@1，并将外部检索平均提升了6.0个百分点。在临床易混淆的鉴别诊断分组中，它持续优于最强基线。MedReCo-VLM在所有比较生成评估中取得了最佳性能，并在胸片上将纵向随访准确率提升了14.5-46.5个百分点，在CT上提升了13.0-27.9个百分点。这些发现表明，实体感知的比较推理可以从常规临床数据中大规模学习，并可能为医学影像AI提供更符合临床实践的基础能力。

</details>


### [4] [Personal AI Agent for Camera Roll VQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05275)
*Thao Nguyen,Krishna Kumar Singh,Donghyun Kim,Yong Jae Lee,Yuheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了面向个人相机胶卷的视觉问答任务（camroll数据集），并设计了配备分层记忆和工具集的高效对话AI代理（camroll-agent），实验表明该代理在长程个性化视觉记忆推理任务上优于多个基线方法，揭示了个性化视觉记忆与标准长程文本记忆之间的差距。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有AI代理在长程上下文理解方面已有研究，但针对个人相机胶卷这一高度个性化、跨多年、包含数百至数千张照片的视觉内容流，AI代理需要理解长时间跨度的个性化视觉记忆并进行高效导航定位，这一问题尚未被充分探索。作者旨在填补这一空白，构建真实场景下的数据集和高效的AI代理系统。

Method: 论文首先收集并人工标注了50位用户、31,476张图像和2,500个QA对，构建camroll数据集。然后设计了camroll-agent，一个配备分层记忆（hierarchical memory）和最小化工具集的对话AI代理，实现对大规模个性化视觉记忆的高效导航。

Result: camroll-agent在多个基线方法和长上下文理解AI代理系统中表现最优，验证了其在个性化视觉记忆推理任务上的有效性。实验结果揭示了个性化视觉记忆与标准长程文本记忆之间存在显著差距，尤其在一致性、视觉细节和用户特定上下文方面。

Conclusion: 个人相机胶卷视觉问答是一项具有挑战性的任务，个性化视觉记忆需要不同于标准长程文本记忆的处理方法。camroll数据集和camroll-agent共同揭示了当前AI代理在长程上下文推理方面的不足，特别是在需要保持一致性、理解视觉细节和用户特定上下文时，仍需进一步研究。

Original Abstract: We study the personal camera roll visual question answering setting. In this setting, a conversational AI assistant can access a user's personal camera roll and retrieve relevant photos to answer queries, ranging from simple factual questions (e.g., ``Name of the food I tried yesterday?'') to more open-ended ones (e.g., ``Recommend some dishes I have never eaten before''). Given the vast nature of the personal camera roll (i.e., multiple years, hundreds to thousands of photos), a successful AI assistant needs to understand a long-horizon, highly personalized visual content stream in order to navigate and locate the correct and/or relevant information. To support this, we collect and manually annotate questions that mimic real-world usage. The final dataset, camroll, contains 50 users, 31,476 images, and 2,500 QA pairs. We further design camroll-agent, a conversational AI agent equipped with hierarchical memory and a minimal set of tools for efficient navigation over large, personalized visual memory. Experimental results show that camroll-agent outperforms numerous baselines and methods for long-context understanding AI agents system. Together, the camroll dataset and camroll-agent highlight the gap in AI agents' long-context reasoning: personalized visual memory requires different approaches from standard long-context textual memory, especially when consistency, visual details, and user-specific context are present.

中文摘要翻译: 我们研究个人相机胶卷视觉问答设定。在该设定中，对话式AI助手可以访问用户的个人相机胶卷并检索相关照片来回答查询，涵盖从简单的事实性问题（例如“我昨天尝试的食物名称是什么？”）到更开放性问题（例如“推荐一些我从未吃过的菜肴”）。由于个人相机胶卷规模庞大（即跨越多年、包含数百至数千张照片），一个成功的AI助手需要理解长跨度、高度个性化的视觉内容流，以便导航并定位正确和/或相关的信息。为此，我们收集并人工标注了模拟真实使用场景的问题。最终数据集camroll包含50位用户、31,476张图像和2,500个QA对。我们进一步设计了camroll-agent，一个配备分层记忆和最小化工具集的对话AI代理，用于在大规模个性化视觉记忆上进行高效导航。实验结果表明，camroll-agent在多个基线方法和长上下文理解AI代理系统中表现优异。camroll数据集和camroll-agent共同揭示了AI代理在长上下文推理方面的差距：个性化视觉记忆需要不同于标准长上下文文本记忆的方法，尤其是在存在一致性、视觉细节和用户特定上下文的情况下。

</details>


### [5] [Do Models Share Safety Representations? Cross-Model Steering for Safe Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05290)
*Tobia Poppi,Silvia Cappelletti,Sara Sarto,Florian Schiffers,Garin Kessler,Marcella Cornia,Lorenzo Baraldi,Rita Cucchiara*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个跨模型安全导向框架，通过源LLM学习安全方向并迁移至目标生成模型，无需目标侧不安全数据即可实现安全控制。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有生成模型的安全控制方法大多依赖于特定模型，需要针对每种新架构重新训练或定制干预，缺乏可迁移、可复用的安全机制。

Method: (1) 在源大语言模型中从配对的安-不安全提示中估计安全方向；(2) 通过仅在良性数据上训练的轻量级对齐，将安全方向迁移至目标生成器；(3) 在推理时应用该方向进行安全引导；(4) 进一步提出多向量扩展，捕捉类别特定的安全行为。

Result: 在文本到图像和文本到视频生成任务中，跨模型迁移的安全方向在ASR降低和CLIP-Score/FID权衡上达到与目标模型本地学习的方向相当的表现，且无需目标侧不安全数据，生成质量不受损。

Conclusion: 安全相关的行为并非完全局限于单模型内部，而是可以通过跨模型一致的潜在方向进行控制，这为构建轻量级、可复用的安全机制开辟了新路径。

Original Abstract: Recent progress in generative modeling has made safety control a central challenge, yet existing approaches remain largely model-specific, requiring retraining or tailored interventions for each new architecture. In this work, we ask whether safety can be represented as a portable latent direction, learned once and reused across heterogeneous generators. We introduce the first framework for cross-model safety steering, in which a safety direction is estimated in a source LLM from paired safe-unsafe prompts, transported to a target generator through a lightweight alignment fitted on benign data alone, and applied at inference time. Crucially, our pipeline never accesses unsafe data on the target side, isolating whether safety can be transferred through shared representation geometry. Beyond a single global direction, we also identify a multi-vector extension that captures category-specific safety behaviors, enabling more selective control. We evaluate our approach in text-to-image and text-to-video generation across diverse source-target model pairs. Across models, transferred safety directions achieve ASR reduction and CLIP-Score/FID trade-offs comparable to directions learned natively on the target model using unsafe data, while requiring no target-side unsafe data. This indicates that safety improvements do not come at the expense of generation quality. Our results point to a modular view of safety: safety-relevant behavior is not purely model-local, but can be controlled through latent directions that persist across models. This suggests a new path toward lightweight, reusable safety mechanisms that do not require target-side unsafe data.

中文摘要翻译: 生成建模的最新进展使安全控制成为核心挑战，然而现有方法大多局限于特定模型，需要针对每种新架构重新训练或进行定制化干预。在这项工作中，我们探讨了安全性是否可以表示为一种可移植的潜在方向，仅需学习一次即可在不同异构生成器之间复用。我们提出了首个跨模型安全导向框架，其中安全方向从源大语言模型中的配对安全-不安全提示中估计，通过仅在良性数据上训练的轻量级对齐迁移至目标生成器，并在推理时应用。关键在于，我们的流程从未访问目标侧的不安全数据，从而探究安全性是否可以通过共享的表示几何进行迁移。在单一全局方向之外，我们还识别出一种多向量扩展，能够捕捉类别特定的安全行为，实现更具选择性的控制。我们在文本到图像和文本到视频生成任务中，评估了多种源-目标模型配对。实验表明，跨模型迁移的安全方向在ASR降低和CLIP-Score/FID权衡上，与在目标模型上使用不安全数据本地学习的方向表现相当，且无需目标侧不安全数据。这表明安全性的提升不会以牺牲生成质量为代价。我们的研究结果指向一种模块化的安全观：与安全相关的行为并非纯粹模型局部的，而是可以通过跨模型持续的潜在方向进行控制。这为构建不依赖目标侧不安全数据的轻量级、可复用安全机制提供了新路径。

</details>


### [6] [TopoPult-SSL: Gland-Mask-Free Cross-Device Meibomian Gland Segmentation via Self-Distilled Weak Clinical Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05347)
*Nicolò Savioli,Luca Del Tongo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TopoPult-SSL两阶段框架，利用廉价临床信号（眼睑轮廓、Pult分级等）实现跨设备睑板腺分割，无需目标域腺体掩码即可在第一阶段达到高精度，第二阶段通过自蒸馏进一步提升性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 临床新成像设备带来域偏移，密集腺体掩码标注昂贵，而睑板腺的廉价临床信号（眼睑轮廓、Pult分级、形态比率等）常规可获取。现有方法难以在无目标域腺体掩码条件下实现有效跨设备分割。

Method: 两阶段框架：阶段1利用目标眼睑掩码和临床元数据驱动的四个弱先验锚点，在训练损失中不含目标腺体掩码的情况下适配源域模型；阶段2在有目标腺体掩码时，通过监督式自蒸馏将互补的阶段1教师模型压缩为单个紧凑学生模型。

Result: 在MGD-1k到CAMG（1000→100张图像，不同设备）基准上，蒸馏模型Dice 0.716±0.006（最佳0.726），超越UA-MT（0.710）和集成教师（0.720），单次推理；无腺体掩码的阶段1变体Precision达0.694，显著优于SAM/MedSAM（0.30-0.34，p<0.001）。

Conclusion: TopoPult-SSL能在无需密集腺体轮廓标注的情况下实现跨设备睑板腺分割，为临床部署提供可行方案，代码已开源。

Original Abstract: Every new clinical imaging device creates a domain shift where dense gland masks are expensive yet cheap clinical signals -- eyelid outlines, Pult grades, morphometric ratios -- are routinely recorded. We present TopoPult-SSL, a two-stage framework for cross-device meibomian gland segmentation. Stage 1 adapts a source-trained model without target gland masks in the training loss, using four weak-prior anchors driven by target eyelid masks and clinical metadata only. Stage 2, when target gland masks are available, distils complementary Stage-1 teachers into a single compact student via supervised self-distillation. We develop and validate the technique on the public MGD-1k to CAMG research benchmark (1,000 to 100 images, different device), where the distilled model achieves Dice 0.716+/-0.006 (best 0.726), surpassing UA-MT (0.710) and the ensemble teacher (0.720) -- with a single pass. The gland-mask-free Stage-1 variant reaches Precision 0.694 vs. 0.30-0.34 for SAM/MedSAM (p<0.001), enabling deployment without dense gland contouring. Code and reproducibility scripts are released.

中文摘要翻译: 每一台新的临床成像设备都会带来域偏移，其中密集的腺体掩码标注成本高昂，而廉价的临床信号——眼睑轮廓、Pult分级、形态比率——却是常规记录的。我们提出TopoPult-SSL，一种用于跨设备睑板腺分割的两阶段框架。阶段1在训练损失中不使用目标腺体掩码，仅通过目标眼睑掩码和临床元数据驱动的四个弱先验锚点来适配源域训练模型。阶段2在目标腺体掩码可用时，通过监督式自蒸馏将互补的阶段1教师模型蒸馏到一个紧凑的学生模型中。我们在公开的MGD-1k到CAMG研究基准（1000张到100张图像，不同设备）上开发和验证该技术，蒸馏模型取得了Dice 0.716±0.006（最佳0.726），超越了UA-MT（0.710）和集成教师（0.720）——且仅需单次推理。无腺体掩码的阶段1变体Precision达到0.694，而SAM/MedSAM仅为0.30-0.34（p<0.001），使得无需密集腺体轮廓标注即可部署。代码和可复现脚本已公开。

</details>


### [7] [LightVesselNet: An Ultra-Lightweight Sub-100K Parameter Network for Retinal Blood Vessel Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05354)
*Shadman Sobhan,Farhana Jalil*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LightVesselNet是一种轻量级视网膜血管分割网络，仅含75K参数，通过紧凑编码器-解码器架构、注意力机制、多尺度特征聚合和亚像素上采样，在五个公开数据集上取得了与大型模型竞争的性能，适合部署于资源受限的边缘设备。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有深度学习视网膜血管分割模型精度高但计算开销大，难以在边缘设备上实际部署，因此需要设计一种高效轻量化的网络以适应资源受限环境。

Method: 采用紧凑的编码器-解码器架构，结合通道注意力和空间注意力机制、瓶颈处的多尺度特征聚合模块、解码器中的亚像素上采样策略，以及专门设计的边缘残差连接以保留细小的血管细节。

Result: 在DRIVE、STARE、CHASEDB1、FIVES和HRF五个数据集上，灵敏度分别达到0.8189、0.8499、0.8640、0.8634、0.8096，Dice系数分别达到0.8070、0.8072、0.8181、0.8649、0.7686，在性能-参数量或性能-计算量方面优于现有方法，跨数据集评估验证了泛化能力。

Conclusion: LightVesselNet在保持高分割精度的同时显著降低了模型复杂度，是低资源临床场景和移动筛查工具中极具潜力的部署候选方案。

Original Abstract: Retinal blood vessel segmentation plays a vital role in the early detection of diabetic retinopathy and glaucoma. While recent deep learning models have achieved great segmentation accuracy, they typically require heavy computational resources, making real-world deployment on edge devices difficult. In this paper, we propose LightVesselNet, an efficient neural network designed for retinal vessel segmentation in a resource-constrained environment. Despite containing only 75K parameters, LightVesselNet performs competitively with much larger models. The network employs a compact encoder decoder architecture enhanced with channel and spatial attention mechanisms, a multi-scale feature aggregation module at the bottleneck, and a subpixel upsampling strategy in the decoder. A dedicated edge residual connection preserves fine vessel detail throughout decoding. Extensive experiments on five publicly available datasets: DRIVE, STARE, CHASEDB1, FIVES, and HRF, yield sensitivity scores of 0.8189, 0.8499, 0.8640, 0.8634, 0.8096, and Dice coefficients of 0.8070, 0.8072, 0.8181, 0.8649, and 0.7686, respectively. LightVesselNet shows improved efficiency (Performance vs Parameter or GFlops) compared to State-of-the-Art models. Cross-dataset evaluation confirms the model's generalisation capability. Overall, LightVesselNet is a strong candidate for deployment in low-resource clinical settings and mobile screening tools.

中文摘要翻译: 视网膜血管分割在糖尿病视网膜病变和青光眼的早期检测中起着至关重要的作用。虽然最近的深度学习模型取得了很高的分割精度，但它们通常需要大量的计算资源，使得在实际边缘设备上的部署变得困难。本文提出了LightVesselNet，一种为资源受限环境下视网膜血管分割设计的高效神经网络。尽管仅包含75K个参数，LightVesselNet的性能与更大的模型相竞争。该网络采用紧凑的编码器-解码器架构，并增强了通道和空间注意力机制、瓶颈处的多尺度特征聚合模块以及解码器中的亚像素上采样策略。一个专门的边缘残差连接在整个解码过程中保留了精细的血管细节。在五个公开数据集（DRIVE、STARE、CHASEDB1、FIVES和HRF）上的大量实验分别得到了0.8189、0.8499、0.8640、0.8634、0.8096的灵敏度，以及0.8070、0.8072、0.8181、0.8649和0.7686的Dice系数。LightVesselNet在效率（性能与参数量或GFlops之比）上优于现有最先进模型。跨数据集评估证实了模型的泛化能力。总体而言，LightVesselNet是低资源临床环境和移动筛查工具中部署的有力候选方案。

</details>


### [8] [Recovering Physically Plausible Human-Object Interactions from Monocular Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05359)
*Dingbang Huang,Etienne Vouga,Qixing Huang,Georgios Pavlakos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RePHO提出了一种基于物理引导的重建框架，通过强化学习策略优化从单目视频中重建物理合理的人-物交互序列，有效解决了穿透和物体漂浮等物理不真实问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的基于运动学的方法虽然能生成视觉上合理的运动，但经常产生物理上不真实的伪影，如相互穿透和物体漂浮。为克服这些问题，本文引入物理引导的重建框架。

Method: 首先进行运动学估计，然后通过强化学习训练一个策略来在物理模拟器中复现交互过程。由于运动学估计通常包含噪声，直接进行RL训练可能失败，因此提出一种自适应采样策略，带有双重自更新机制，用于识别最具信息量和最可靠的运动学重建帧。该过程逐步提升重建质量，生成物理一致的人-物交互序列。

Result: 在两个标准人-物交互基准数据集上进行了验证，在物理合理性指标上相比现有最先进方法取得了显著提升。

Conclusion: RePHO能够有效生成物理一致的人-物交互序列，通过自适应采样和双重自更新机制解决了噪声运动学估计下的RL训练难题，为单目视频中物理合理的人-物交互重建提供了有效方案。

Original Abstract: In this paper, we propose RePHO, a method to reconstruct physically plausible human-object interactions (HOI) from monocular videos. While existing kinematic-based approaches produce visually plausible motion, they often result in physically implausible artifacts such as interpenetration and object floating. To overcome these issues, we introduce a physics-guided reconstruction framework. We begin with a kinematic estimate and then refine it by training a policy with reinforcement learning (RL). This policy is optimized to reproduce the interaction in a physics simulator. Because kinematic estimates are typically noisy, naive RL training can fail. Therefore, we propose an adaptive sampling strategy with a dual self-updating mechanism that can identify the frames with the most informative and reliable kinematic reconstruction. Our process progressively improves reconstruction quality and yields physically consistent HOI sequences. We demonstrate our approach on two standard HOI benchmarks and achieve clear improvements in physical plausibility metrics over state-of-the-art methods. Project Page: https://dingbang777.github.io/RePHO/

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出RePHO，一种从单目视频中重建物理合理的人-物交互（HOI）的方法。现有的基于运动学的方法虽然能生成视觉上合理的运动，但经常产生物理上不真实的伪影，如相互穿透和物体漂浮。为克服这些问题，我们引入了一个物理引导的重建框架。我们从运动学估计开始，然后通过强化学习（RL）训练一个策略进行优化。该策略被优化以在物理模拟器中复现交互过程。由于运动学估计通常包含噪声，直接进行RL训练可能会失败。因此，我们提出一种自适应采样策略，带有双重自更新机制，用于识别最具信息量和最可靠的运动学重建帧。该过程逐步提升重建质量，生成物理一致的人-物交互序列。我们在两个标准HOI基准数据集上展示了我们的方法，并在物理合理性指标上相比现有最先进方法取得了显著提升。项目页面：https://dingbang777.github.io/RePHO/

</details>


### [9] [Biomazon: A Multimodal Dataset for 3D Forest Structure and Biomass Modeling in the Amazon Basin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05368)
*Sayan Mandal,Rocco Sedona,Simon Besnard,Mikhail Urbazaev,Morris Riedel,Ehsan Zandi,Gabriele Cavallaro*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了Biomazon——亚马逊盆地首个20米分辨率多模态基准数据集，用于联合预测GEDI RH廓线（有序百分位）和AGBD，并建立了共享编码器-解码器基线框架，通过消融实验评估骨干网络、模态贡献和辅助嵌入的影响。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有ML方法大多将树冠高度代理（如RH95/RH98）或AGBD作为独立标量预测，而非学习森林垂直结构的有序廓线，缺乏一个多模态ML基准来联合预测完整GEDI RH廓线与AGBD，或评估强制RH百分位间物理一致排序的方法。

Method: 构建了Biomazon多模态基准数据集（20米分辨率，覆盖亚马逊盆地），包含GEDI RH和AGBD目标以及多传感器预测因子（Sentinel-1/2、ALOS-2 PALSAR-2、Copernicus DEM、Dynamic World LULC、AlphaEarth嵌入），采用标准化空间划分和评估协议。使用共享编码器-解码器结合任务特定头的基线框架，进行全面的消融研究。

Result: 通过消融实验量化了（i）骨干网络/模型规模、（ii）模态贡献、（iii）辅助嵌入在独立和融合设置下的效果；报告了单目标和联合目标结果以量化统一训练协议下的权衡；通过与现有网格化产品（GEDI L4D RH10-RH98和AGBD）的区域对齐比较，将基线性能置于上下文中。

Conclusion: Biomazon及其配套协议和基线结果，为热带森林中结构一致的RH廓线预测和结构-生物量建模的未来研究建立了参考基准。

Original Abstract: Accurate, spatially explicit characterization of tropical forest structure is essential for carbon accounting and ecosystem monitoring, yet most ML pipelines predict canopy-top height proxies (e.g., RH95/RH98) or AGBD as separate scalar targets, rather than learning the forest vertical structure as an ordered profile. The community lacks a ML-ready multimodal benchmark for predicting the entire GEDI RH profile jointly with AGBD, or for evaluating methods that enforce physically consistent ordering across RH percentiles. We address this with Biomazon, a 20 m multimodal benchmark dataset over the Amazon Basin that pairs GEDI RH and AGBD targets with multi-sensor predictors (Sentinel-1/2, ALOS-2 PALSAR-2, Copernicus DEM, Dynamic World LULC, and AlphaEarth embeddings) under standardized spatial splits and evaluation protocols. Using a shared encoder-decoder with task-specific heads as a baseline framework, we conduct a comprehensive ablation study of (i) backbone/model scale, (ii) modality contributions, and (iii) the use of auxiliary embeddings under standalone and fusion settings, and we report both single-target and joint-target results to quantify tradeoffs under a unified training protocol. Finally, we contextualize baseline performance through regionally aligned comparisons against existing gridded products, including GEDI L4D RH10-RH98 and AGBD, at matching temporal scale. Biomazon, together with the accompanying protocols and baseline results, establishes a reference benchmark for future work on structurally consistent RH-profile prediction and structure-biomass modeling in tropical forests.

中文摘要翻译: 对热带森林结构进行精确、空间显式的表征对于碳核算和生态系统监测至关重要。然而，大多数机器学习流程将冠层顶部高度代理（如RH95/RH98）或地上生物量密度（AGBD）作为独立的标量目标进行预测，而非将森林垂直结构作为有序廓线进行学习。该领域缺乏一个机器学习就绪的多模态基准，用于联合预测完整的GEDI RH廓线与AGBD，或评估强制RH百分位间物理一致性排序的方法。我们提出的Biomazon填补了这一空白——一个覆盖亚马逊盆地的20米分辨率多模态基准数据集，将GEDI RH和AGBD目标与多传感器预测因子（Sentinel-1/2、ALOS-2 PALSAR-2、Copernicus DEM、Dynamic World土地利用土地覆盖和AlphaEarth嵌入）配对，并采用标准化的空间划分和评估协议。我们采用共享编码器-解码器搭配任务特定头的基线框架，开展了全面的消融研究，涵盖(i)骨干网络/模型规模、(ii)模态贡献，以及(iii)在独立和融合设置下辅助嵌入的使用，并报告了单目标和联合目标结果，以量化统一训练协议下的权衡。最后，通过与现有网格化产品（包括GEDI L4D RH10-RH98和AGBD）在匹配时间尺度上进行区域对齐比较，对基线性能进行了上下文化评估。Biomazon及其配套协议和基线结果，为未来在热带森林中进行结构一致的RH廓线预测和结构-生物量建模的研究建立了参考基准。

</details>


### [10] [Three-Dimensional Retinal Microvasculature Restoration in OCT Angiography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05375)
*Yukun Guo,Min Gao,Tristan T. Hormel,Steven T. Bailey,Thomas S. Hwang,Yali Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于深度学习的算法，从单次OCTA体数据中恢复毛细血管解剖血管结构，显著提升图像质量和微血管 fidelity。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有OCTA图像质量提升方法主要关注噪声抑制、投影伪影去除或信号增强，忽略了三维血管结构的完整性，难以可靠量化视网膜血流和无灌注区域。

Method: 采用EfficientNet-B5作为编码器，结合并发空间和通道squeeze-and-excitation模块的解码器，通过跳跃连接保持空间分辨率。使用三个相邻B帧作为输入预测恢复后的中间B帧。

Result: 与原始单次OCTA相比，模型显著提升PSNR（26.16 vs 22.23）和SSIM（0.91 vs 0.72）；在2D和3D微血管fidelity上Dice系数分别提升至少3.8%和51.2%。

Conclusion: 所提深度学习模型能有效从单次OCTA体数据中恢复毛细血管解剖结构，显著改善图像质量和微血管量化准确性。

Original Abstract: Optical coherence tomographic angiography (OCTA) is a powerful technique for imaging retinal microvasculature. However, acquiring reliable quantification of retinal blood flow and areas of retinal nonperfusion is challenging because of imaging artifacts. Existing methods primarily focus on noise suppression, projection artifact removal, or signal enhancement to improve the image quality of OCTA in cross-sectional or two-dimensional (2D) en face projections, while neglecting the intrinsic three-dimensional vascular architecture. In this study, we propose a deep learning-based algorithm for restoring capillary anatomical vasculature from a single OCTA volume. The network consists of an EfficientNet-B5 encoder and a decoder incorporating concurrent spatial and channel squeeze-and-excitation modules, connected via skip connections to preserve spatial resolution. Three adjacent B-frames are used as input to predict the restored middle B-frame. We evaluated the performance of the model using the peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR) and structural similarity index measure (SSIM) against ground truth generated from averaging multiple scans. The results show that the proposed model significantly (both p < 0.001) improved image quality compared with the original single OCTA volume, with a PSNR of 26.16 +/- 1.26 vs. 22.23 +/- 0.78 and an SSIM of 0.91 +/- 0.02 vs. 0.72 +/- 0.03. The proposed model also significantly (p < 0.001) improved microvascular fidelity, measured by the Dice coefficient overlap between the model output and ground truth, in both 2D and 3D by at least 3.8% and 51.2%, respectively, across several different vascular slabs.

中文摘要翻译: 光学相干断层扫描血管造影（OCTA）是一种用于视网膜微血管成像的强大技术。然而，由于成像伪影的存在，获取可靠的视网膜血流量和无灌注区域量化具有挑战性。现有方法主要关注通过噪声抑制、投影伪影去除或信号增强来改善OCTA在横截面或二维（2D）正面投影中的图像质量，而忽略了内在的三维血管结构。在本研究中，我们提出了一种基于深度学习的算法，用于从单次OCTA体数据中恢复毛细血管解剖血管结构。该网络由一个EfficientNet-B5编码器和一个采用并发空间与通道squeeze-and-excitation模块的解码器组成，通过跳跃连接来保持空间分辨率。使用三个相邻B帧作为输入，预测恢复后的中间B帧。我们使用峰值信噪比（PSNR）和结构相似性指数（SSIM）评估模型性能，以平均多次扫描生成的图像作为真值。结果表明，与原始单次OCTA体数据相比，所提模型显著（p < 0.001）改善了图像质量，PSNR从22.23±0.78提升至26.16±1.26，SSIM从0.72±0.03提升至0.91±0.02。在多个不同血管分层中，模型输出与真值之间的Dice系数重叠测量显示，所提模型在2D和3D微血管保真度上分别显著（p < 0.001）提升了至少3.8%和51.2%。

</details>


### [11] [Deep Learning-assisted AMD Staging based on OCT and OCT Angiography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05379)
*Yukun Guo,Tristan T. Hormel,An-Lun Wu,Liqin Gao,Min Gao,Steven T. Bailey,Yali Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文利用OCT和OCTA数据，开发并评估了基于深度学习的年龄相关性黄斑变性（AMD）严重程度自动分级模型。三种模型（生物标志物图、2D投影、3D体积）均表现出优秀的AMD分期性能（QWK≥0.83），其中基于生物标志物的模型综合性能最佳（QWK=0.85±0.03），且对早期AMD检测尤为有效。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 利用深度学习模型对AMD的严重程度进行自动分级，以辅助临床诊断，并探索不同输入模态（生物标志物图、2D投影、3D体积）对分级性能的影响。

Method: 收集271名参与者（≥50岁）的OCT/OCTA数据，根据AREDS简化严重程度量表将AMD分为四个阶段。开发三种深度学习模型：基于分割病理特征（视网膜液、玻璃膜疣、地图样萎缩、黄斑新生血管）的生物标志物图模型、基于2D en face OCT/OCTA投影的模型、以及基于3D OCT/OCTA体积的模型。使用EfficientNet架构，结合归一化输入、数据增强和五折交叉验证进行训练。

Result: 分析了271名参与者351只眼的2030个OCT/OCTA体积。所有模型均表现出优秀的AMD分期性能（QWK≥0.83）。生物标志物图模型综合性能最佳（QWK=0.85±0.03），对早期AMD检测F1-score为0.59±0.14。3D模型与2D模型性能相当（QWK=0.83±0.04 vs 0.83±0.09），2D模型精度最高（0.79±0.06），最准确识别无AMD的眼睛。

Conclusion: 基于OCT/OCTA数据的深度学习模型能够准确自动地对AMD严重程度进行分级。其中，生物标志物图模型综合性能最均衡，对早期AMD检测具有特别价值。

Original Abstract: To develop and evaluate deep learning models for automated grading of age-related macular degeneration (AMD) severity using optical coherence tomography (OCT) and OCT angiography (OCTA) data. Two hundred seventy-one participants aged >= 50 years with varying AMD severities. Central macular 6 x 6 mm OCT/OCTA volumes were acquired using a swept-source OCTA system (SOLIX; Visionix/Optovue Inc., CA). AMD severity was graded into four stages (No AMD, Early AMD, Intermediate AMD, and Advanced AMD) according to the AREDS simplified severity scale. Three deep learning models were developed using different input modalities: (1) biomarker maps derived from segmented pathological features, including retinal fluid, drusen, geographic atrophy (GA), and macular neovascularization (MNV); (2) two-dimensional (2D) en face OCT and OCTA projections; and (3) three-dimensional (3D) OCT/OCTA volumes. EfficientNet-based architectures were trained using normalized inputs, data augmentation, and five-fold cross-validation. A total of 2,030 OCT/OCTA volumes from 351 eyes of 271 participants were analyzed. All models demonstrated strong AMD staging performance with substantial agreement with the reference standard (QWK >= 0.83). The biomarker-based model achieved the highest overall performance (QWK = 0.85 +/- 0.03, mean +/- standard deviation) and the best detection of early AMD (F1-score = 0.59 +/- 0.14). The 3D model achieved performance comparable to the 2D OCT/OCTA model (QWK = 0.83 +/- 0.04 vs. 0.83 +/- 0.09), while the 2D OCT/OCTA model showed the highest precision (0.79 +/- 0.06) and most accurately identified eyes without AMD. Deep learning models using OCT/OCTA data can accurately and automatically grade AMD severity. Among the evaluated approaches, the biomarker-based model provided the most balanced performance and showed particular value for early AMD detection.

中文摘要翻译: 目的：利用光学相干断层扫描（OCT）和OCT血管成像（OCTA）数据，开发并评估用于年龄相关性黄斑变性（AMD）严重程度自动分级的深度学习模型。方法：纳入271名年龄≥50岁、具有不同AMD严重程度的参与者。使用扫频源OCTA系统（SOLIX；Visionix/Optovue公司，加州）采集中央黄斑区6×6 mm的OCT/OCTA容积图像。根据AREDS简化严重程度量表，将AMD严重程度分为四个阶段（无AMD、早期AMD、中期AMD和晚期AMD）。开发了三种使用不同输入模态的深度学习模型：（1）基于分割病理特征（包括视网膜液、玻璃膜疣、地图样萎缩和黄斑新生血管）导出的生物标志物图；（2）二维（2D）en face OCT和OCTA投影图像；（3）三维（3D）OCT/OCTA容积图像。采用基于EfficientNet的架构，结合归一化输入、数据增强和五折交叉验证进行训练。结果：共分析了来自271名参与者351只眼睛的2030个OCT/OCTA容积图像。所有模型均表现出强大的AMD分期性能，与参考标准具有高度一致性（QWK≥0.83）。基于生物标志物的模型取得了最佳综合性能（QWK=0.85±0.03，均值±标准差），并在早期AMD检测中表现最优（F1分数=0.59±0.14）。3D模型的性能与2D OCT/OCTA模型相当（QWK=0.83±0.04 vs. 0.83±0.09），而2D OCT/OCTA模型显示出最高的精确率（0.79±0.06），并能最准确地识别无AMD的眼睛。结论：基于OCT/OCTA数据的深度学习模型能够准确自动地对AMD严重程度进行分级。在评估的多种方法中，基于生物标志物的模型提供了最均衡的性能，在早期AMD检测方面展现出特别的价值。

</details>


### [12] [UniPixie: Unified and Probabilistic 3D Physics Learning via Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05399)
*Qilin Huang,Quynh Anh Huynh,Long Le,Chen Wang,Chuhao Chen,Ryan Lucas,Eric Eaton,Lingjie Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UNIPIXIE通过将物理属性预测重构为可控连续分布学习，在PIXIEMULTIVERSE数据集上训练，实现了从单张图像到多物理求解器兼容的材料场生成，将杨氏模量预测误差降低超50%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有前馈网络仅能从视觉外观预测单一物理属性（点估计），无法捕捉真实世界中固有的物理模糊性（即同一外观可能对应多种物理属性）。

Method: 提出UNIPIXIE框架：1）构建PIXIEMULTIVERSE数据集，沿物体从最软到最硬的光谱学习连续参数化路径；2）通过单一直觉参数实现可控的、多样化的物理合理材料场生成；3）提出统一架构，为多种物理求解器（连续介质MPM、基于LBS的降阶变形、锚点弹簧-质量系统）生成仿真就绪参数。

Result: 不仅生成了丰富多样的合理动力学结果，还将杨氏模量预测误差相较于最强确定性基线降低了超过50%。

Conclusion: UNIPIXIE弥合了静态点估计与物理现实连续本质之间的鸿沟，为物理属性预测提供了从确定性到可控连续分布的重要范式转变。

Original Abstract: Existing feed-forward networks excel at predicting a single set of physical properties from visual appearance, but this point-estimate paradigm fundamentally fails to capture the real world's inherent physical ambiguity. We address this by reframing physics prediction as a task of learning a controllable, continuous distribution of material properties. We introduce UNIPIXIE, a framework trained to predict a continuous and parameterized path of physically plausible material properties from a single visual input. By learning a direct mapping along an object's softest-to-stiffest spectrum on our PIXIEMULTIVERSE dataset, UNIPIXIE allows for controllable generation of diverse, physically valid material fields via a single intuitive parameter. Crucially, UNIPIXIE introduces a novel unified architecture to produce simulation-ready parameters for diverse physics solvers, including continuum-based Material Point Method (MPM), reduced-order deformation based on Linear Blend Skinning (LBS), and anchor-based Spring-Mass systems, addressing a key portability issue in prior work. Experiments show our approach not only generates a rich variety of plausible dynamics but also reduces Young's Modulus prediction error by over 50% against the strongest deterministic baseline, bridging the gap between static point estimates and the continuous nature of physical reality. Project page: https://unipixie.github.io/

中文摘要翻译: 现有的前馈网络擅长从视觉外观预测单一组物理属性，但这种点估计范式从根本上无法捕捉真实世界固有的物理模糊性。我们通过将物理预测重构为学习可控的、连续的材料属性分布任务来解决这一问题。我们提出UNIPIXIE，一个经过训练可从单张视觉输入预测连续且参数化的物理合理材料属性路径的框架。通过在PIXIEMULTIVERSE数据集上学习沿物体从最软到最硬光谱的直接映射，UNIPIXIE允许通过单一直觉参数可控地生成多样化的、物理有效的材料场。关键在于，UNIPIXIE引入了一种新颖的统一架构，为多种物理求解器（包括基于连续介质的物质点法（MPM）、基于线性混合蒙皮（LBS）的降阶变形以及基于锚点的弹簧-质量系统）生成仿真就绪参数，解决了先前工作中关键的可移植性问题。实验表明，我们的方法不仅生成了丰富多样的合理动力学结果，还将杨氏模量预测误差相较于最强确定性基线降低了超过50%，弥合了静态点估计与物理现实连续本质之间的鸿沟。项目页面：https://unipixie.github.io/

</details>


### [13] [Would you still call this Dax? Novel Visual References in VLMs and Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05409)
*Ada Defne Tür,Gaurav Kamath,Joyce Chai,Siva Reddy,Benno Krojer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个新颖视觉参考数据集（NVRD），包含19,176张图像和90个视觉概念，用于研究视觉语言模型（VLM）在接触新视觉概念后如何将视觉参考映射到语言。实验发现，当新概念与预训练知识冲突时，模型难以在上下文中学习；且模型对视觉扰动的泛化表现出过度泛化，与人类判断存在显著差异。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有研究主要关注对熟悉概念的视觉增强，而VLM如何将全新的视觉参考映射到语言，尤其是在与预训练知识矛盾时，这一过程尚未被充分探索。

Method: 构建了NVRD数据集：包含19,176张图像，涵盖90个视觉概念，每个概念有多达20个递增扰动版本以测试泛化能力。评估了3个开源和2个闭源VLM，同时收集了2,400个人类判断，进行人机对比。

Result: (i) 当新概念与预训练先验知识矛盾时，模型难以在上下文中学习新概念；(ii) 模型与人类对视觉扰动的敏感性相关，但模型显著过度泛化，将学到的标签扩展到人类拒绝的刺激上。

Conclusion: 贡献了NVRD数据集，作为研究人类和机器视觉概念学习的语料库和基准。

Original Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs), like human learners, are frequently exposed to new visual concepts, but how they map novel visual references to language after exposure remains largely underexplored, particularly when those references contradict prior knowledge from pre-training. To study this, we present the Novel Visual References Dataset (NVRD): 19,176 images spanning 90 visual concepts across different levels of visual novelty, each with up to 20 increasingly perturbed versions of the original object to probe generalization. Unlike prior work on visual augmentations of familiar concepts, NVRD comprises entirely novel, open-ended stimuli constructed from scratch, mirroring how humans encounter genuinely new concepts. We evaluate 3 open- and 2 closed-source models alongside 2,400 human judgments for direct human-model comparison, and find that (i) models struggle to acquire novel concepts in-context when they contradict prior knowledge, and (ii) while models and humans show correlated sensitivity to visual perturbations, models significantly overgeneralize, extending learned labels to stimuli that humans reject. We contribute NVRD as a corpus and benchmark for research on visual concept learning in both humans and machines.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉语言模型（VLM）如同人类学习者，经常暴露于新的视觉概念之下，但它们在接触这些概念后如何将新颖的视觉参考映射到语言，这一问题在很大程度上仍未得到充分探索，特别是当这些参考与预训练阶段的先验知识相矛盾时。为研究这一问题，我们提出了新颖视觉参考数据集（NVRD）：包含19,176张图像，涵盖90个视觉概念，这些概念处于不同的视觉新颖性水平，每个概念最多有20个递增扰动的原始对象版本，以探索泛化能力。与以往关于熟悉概念视觉增强的工作不同，NVRD包含从头构建的完全新颖、开放式的刺激，模拟人类遇到真正新概念的方式。我们评估了3个开源模型和2个闭源模型，同时收集了2,400个人类判断，用于直接的人机比较。我们发现：(i) 当新概念与先验知识矛盾时，模型难以在上下文中学习新颖概念；(ii) 虽然模型和人类对视觉扰动的敏感性呈现相关性，但模型显著过度泛化，将学到的标签扩展到人类拒绝接受的刺激上。我们贡献NVRD作为研究人类和机器视觉概念学习的语料库和基准。

</details>


### [14] [Disentangled Fine-Grained Prototype Learning for Incomplete Image-Tabular Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05455)
*Feixiang Zhou,Jianyang Xie,Zhuangzhi Gao,Qinkai Yu,Fu Wang,Yuheng Fan,Jing Li,Zheheng Jiang,Yitian Zhao,Yanda Meng,He Zhao,Gregory Y. H. Lip,Yalin Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DFPL框架，通过细粒度原型学习解决图像-表格多模态学习中的模态缺失问题。核心包括共享-特定原型建模（SSPM）实现原型级解耦、原型引导的细粒度对齐（PFA）保持分布与语义一致性，以及类别感知多尺度聚合（CMA）自适应融合多层级特征。在三个基准上超越现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法通过全局token平均特征的解耦与对齐学习模态不变表示，仅捕获粗粒度的跨模态一致性，忽略了图像与表格模态间细粒度的语义和分布错位，导致在模态缺失场景下无法充分利用互补线索。

Method: 1) 共享-特定原型建模（SSPM）：提取紧凑且多样化的共享原型和模态特定原型，在原型层面进行解耦以抑制冗余的模态内相关性。2) 原型引导的细粒度对齐（PFA）：在统一原型空间内联合进行原型级分布匹配和原型到类别的语义对齐，保持细粒度分布与语义一致性。3) 类别感知多尺度聚合（CMA）：从全局和原型层面自适应聚合共享语义与模态特定特征，实现鲁棒预测。

Result: 在三个不同的图像-表格多模态基准上，在多种模态缺失设置下，所提方法显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过细粒度原型学习有效解决了高度异构的图像-表格模态在缺失场景下的对齐与融合问题，为多模态学习提供了一种新的范式。

Original Abstract: The missing-modality problem poses a significant challenge in image-tabular multimodal learning across a wide range of multimedia applications, including product understanding, recommendation systems, and medical diagnosis. This challenge is particularly pronounced when the two modalities are highly heterogeneous, as images and tabular attributes differ substantially in their semantic granularity and data distributions. Existing methods learn modality-invariant representations through disentanglement and alignment over global token-averaged features, capturing only coarse cross-modal consistency and overlooking fine-grained semantic and distributional misalignment, which hampers the exploitation of complementary cues under missing modalities. To address this, we propose DFPL, a novel framework for fine-grained prototype learning. Specifically, Shared-Specific Prototype Modeling (SSPM) extracts compact and diverse shared and modality-specific prototypes, and further performs prototype-level disentanglement to suppress redundant intra-modality correlations. Additionally, we propose a Prototype-guided Fine-grained Alignment (PFA) module that jointly enforces prototype-level distribution matching and prototype-to-class semantic alignment within a unified prototype space, thereby preserving both fine-grained distributional and semantic consistency across modalities. We further introduce a Class-aware Multi-scale Aggregation (CMA) module to adaptively aggregate shared semantics and modality-specific characteristics from global and prototype levels for robust predictions. Extensive experiments on three diverse image-tabular benchmarks demonstrate the superiority of our method compared to the previous approaches under various missing-modality settings. Code will be made publicly available.

中文摘要翻译: 模态缺失问题在广泛的 multimodal 应用中（包括产品理解、推荐系统和医学诊断）对图像-表格多模态学习构成了重大挑战。当两种模态高度异构时，这一问题尤为突出，因为图像与表格属性在语义粒度和数据分布上存在显著差异。现有方法通过对全局token平均特征进行解耦和对齐来学习模态不变表示，仅捕获了粗粒度的跨模态一致性，忽视了细粒度的语义与分布错位，这阻碍了在模态缺失场景下对互补线索的利用。为解决这一问题，我们提出DFPL，一种用于细粒度原型学习的新型框架。具体地，共享-特定原型建模（SSPM）提取紧凑且多样化的共享原型和模态特定原型，并进一步在原型层面进行解耦以抑制冗余的模态内相关性。此外，我们提出原型引导的细粒度对齐（PFA）模块，在统一的原型空间内联合执行原型级分布匹配和原型到类别的语义对齐，从而在模态间保持细粒度的分布与语义一致性。我们还引入类别感知多尺度聚合（CMA）模块，从全局和原型层面自适应地聚合共享语义和模态特定特征，以实现鲁棒预测。在三个不同的图像-表格多模态基准上的大量实验表明，在多种模态缺失设置下，我们的方法相较于先前方法具有优越性。代码将公开提供。

</details>


### [15] [Horse Eye Blink Detection and Classification for Equine Affective State Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05458)
*João Alves,Signe Møller-Skuldbøl,Pia Haubro Andersen,Rikke Gade*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对马匹面部动作单元（AU）中眨眼动作的自动检测问题，提出了三种方法（基于帧的YOLOv12检测器、光流幅度阈值法和微调VideoMAE模型），在公开数据集上实现了眨眼分类宏F1=0.898、二值眨眼检测F1=0.926，展示了细粒度AU检测在马匹健康监测中的潜力与挑战。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 马匹面部动作单元（AU）的自动检测是评估马匹疼痛和情感状态的有前景但探索不足的方向。半眨眼和全眨眼是疼痛与压力的可靠指标，但由于其作为微表情的细微特性，肉眼难以捕捉，仅能通过逐帧视频检查才能识别，因此实现可靠的视频自动检测极具挑战性。

Method: 本文开发并评估了三种马匹视频眨眼自动分类方法：(1) 基于帧的YOLOv12检测器；(2) 光流幅度阈值化方法；(3) 微调后的VideoMAE模型。所有方法均在公开数据集上进行测试。

Result: 在眨眼分类任务上取得宏F1分数0.898，在二值眨眼检测任务上取得F1分数0.926。

Conclusion: 实验结果突显了细粒度面部动作单元（AU）检测在马匹健康监测中的潜力，同时也揭示了该任务固有的挑战。

Original Abstract: Automated detection of equine facial action units (AUs) is a promising yet under-explored avenue for pain and affective state assessment in horses. Half and full-blink movements are recognised indicators of pain and stress, but as micro-expressions, their subtle, fine-grained nature makes them easily missed by the naked eye and only discernible through frame-by-frame video inspection, making reliable automated detection from video a particularly demanding task. We develop and evaluate three methods for automated blink classification from horse videos: a frame-based YOLOv12 detector, an optical flow magnitude thresholding approach, and a fine-tuned VideoMAE model, tested on a publicly available dataset. We achieve a macro-F1 score of 0.898 when doing blink classification and 0.926 on binary blink detection. Our results highlight both the potential and the inherent challenges of fine-grained AU detection for equine welfare monitoring.

中文摘要翻译: 自动检测马匹面部动作单元（AUs）是评估马匹疼痛和情感状态的一个有前景但尚未充分探索的方向。半眨眼和全眨眼运动被认为是疼痛和压力的可靠指标，但由于它们属于微表情，其细微、精细的特性使得肉眼难以捕捉，仅能通过逐帧视频检查才能识别，这使得从视频中进行可靠的自动检测成为一项极具挑战性的任务。本文开发并评估了三种从马匹视频中进行眨眼自动分类的方法：基于帧的YOLOv12检测器、光流幅度阈值化方法以及微调后的VideoMAE模型，并在公开数据集上进行了测试。我们在眨眼分类任务上取得了0.898的宏F1分数，在二值眨眼检测任务上取得了0.926的F1分数。我们的结果突显了细粒度AU检测在马匹健康监测中的潜力及其固有的挑战。

</details>


### [16] [ORACLE-CT: Anatomy-Aware Support Pooling for CT Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05460)
*Lavsen Dahal,Yubraj Bhandari,Geoffrey Rubin,Joseph Y. Lo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ORACLE-CT提出了一种基于解剖感知的聚合框架，通过多器官分割实现标签特定的注意力池化，提升了腹部CT疾病分类的性能和外部泛化能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 腹部CT疾病分类中，诊断证据通常局限于特定器官或解剖区域，但现有研究级分类器使用与解剖无关的池化或注意力机制聚合编码器特征，导致局部化疾病证据与全局证据聚合之间存在不匹配。

Method: 提出ORACLE-CT框架，一种编码器无关的解剖感知聚合框架，利用多器官分割定义标签特定的解剖支持区域，并将注意力池化限制在相关区域内，支持单器官、多器官联合、比较性、局部化和全局五种支持策略。

Result: 在MERLIN上，支持掩码池化将DINOv3的宏AUROC/AUPRC从0.838/0.638提升至0.858/0.676，I3D-ResNet-121从0.829/0.617提升至0.848/0.659。在外部数据集上，DINOv3在Duke-Abdomen上从0.802/0.628提升至0.835/0.683，在AMOS上从0.742/0.313提升至0.762/0.350。

Conclusion: ORACLE-CT框架在提升疾病分类判别能力和外部鲁棒性的同时，保持了预测与解剖证据之间的可审计关联。

Original Abstract: Abdominal CT disease classification is challenging because each scan is a large 3D volume with many possible findings, while diagnostic evidence is often confined to specific organs or anatomical compartments. Most study-level classifiers aggregate encoder features using anatomy-agnostic pooling or attention, creating a mismatch between localized disease evidence and global evidence aggregation. We propose ORACLE--CT, an encoder-agnostic anatomy-aware aggregation framework that uses multi-organ segmentation to define label-specific anatomical supports and restrict attention pooling to relevant regions. The framework supports single-organ, multi-organ union, comparative, localized, and global support strategies.
  We evaluate ORACLE--CT with three encoder families: DINOv3, I3D--ResNet-121, and the radiology-native Pillar--0 encoder. Models are trained end-to-end on MERLIN and evaluated internally and under frozen external transfer to Duke--Abdomen and AMOS. Compared with global average pooling, support-masked pooling improved MERLIN macro-AUROC/AUPRC from 0.838/0.638 to 0.858/0.676 for DINOv3 and from 0.829/0.617 to 0.848/0.659 for I3D--ResNet-121. On harmonized 10-label external evaluation, DINOv3 improved on Duke--Abdomen from 0.802/0.628 to 0.835/0.683 and on AMOS from 0.742/0.313 to 0.762/0.350, with similar gains for I3D--ResNet-121. For Pillar--0, most gains came from learned attention, with smaller additional benefit from anatomical masking. ORACLE--CT improves discrimination and external robustness while preserving an auditable link between predictions and anatomical evidence.

中文摘要翻译: 腹部CT疾病分类具有挑战性，因为每个扫描都是包含大量可能发现的大规模三维体积，而诊断证据通常局限于特定器官或解剖隔室。大多数研究级分类器使用与解剖无关的池化或注意力机制聚合编码器特征，造成了局部化疾病证据与全局证据聚合之间的不匹配。我们提出ORACLE-CT，一种编码器无关的解剖感知聚合框架，利用多器官分割定义标签特定的解剖支持区域，并将注意力池化限制在相关区域内。该框架支持单器官、多器官联合、比较性、局部化和全局五种支持策略。我们使用三种编码器家族评估ORACLE-CT：DINOv3、I3D-ResNet-121和放射学原生Pillar-0编码器。模型在MERLIN上进行端到端训练，并在内部评估以及在冻结外部迁移条件下对Duke-Abdomen和AMOS进行评估。与全局平均池化相比，支持掩码池化将DINOv3的MERLIN宏AUROC/AUPRC从0.838/0.638提升至0.858/0.676，将I3D-ResNet-121从0.829/0.617提升至0.848/0.659。在统一的10标签外部评估中，DINOv3在Duke-Abdomen上从0.802/0.628提升至0.835/0.683，在AMOS上从0.742/0.313提升至0.762/0.350，I3D-ResNet-121也获得了类似的提升。对于Pillar-0，主要增益来自学习到的注意力，解剖掩码带来的额外收益较小。ORACLE-CT在提升判别能力和外部鲁棒性的同时，保持了预测与解剖证据之间的可审计关联。

</details>


### [17] [Formal Concept Lattices are Good Semantic Scaffolds for Concept-Based Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05471)
*Deepika SN Vemuri,Sayanta Adhikari,Ankit Saha,Krishn Vishwas Kher,Vineeth N Balasubramanian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出利用形式概念分析中的概念格作为语义支架，引导神经网络在不同深度层次上学习具有层级结构的概念表示，从而提升模型的可解释性和干预能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有概念模型通常将所有概念视为在同一网络层学习的扁平、无结构集合，忽略了人类语义理解中概念从一般到特殊的分层组织这一基本特性。虽然深度网络确实学习了视觉特征的层次结构，但这种结构很少与显式的语义层次对齐。

Method: 借鉴形式概念分析（Formal Concept Analysis），利用形式概念格（formal concept lattices）提供原则性的语义支架来指导神经网络学习。概念格能根据概念的泛化程度自然地确定其在网络中的学习位置，从而使模型在整个网络深度中逐步发展出具有语义基础的层次化表示。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上的实验结果表明，该方法生成了更可解释的嵌入表示，支持更有效的干预操作，并且学习到的概念表示既富有语义意义又具有层次结构。

Conclusion: 将形式概念格与神经网络结合，能够引导模型学习与人类语义层次对齐的结构化概念表示，显著提升模型的可解释性和干预效果。

Original Abstract: Learning semantics is essential for deep learning models to be interpretable and better aligned with human reasoning. Concept-based models approach this by representing classes through meaningful semantic abstractions, but typically treat all concepts as a flat, unstructured set learned at a single neural network layer. This overlooks a fundamental property of human semantic understanding: concepts being organized hierarchically, from general to specific. While deep networks do learn a hierarchy of visual features, this structure is rarely aligned with explicit semantic hierarchies. Drawing on Formal Concept Analysis, we demonstrate that formal concept lattices provide principled semantic scaffolds to guide neural network learning. These lattices naturally identify where in the network concepts should be learned based on their level of generality. This allows the model to develop staged, semantically grounded representations throughout its depth. Empirical results on real-world datasets show that our models produce more interpretable embeddings, support more effective interventions, and learn concept representations that are both meaningful and hierarchically structured.

中文摘要翻译: 学习语义对于深度学习模型实现可解释性以及与人类推理更好对齐至关重要。基于概念的模型通过有意义的语义抽象来表示类别，但通常将所有概念视为在单一神经网络层学习的扁平、无结构集合。这忽略了人类语义理解的一个基本特性：概念从一般到特殊分层组织。虽然深度网络确实学习了视觉特征的层次结构，但这种结构很少与显式的语义层次对齐。借鉴形式概念分析，我们证明形式概念格提供了原则性的语义支架来指导神经网络学习。这些概念格根据概念的泛化程度自然地确定其在网络中的学习位置。这使得模型能够在其整个深度中逐步发展出具有语义基础的层次化表示。在真实世界数据集上的实验结果表明，我们的模型生成了更可解释的嵌入表示，支持更有效的干预操作，并且学习到的概念表示既富有语义意义又具有层次结构。

</details>


### [18] [Can We Predict The Human Preference For Text-to-Image Content Prior To Generation And Is It Even Useful To Do So?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05478)
*Joong Ho Kim,Keith G. Mills*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了扩散模型中初始随机噪声对生成质量的影响，并提出在生成前预测人类偏好评分（HPM）以优化生成质量，且硬件开销可忽略不计。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 扩散模型的随机噪声直接影响生成质量，尤其在小型本地部署场景中更加明显。现有方法需消耗大量计算资源生成后才能评估质量，因此作者希望探索是否能在投入计算资源之前预测生成质量，并利用这种预测来提升生成效果。

Method: 提出在扩散模型实际生成之前，先预测标量人类偏好指标（HPM）分数，然后利用该预测来引导或优化生成过程，以提高输出图像质量。同时比较了不同HPM指标在此任务中的适用性。

Result: 实验表明：1）可以在生成前有效预测HPM分数；2）利用这种预测可以改善生成图像质量；3）实现该方法的硬件开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 在扩散模型中提前预测人类偏好评分是可行且高效的，能够在不显著增加计算负担的前提下提升生成质量，为本地部署等资源受限场景提供了一种实用优化策略。

Original Abstract: Diffusion Models (DM) have revolutionized text-driven generation by enabling the synthesis of high-quality, photorealistic visual content from user prompts. Whereas prior advances in visual generation such as VAEs and GANs were primarily evaluated on perceptual or visual similarity metrics such as FID PSNR, DM advances have fostered the development of more advanced Human Preference Metrics (HPM) that model and quantify human judgment as scalar values. However, DMs synthesize content using an inherently stochastic process where random noise seeds generation. The initial random noise directly affects the quality of generated outputs, both qualitatively and quantitatively. This influence is pronounced in smaller models for local deployment scenarios. Given this phenomenon, we first investigate to what extent we can predict scalar HPM scores prior to committing compute resources for generation. Further, we then investigate to what extent we can leverage such prediction to improve the quality of generated images, and also study which HPMs are best suited for this task. Our investigation reveals that not only is this possible, but that it is feasible to achieve negligible hardware overhead.

中文摘要翻译: 扩散模型（DM）通过从用户提示中生成高质量、逼真的视觉内容，彻底改变了文本驱动的生成技术。以往的视觉生成进展（如VAE和GAN）主要通过感知或视觉相似性指标（如FID、PSNR）进行评估，而DM的进步催生了更先进的人类偏好指标（HPM），这些指标将人类判断建模并量化为标量值。然而，DM在合成内容时采用了一种固有的随机过程，其中随机噪声种子决定了生成结果。初始随机噪声会直接定性和定量地影响生成输出的质量。这种影响在用于本地部署的小型模型中尤为显著。基于这一现象，我们首先研究了在投入计算资源进行生成之前，能在多大程度上预测标量HPM分数。进一步地，我们研究了能在多大程度上利用这种预测来提升生成图像的质量，并探讨了哪些HPM最适合此任务。我们的研究表明，不仅这种预测是可行的，而且实现该任务的硬件开销可以忽略不计。

</details>


### [19] [LLM-Guided ANN Index Optimization for Human-Object Interaction Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05489)
*Shahrzad Esmat,Chaunte W. Lacewell,Sameh Gobriel,Nilesh Jain,Ali Jannesari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于大语言模型的分阶段智能体方法，用于优化多阶段检索系统中高度耦合的超参数，在HICO-DET基准上比Optuna TPE提升33.3%，吞吐量比UniIR提升15.3倍。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统超参数优化方法（如TPE、高斯过程贝叶斯优化）基于独立性假设，无法有效探索多阶段检索系统中高度耦合的参数空间，需要一种能感知完整优化历史的方法来联合优化耦合参数。

Method: 提出一种分阶段感知的大语言模型智能体，将优化过程划分为探索、利用和微调三个阶段，在每个阶段基于完整的优化历史来条件化生成下一组候选超参数，从而导航耦合参数空间。

Result: 在HICO-DET上，相比Optuna TPE提升33.3%，比VDTuner提升34.2%，吞吐量比UniIR提升15.3倍。在耦合度高的HICO-DET上优势显著，在耦合度适中的GLDv2和近独立的SIFT1M上与基线收敛。跨系统迁移到Milvus后无需修改仍排名第一。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的分阶段感知智能体能有效克服传统HPO方法的独立性假设局限，在高度耦合的参数空间中取得显著性能提升，且具有良好的跨平台迁移能力，优势随参数耦合度增强而增大。

Original Abstract: Retrieval systems underpin modern AI applications -- spanning visual search, recommendation engines, and multi-modal question answering. Modern multi-stage retrieval systems require the joint optimization of highly coupled parameters, yet traditional hyperparameter optimization (HPO) methods -- including Tree-structured Parzen Estimators (TPE) and Gaussian Process Bayesian Optimization -- rely on an independence assumption that fundamentally prevents them from navigating these coupled configuration spaces. We address this limitation with a phase-aware large language model (LLM) agent that conditions each proposal on its full optimization history, navigating the coupled parameter space across phase-partitioned exploration, exploitation, and fine-tuning stages. Evaluated on the HICO-DET human-object interaction retrieval benchmark using Intel VDMS (Visual Data Management System), our agent outperforms Optuna TPE by +33.3% and VDTuner by +34.2% under SIEVE (Safeguarded Index Evaluation of Vector-search Efficiency, a quality-constrained throughput metric), delivering a 15.3x throughput gain over UniIR. Validation across three benchmarks confirms that the agent's advantage grows with the degree of parameter coupling: +33.3% on HICO-DET (high coupling), methods converge within 1% on GLDv2 (moderate coupling) and within 3.6% on SIFT1M (near-independent control). Cross-system validation on Milvus confirms the optimizer ranks first on all three datasets without modification, demonstrating transferability across vector database management system (VDBMS) platforms.

中文摘要翻译: 检索系统支撑着现代人工智能应用——涵盖视觉搜索、推荐引擎和多模态问答。现代多阶段检索系统需要联合优化高度耦合的参数，然而传统的超参数优化方法——包括树结构帕尔森估计器（TPE）和高斯过程贝叶斯优化——依赖于独立性假设，这从根本上阻碍了它们在这些耦合配置空间中的导航能力。我们通过一种分阶段感知的大语言模型（LLM）智能体来应对这一局限，该智能体基于完整的优化历史对每个提案进行条件化，在分阶段的探索、利用和微调阶段中导航耦合参数空间。在采用Intel VDMS（视觉数据管理系统）的HICO-DET人-物交互检索基准上评估时，我们的智能体在SIEVE（向量搜索效率的安全索引评估，一种质量约束的吞吐量指标）下分别优于Optuna TPE 33.3%和VDTuner 34.2%，相比UniIR实现了15.3倍的吞吐量增益。跨三个基准的验证表明，该智能体的优势随参数耦合程度的增加而增大：在HICO-DET（高耦合）上提升33.3%，在GLDv2（中等耦合）上方法收敛于1%以内，在SIFT1M（近独立控制）上收敛于3.6%以内。在Milvus上的跨系统验证证实，该优化器无需修改即可在所有三个数据集上排名第一，展示了跨向量数据库管理系统（VDBMS）平台的可迁移性。

</details>


### [20] [Unpaired RGB-Thermal Gaussian-Splatting Using Visual Geometric Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05491)
*Jean Cordonnier,Chenghao Xu,Olga Fink,Malcolm Mielle*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需配对校准的多模态RGB-热成像新视角合成框架，利用VGGT独立估计相机位姿并通过Procrustes算法对齐，结合多模态3D高斯泼溅方法，在保持RGB保真度的同时实现了热成像视图的合成，并建立了评估跨模态一致性的基准框架。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有RGB-热成像新视角合成方法依赖精确校准的配对图像或立体设备，限制了可扩展性和实际部署。为解决这一问题，本文旨在实现无需配对校准的RGB-热成像新视角合成。

Method: 首先利用VGGT（3D前馈Transformer架构）独立估计每种模态的相机位姿；然后通过Procrustes算法结合跨模态特征匹配进行位姿对齐，实现无需配对校准的联合配准；最后提出一种多模态3D高斯泼溅方法，从非配对的RGB和热图像中直接学习以实现新视角合成。

Result: 在多种场景上的实验表明，该方法在热成像视图合成中取得了有竞争力的性能，同时保持了RGB保真度。此外，现有重建方法会产生缺乏跨模态一致性的模态特异性重建结果。

Conclusion: 提出了一种无需配对校准的RGB-热成像多模态新视角合成框架，并引入了一个基准框架来严格评估单模态图像合成和多模态重建场景的一致性。

Original Abstract: Multi-modal novel view synthesis (NVS) combining RGB and thermal imagery enables precise 3D scene reconstruction with visual and thermal information. However, existing methods typically rely on precisely calibrated RGB-thermal image pairs or stereo setups, limiting scalability and practical deployment. To address this, we introduce a framework for unpaired RGB-thermal NVS that leverages VGGT, a 3D feed-forward transformer architecture, to independently estimate camera poses for each modality. The pose sets are then aligned using the Procrustes algorithm with a cross-modal feature matcher, enabling joint registration without paired calibration. Building on this alignment, we further propose a multi-modal 3D Gaussian Splatting approach that learns directly from unpaired RGB and thermal images. Experiments on diverse scenes demonstrate that our method achieves competitive performance in thermal view synthesis while maintaining RGB fidelity. Moreover, we show that existing reconstruction approaches can produce modality-specific reconstructions that lack cross-modal consistency. We thus introduce a benchmarking framework to rigorously evaluate both per-modality image synthesis and the multi-modal coherence of reconstructed scenes.

中文摘要翻译: 结合RGB和热成像的多模态新视角合成（NVS）能够利用视觉和热信息实现精确的3D场景重建。然而，现有方法通常依赖于精确校准的RGB-热成像图像对或立体设备，限制了可扩展性和实际部署。为解决这一问题，我们引入了一个用于非配对RGB-热成像NVS的框架，该框架利用VGGT——一种3D前馈Transformer架构——独立估计每种模态的相机位姿。然后通过Procrustes算法结合跨模态特征匹配器对这些位姿集进行对齐，无需配对校准即可实现联合配准。在此对齐基础上，我们进一步提出了一种多模态3D高斯泼溅方法，直接从非配对的RGB和热图像中学习。在多种场景上的实验表明，我们的方法在热成像视图合成中取得了有竞争力的性能，同时保持了RGB保真度。此外，我们发现现有的重建方法会产生缺乏跨模态一致性的模态特异性重建结果。因此，我们引入了一个基准框架，用于严格评估每种模态的图像合成以及重建场景的多模态一致性。

</details>


### [21] [Robust Scene Transfer for PointGoal Navigation via Privileged Sensor Guided Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05506)
*Amirhossein Zhalehmehrabi,Tiziano Tezze,Alberto Castelini,Alessandro Farinelli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种传感器引导的自适应对比学习框架，在PointGoal导航中利用特权LiDAR信号引导视觉表征学习，使编码器捕获导航相关结构而非场景外观，显著提升策略在跨场景迁移中的泛化能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视觉导航方法依赖大量标注数据或大规模预训练视觉模型，但视觉编码器容易过拟合于场景特定的外观特征，缺乏导航任务相关的几何结构感知，导致在场景迁移时泛化性能不佳。

Method: 提出传感器引导的自适应对比学习框架：(1) 在训练阶段，利用特权LiDAR传感信号通过几何感知相似度度量和自适应温度缩放指导对比学习目标；(2) 将训练好的编码器独立预训练后冻结，作为强化学习的感知骨干网络，将表征学习与策略优化解耦；(3) 引入表征预训练与策略学习之间的跨阶段域不匹配，抑制环境特定捷径，促进对任务相关特征的依赖。

Result: 在高保真仿真实验中，该方法在多样化的室内外环境中显著提升了策略级的场景迁移能力；仅依赖单目RGB观测与标准任务输入（目标位置、本体感知信号），无需LiDAR等特权传感器，性能优于大规模预训练视觉模型和标准对比学习基线，尤其在严重外观和语义偏移场景下优势明显。

Conclusion: 通过传感器引导的自适应对比学习解耦视觉表征学习与策略优化，能够使视觉编码器学习到导航相关的几何结构信息，有效提升跨场景泛化能力，为无特权传感器的部署提供了有效方案。

Original Abstract: We propose a sensor-guided adaptive contrastive learning framework for visual representation learning in PointGoal navigation. During training, privileged LiDAR sensing guides the contrastive objective through a geometry-aware similarity metric and adaptive temperature scaling, encouraging visual embeddings to capture navigation-relevant structure rather than scene-specific appearance. The resulting encoder is pretrained independently, frozen, and used as the perceptual backbone for reinforcement learning, decoupling representation learning from policy optimization. We further introduce a cross-stage domain mismatch between representation pretraining and policy learning to suppress environment-specific shortcuts and promote reliance on task-relevant features.
  Extensive experiments in high-fidelity simulation demonstrate that our approach significantly improves policy-level scene transfer across diverse indoor and outdoor environments. At deployment, the agent relies only on monocular RGB observations together with standard task-related inputs such as goal position and proprioceptive signals, without access to LiDAR or other privileged sensors. Our method outperforms large pretrained vision models and standard contrastive baselines under severe appearance and semantic shifts. We also release a multimodal dataset to support future research on privileged-guided visual representation learning for navigation. The code is available at:

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了一种传感器引导的自适应对比学习框架，用于PointGoal导航中的视觉表征学习。在训练阶段，特权LiDAR传感通过几何感知相似度度量与自适应温度缩放引导对比学习目标，促使视觉嵌入捕获导航相关的结构信息而非场景特定的外观特征。所得的编码器被独立预训练后冻结，并作为强化学习的感知骨干网络，从而将表征学习与策略优化解耦。我们进一步引入表征预训练与策略学习之间的跨阶段域不匹配，以抑制环境特定的捷径，并促进对任务相关特征的依赖。在高保真仿真中的大量实验表明，我们的方法在多样化的室内外环境中显著提升了策略级别的场景迁移能力。在部署时，智能体仅依赖单目RGB观测以及目标位置和本体感知信号等标准任务输入，无需LiDAR或其他特权传感器。我们的方法在严重的外观和语义偏移场景下优于大规模预训练视觉模型和标准对比学习基线。我们还发布了一个多模态数据集，以支持未来关于特权引导的导航视觉表征学习的研究。代码已开源。

</details>


### [22] [BRepCLIP: Contrastive Multimodal Pretraining on BRep Primitives for CAD Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05515)
*Muhammad Usama,Didier Stricker,Mohammad Sadil Khan,Muhammad Zeshan Afzal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BRepCLIP，首个将CAD边界表示（BRep）与语言和图像嵌入通过对比预训练对齐的框架。通过将CAD对象编码为面和边令牌序列，使用Transformer编码器聚合为全局BRep嵌入，并与CLIP对齐，显著提升了检索和零样本分类性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 3D表示学习主要集中在点云和网格上，而CAD的原生格式——边界表示（BRep），其编码了精确的参数化曲面、曲线及其拓扑结构，作为表示学习基座几乎没有受到关注。作者旨在填补这一空白，实现BRep几何与多模态（语言、图像）嵌入的对齐。

Method: 将每个CAD对象建模为面令牌和边令牌的序列，分别为曲面和曲线几何构建独立的离散词汇表，并增强空间和语义描述符（如曲面类型：圆柱、环面、NURBS；曲线基元：直线、圆弧、B样条）。使用Transformer编码器聚合这些令牌得到全局BRep嵌入，通过联合对比目标与CLIP的文本和图像编码器对齐。

Result: 相比现有基于点云的方法，在ABC、CADParser和Automate数据集上Top-1检索分别提升40.4%、22.0%和23.9%；在FabWave上零样本分类Top-1提升15%。

Conclusion: BRepCLIP能够生成更具判别性和语义基础性的嵌入，作为CAD感知的相似性度量可用于评估文本和图像条件驱动的CAD生成任务，证明了结构感知预训练对于多模态CAD理解的重要性。

Original Abstract: Learning representations of CAD models is a largely open problem. While 3D representation learning has flourished around point clouds and meshes, the native format of CAD - boundary representations BReps, which encodes exact parametric surfaces, curves, and their topology, has received little attention as a representation learning substrate. We introduce BRepCLIP, the first framework to align BRep geometry with language and image embeddings through contrastive pretraining. We model each CAD object as a sequence of face and edge tokens with separate discrete vocabularies for surface and curve geometry, augmented with spatial and semantic descriptors that capture surface types (e.g., cylindrical, torus, NURBS) and curve primitives (e.g., line, arc, B-spline). A transformer encoder aggregates these tokens into a global BRep embedding, aligned with CLIP's text and image encoders via a joint contrastive objective. BRepCLIP generates more discriminative and semantically grounded embeddings than existing point-based alternatives, improving Top-1 retrieval over OpenShape by 40.4%, 22.0%, and 23.9% on ABC, CADParser, and Automate, respectively, and improving zero-shot classification on FabWave by 15% in Top-1 score. We further demonstrate its utility as a CAD-aware similarity metric for evaluating text and image-conditioned CAD generation, establishing the importance of structure-aware pretraining for multimodal CAD understanding. Project page is available at https://muhammadusama100.github.io/BrepClip2026/

中文摘要翻译: 学习CAD模型的表示在很大程度上是一个悬而未决的问题。尽管3D表示学习已在点云和网格领域蓬勃发展，但CAD的原生格式——边界表示（BRep），它编码了精确的参数化曲面、曲线及其拓扑结构，作为表示学习基座却鲜少受到关注。我们提出了BRepCLIP，这是首个通过对比预训练将BRep几何与语言和图像嵌入进行对齐的框架。我们将每个CAD对象建模为面令牌和边令牌的序列，并为曲面和曲线几何构建了独立的离散词汇表，同时增强了捕捉曲面类型（如圆柱面、环面、NURBS）和曲线基元（如直线、圆弧、B样条）的空间和语义描述符。一个Transformer编码器将这些令牌聚合为全局BRep嵌入，并通过联合对比目标与CLIP的文本和图像编码器进行对齐。与现有的基于点云的替代方法相比，BRepCLIP能够生成更具判别性和语义基础性的嵌入，在ABC、CADParser和Automate数据集上Top-1检索分别提升了40.4%、22.0%和23.9%，在FabWave数据集上零样本分类的Top-1分数提升了15%。我们进一步展示了其作为CAD感知相似性度量在评估文本和图像条件驱动的CAD生成任务中的用途，证明了结构感知预训练对于多模态CAD理解的重要性。项目页面见https://muhammadusama100.github.io/BrepClip2026/

</details>


### [23] [Almieyar-Oryx-BloomBench: A Bilingual Multimodal Benchmark for Cognitively Informed Evaluation of Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05531)
*Mohammad Mahdi Abootorabi,Omid Ghahroodi,Anas Madkoor,Marzia Nouri,Doratossadat Dastgheib,Mohamed Hefeeda,Ehsaneddin Asgari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BloomBench 是一个基于认知学（Bloom分类法）的双语（英-阿）多模态基准，用于系统诊断VLM在六个认知层级上的能力，发现当前模型存在认知不对称性（语义理解强、事实回忆与创意合成弱）以及跨语言推理差距。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有VLM基准评估任务碎片化、缺乏认知深度，无法揭示模型的真正推理弱点，也无法为针对性改进提供指导。

Method: 基于Bloom分类法，设计六个认知层级（记忆、理解、应用、分析、评价、创造）的图像-问题-答案任务，采用半自动化流水线构建数据集，并通过分层混合质量保证协议进行验证，确保可扩展性、文化包容性和语言保真度。

Result: (1) 当前最先进VLM在语义理解层表现强劲，但在事实回忆和创意合成层表现不佳，呈现显著的认知不对称性；(2) 阿拉伯语与英语之间存在显著的跨语言多模态推理性能差距。

Conclusion: 当前通用多模态能力掩盖了VLM在特定认知层面的深层缺陷，本研究为开发认知对齐且更具包容性的VLM奠定了基础。

Original Abstract: Despite the rapid progress of Vision-Language Models (VLMs), the field lacks benchmarks that rigorously diagnose their true reasoning abilities and chart meaningful progress toward human-like multimodal intelligence. Most existing evaluations focus on piecemeal or disconnected tasks, obscuring critical cognitive weaknesses and providing little insight for targeted improvement. To address this gap, we introduce BloomBench, part of the Almieyar benchmarking series, the first cognitively human-grounded, bilingual (English-Arabic) multimodal benchmark for VLMs. Grounded in Bloom's Taxonomy, BloomBench systematically evaluates six levels of cognition (Remember, Understand, Apply, Analyze, Evaluate, Create) through carefully designed image-question-answer tasks. Built with a semi-automated pipeline and validated through a stratified hybrid quality assurance protocol, it ensures scalability, cultural inclusivity, and linguistic fidelity. Leveraging this framework, we conduct a comprehensive study of state-of-the-art VLMs to diagnose their cognitive profiles. Our analysis reveals a sharp cognitive asymmetry: while state-of-the-art models achieve strong performance ceilings in semantic understanding, they struggle substantially with factual recall and creative synthesis. This demonstrates that current general multimodal proficiency masks deeper limitations in specific cognitive layers. Furthermore, our study highlights a critical performance gap between Arabic and English, exposing limitations in current cross-lingual multimodal reasoning. These findings establish a foundation for developing more cognitively aligned and inclusive VLMs. The benchmark framework and dataset is available at: https://github.com/qcri/Almieyar-Oryx-BloomBench.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管视觉-语言模型（VLM）取得了快速进展，但该领域仍缺乏能够严格诊断其真正推理能力并衡量向类人多模态智能迈进的基准。大多数现有评估关注碎片化或不连贯的任务，掩盖了关键认知弱点，且对针对性改进提供的洞察甚少。为解决这一差距，我们引入BloomBench——Almieyar基准系列的一部分——首个基于人类认知基础的双语（英语-阿拉伯语）多模态基准。该基准植根于Bloom分类法，通过精心设计的图像-问题-答案任务，系统性地评估六个认知层级（记忆、理解、应用、分析、评价、创造）。借助半自动化流水线构建，并通过分层混合质量保证协议验证，确保了可扩展性、文化包容性和语言保真度。利用这一框架，我们对最先进VLM进行全面研究以诊断其认知剖面。我们的分析揭示了一种尖锐的认知不对称性：最先进模型在语义理解方面达到强劲的性能上限，但在事实回忆和创意合成方面表现挣扎。这表明当前通用多模态能力掩盖了特定认知层面的更深层局限。此外，我们的研究突显了阿拉伯语与英语之间的显著性能差距，揭示了当前跨语言多模态推理的局限性。这些发现为开发更具认知对齐性和包容性的VLM奠定了基础。基准框架和数据集可访问：https://github.com/qcri/Almieyar-Oryx-BloomBench。

</details>


### [24] [Noise-Aware Visual Representation Learning for Medical Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05535)
*I Putu Adi Pratama,Bahadorreza Ofoghi,Atul Sajjanhar,Shang Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种噪声感知的医学视觉问答框架，通过在LLM前引入去噪自编码器来学习鲁棒的视觉表示，并使用LoRA进行参数高效微调，在SLAKE和PathVQA基准上提升了鲁棒性和性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有Med-VQA方法通常直接将现成的视觉编码器与大语言模型通过轻量映射网络连接，忽视了视觉表示中噪声和小幅无关变化的影响，导致模型对噪声敏感。

Method: 在视觉嵌入映射到LLM输入空间之前引入一个去噪自编码器，预训练使其从被破坏的输入中重建干净的视觉嵌入，从而学习鲁棒的视觉表示；随后通过MLP将鲁棒嵌入投影到语言模型嵌入空间形成视觉前缀token；采用LoRA进行参数高效微调。

Result: 在SLAKE和PathVQA基准上，所提方法对噪声输入嵌入具有更好的鲁棒性，同时在多项评价指标上保持了具有竞争力的干净性能。

Conclusion: 学习更鲁棒的视觉表示可以有效提升Med-VQA的性能和鲁棒性。

Original Abstract: Medical visual question answering (Med-VQA) has strong potential for clinical decision support by enabling AI models to interpret medical images and answer clinically relevant queries. Recent approaches typically connect off-the-shelf vision encoders with large language models (LLMs) through lightweight mapping networks to reduce computational cost. However, these methods often overlook the importance of handling noise and small irrelevant changes in visual representations. To address these challenges, we propose a noise-aware Med-VQA framework that incorporates a denoising autoencoder before visual embeddings are mapped into the input space of an LLM. The denoising autoencoder is pretrained to reconstruct clean visual embeddings from corrupted inputs, encouraging the model to learn robust visual representations that are less sensitive to noise. The resulting embeddings are then projected into the language model embedding space using a multi-layer perceptron (MLP), forming visual prefix tokens that provide image information to the LLM. To enable efficient adaptation without full retraining, we employ parameter-efficient fine-tuning using low-rank adaptation (LoRA). The proposed method is evaluated on the SLAKE and PathVQA benchmarks. Experimental results show improved robustness to noisy input embeddings while maintaining competitive clean performance across multiple evaluation criteria. These findings suggest that learning more robust visual representations can enhance Med-VQA performance and robustness.

中文摘要翻译: 医学视觉问答（Med-VQA）通过使AI模型能够解释医学图像并回答临床相关问题，在临床决策支持方面具有巨大潜力。近期的方法通常通过轻量映射网络将现成的视觉编码器与大语言模型（LLM）连接起来，以降低计算成本。然而，这些方法往往忽视了处理视觉表示中噪声和小幅无关变化的重要性。为应对这些挑战，我们提出了一种噪声感知的Med-VQA框架，该框架在视觉嵌入映射到LLM输入空间之前引入了一个去噪自编码器。该去噪自编码器经过预训练，能够从被破坏的输入中重建干净的视觉嵌入，从而鼓励模型学习对噪声不敏感的鲁棒视觉表示。随后，使用多层感知机（MLP）将得到的嵌入投影到语言模型嵌入空间，形成向LLM提供图像信息的视觉前缀token。为了实现无需完整重新训练的高效适配，我们采用低秩适配（LoRA）进行参数高效微调。所提出的方法在SLAKE和PathVQA基准上进行了评估。实验结果表明，该方法在保持多项评价指标上具有竞争力的干净性能的同时，对噪声输入嵌入表现出更好的鲁棒性。这些发现表明，学习更鲁棒的视觉表示可以提升Med-VQA的性能和鲁棒性。

</details>


### [25] [Dual Feature Decoupling for Fine-Grained OOD Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05536)
*Xiaokun Li,Yaping Huang,Qingji Guan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出双特征解耦网络（DFDNet），通过空间-频率解耦和重建引导解耦两个模块，解决细粒度图像分类中的分布外检测问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有OOD检测方法多假设大类间分布差异大，忽视了细粒度任务（如医学图像分类、车辆识别）中类间差异微小、背景干扰严重的问题，导致检测极具挑战性。

Method: 提出DFDNet，包含两个核心模块：(1) 空间-频率解耦模块，保留对分类有判别力的内容特征，抑制任务无关的风格信息；(2) 重建引导解耦模块，通过像素级对抗重建任务，去除低级非判别性信息，增强类别特定的高级语义表征。

Result: 在多个数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法取得了具有竞争力的性能提升。

Conclusion: 从特征解耦的角度有效解决了细粒度OOD检测难题，通过分离判别性内容特征与无关信息，提升了模型在细粒度场景下的分布外检测能力。

Original Abstract: Out-of-distribution detection (OOD) is an indispensable technique when applying machine learning models to real-world scenarios. Most existing OOD detection methods have been developed under the idealized assumption of large inter-class distributional differences, while largely overlooking fine-grained tasks characterized by subtle variations, such as medical image classification and vehicle recognition. The high visual similarity among fine-grained subcategories, together with the interference of background factors, makes OOD detection extremely challenging. To tackle this problem, we propose a novel Dual Feature Decoupling Network (DFDNet), which addresses fine-grained OOD detection from the perspective of feature disentanglement. The proposed DFDNet comprises two key components: a spatial-frequency decoupling module and a reconstruction-guided decoupling module. The spatial-frequency decoupling module is designed to preserve content features that are discriminative for classification while suppressing task-irrelevant style information. On the other hand, the reconstruction-guided decoupling module introduces a novel pixel-level adversarial reconstruction task to further remove low-level, non-discriminative information and enhance category-specific high-level semantic representations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves competitive performance improvements on multiple datasets.

中文摘要翻译: 分布外检测是将机器学习模型应用于现实场景时不可或缺的技术。现有的大多数OOD检测方法都是在假设大类间分布差异大的理想化条件下开发的，而很大程度上忽视了以细微变化为特征的细粒度任务，例如医学图像分类和车辆识别。细粒度子类别间的高度视觉相似性，加上背景因素的干扰，使得OOD检测极具挑战性。为了解决这一问题，我们提出了一种新颖的双特征解耦网络（DFDNet），从特征解耦的角度解决细粒度OOD检测问题。提出的DFDNet包含两个关键组件：空间-频率解耦模块和重建引导解耦模块。空间-频率解耦模块旨在保留对分类具有判别力的内容特征，同时抑制任务无关的风格信息。另一方面，重建引导解耦模块引入了一种新颖的像素级对抗重建任务，以进一步去除低级的、非判别性信息，并增强类别特定的高级语义表征。大量实验表明，我们的方法在多个数据集上取得了具有竞争力的性能提升。

</details>


### [26] [UltraVR: A Diagnostic Ultra-Resolution Image-VQA Benchmark for Evidence-Grounded Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05576)
*Gexin Huang,Yanting Yang,Myeongkyun Kang,Beidi Zhao,Jun Zhou,Chen Zhou,Gang Wang,Zu-hua Gao,Xiaoxiao Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 UltraVR，一个面向超分辨率图像的证据驱动视觉推理诊断基准，包含结构化思维链标注，揭示了当前 VLM 在超分辨率推理中主要失败于证据定位和局部感知环节。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有 VLM 评测多聚焦于最终答案准确率，缺乏对模型在超分辨率图像上是否真正获取并整合关键视觉证据的过程级诊断能力。

Method: 构建 UltraVR 基准，涵盖 CCTV 监控、遥感、全切片病理和工业异常检测四个高价值场景；每个实例除标准 QA 外，还包含结构化的真实思维链，附带分步问题、中间答案和推理标签（证据定位、局部感知、量化、证据整合、决策推理），支持过程级诊断。

Result: 当前前沿 VLM 在超分辨率推理上远不可靠；错误集中在证据定位和局部感知阶段，而当提供中间视觉事实时，下游推理通常能够恢复。

Conclusion: UltraVR 不仅可衡量 VLM 是否答对，还能诊断其超分辨率推理过程在哪个环节断裂，是一个有效的诊断性测试平台。

Original Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) excel on visual question answering and multimodal reasoning benchmarks. Yet their capability on ultra-resolution images - where critical evidence is tiny, subtle, spatially distant, or distributed - remains unclear. Existing evaluations largely report final-answer accuracy, offering limited insight into whether models acquire and integrate the necessary visual evidence. We introduce UltraVR, a diagnostic benchmark for evidence-grounded visual reasoning over ultra-resolution images. UltraVR spans four high-value scenarios: CCTV surveillance, remote sensing (RS), whole-slide image (WSI) pathology, and industrial anomaly detection (AD). These domains pose complementary challenges: fine-grained object grounding in crowded CCTV scenes, long-range spatial comparison in RS, multi-scale evidence navigation in WSI, and subtle irregularity detection in repetitive industrial layouts. Beyond standard QA triples, each instance includes a structured ground-truth chain of thought with step-level questions, intermediate answers, and reasoning labels. These labels decompose reasoning into evidence grounding, local perception, quantification, evidence integration, and decision inference, enabling process-level diagnosis over black-box scoring. Using UltraVR, we evaluate frontier VLMs and show that current models remain far from reliable on ultra-resolution reasoning. Importantly, the structured annotations allow us to localize failures across the visual-to-decision pipeline: errors concentrate in evidence grounding and local perception, while downstream inference often recovers when intermediate visual facts are supplied. These findings demonstrate UltraVR as a diagnostic testbed for measuring not only whether VLMs answer correctly, but where their ultra-resolution reasoning process breaks.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉-语言模型（VLM）在视觉问答和多模态推理基准测试中表现出色。然而，它们在超分辨率图像上的能力——即关键证据微小、细微、空间上遥远或分布广泛的情况——仍不明确。现有评估大多报告最终答案准确率，对于模型是否获取并整合了必要的视觉证据所提供的洞察有限。我们提出了 UltraVR，一个面向超分辨率图像上证据驱动视觉推理的诊断性基准。UltraVR 涵盖四个高价值场景：CCTV 监控、遥感（RS）、全切片图像（WSI）病理以及工业异常检测（AD）。这些领域带来了互补性挑战：拥挤的 CCTV 场景中的细粒度目标定位、遥感中的长距离空间比较、全切片图像中的多尺度证据导航，以及重复性工业布局中的细微不规则检测。除了标准的 QA 三元组，每个实例还包含一个结构化的真实思维链，带有分步问题、中间答案和推理标签。这些标签将推理分解为证据定位、局部感知、量化、证据整合和决策推理，从而实现对黑盒评分的过程级诊断。利用 UltraVR，我们评估了前沿 VLM，结果表明当前模型在超分辨率推理上远未达到可靠水平。重要的是，结构化标注使我们能够定位从视觉到决策管线的失败环节：错误集中在证据定位和局部感知，而当提供中间视觉事实时，下游推理通常能够恢复。这些发现表明 UltraVR 是一个诊断性测试平台，不仅可以衡量 VLM 是否回答正确，还能定位其超分辨率推理过程在何处断裂。

</details>


### [27] [BMCR: Adaptive Backbone Module Composition via Reinforcement Learning for Remote Sensing Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05586)
*Wenlin Liu,Xikun Hu,Ping Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BMCR框架，通过强化学习动态组合CNN和ViT骨干模块，实现遥感目标检测中自适应推理路径的选择，在三个基准数据集上超越现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的遥感目标检测器通常依赖单一固定骨干网络或手动设计的混合架构，无法根据输入图像的复杂度自适应地利用CNN的局部细节捕捉能力和ViT的全局上下文建模能力。为此，本文旨在实现跨CNN和ViT家族的模块化动态组合。

Method: 1) 构建可扩展模块工具箱，将CNN和ViT骨干分解为可复用功能模块，并封装结构、语义和计算元数据；2) 设计基于最优传输的轻量级过渡接口，对齐网格型CNN特征与序列型ViT表示；3) 将骨干组合建模为序列决策问题，策略网络根据中间多尺度观测逐步选择任务相关模块；4) 提出自适应模块协同优化策略，协调模块更新、路由探索和奖励分配。

Result: 在DOTA-v1.0、DOTA-v1.5和DIOR-R数据集上分别达到79.31%、73.41%和71.86% mAP，超越强静态和动态基线最多2.5个点，同时保持有竞争力的效率。

Conclusion: 通过强化学习动态组合CNN和ViT模块，BMCR实现了输入自适应的跨家族骨干网络构建，在遥感目标检测任务上取得了优越性能和良好效率，验证了动态模块化组合策略的有效性。

Original Abstract: In remote sensing object detection, Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) excel at capturing local details while Vision Transformers (ViTs) are better at global context modeling. However, existing detectors typically rely on a single fixed backbone or a manually designed hybrid architecture, and thus fail to adaptively exploit these complementary strengths across inputs of diverse complexity. To address this limitation, we propose Backbone Module Composition via Reinforcement Learning (BMCR). BMCR dynamically assembles input-adaptive inference paths from reusable modules decomposed from off-the-shelf CNN and ViT backbones. To enable such cross-family composition, we first construct an extensible module toolbox. Specifically, we decompose representative CNN and ViT backbones into reusable functional modules and encapsulate each module with explicit structural, semantic, and computational metadata for compatibility-aware assembly. To bridge the gap between grid-based CNN features and token-based ViT representations, we design a lightweight Optimal Transport (OT) based transition interface that ensures distribution-aware alignment while respecting spatial consistency. The backbone composition process is then formulated as a sequential decision problem, in which a policy network progressively selects task-relevant modules according to intermediate multi-scale observations. To stabilize the joint optimization of reusable modules and the routing policy, we further develop an Adaptive Module Cooperative Optimization (AMCO) strategy that coordinates module updating, routing exploration, and reward assignment during training. On DOTA-v1.0, DOTA-v1.5 and DIOR-R, BMCR achieves 79.31\%, 73.41\% and 71.86\% mAP, respectively, surpassing strong static and dynamic baselines by up to 2.5 points while maintaining competitive efficiency.

中文摘要翻译: 在遥感目标检测中，卷积神经网络擅长捕捉局部细节，而视觉Transformer更擅长建模全局上下文。然而，现有检测器通常依赖单一的固定骨干网络或人工设计的混合架构，因此无法根据输入的不同复杂度自适应地利用这些互补优势。为解决这一局限性，我们提出基于强化学习的骨干模块组合方法（BMCR）。BMCR从现成的CNN和ViT骨干网络中分解出可复用模块，并动态组装出适应输入的推理路径。为实现这种跨族组合，我们首先构建了一个可扩展的模块工具箱。具体而言，我们将代表性的CNN和ViT骨干网络分解为可复用的功能模块，并为每个模块封装了明确的结构、语义和计算元数据，以支持兼容性感知的组装。为弥合基于网格的CNN特征与基于序列的ViT表示之间的差距，我们设计了一种基于轻量级最优传输的过渡接口，在保持空间一致性的同时确保分布感知的对齐。随后，骨干组合过程被建模为一个序列决策问题，其中策略网络根据中间多尺度观测逐步选择任务相关模块。为稳定可复用模块和路由策略的联合优化，我们进一步提出了一种自适应模块协同优化策略，在训练过程中协调模块更新、路由探索和奖励分配。在DOTA-v1.0、DOTA-v1.5和DIOR-R数据集上，BMCR分别达到79.31%、73.41%和71.86%的mAP，超越强静态和动态基线最多2.5个点，同时保持了有竞争力的效率。

</details>


### [28] [HDST-GNN: Heterogeneous Dynamic Spatiotemporal Graph Neural Networks for Multi-Object Tracking in UAV Aerial Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05587)
*Phillip Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HDST-GNN，一种异构动态时空图神经网络，用于解决无人机航拍多目标跟踪中因高度变化、小目标密集和遮挡导致的身份切换问题，通过自适应高度图构建、异构节点表示和遮挡门控聚合，显著提升跟踪性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于图的跟踪器假设固定的空间上下文并统一对待所有对象，忽略了检测、活跃轨迹和丢失目标等异构生命周期状态，无法适应无人机航拍中序列间高度变化、目标小而密集以及频繁遮挡导致的身份切换问题。

Method: 1) 高度自适应边构建：通过平均目标面积估计相机高度代理，动态调整图连接半径；2) 异构节点表示：将检测（D型）、确认轨迹（T型）和丢失轨迹（L型）建模为不同节点类型，分别进行投影和类型化边关系处理；3) 遮挡门控时间聚合：根据节点的遮挡置信度对其注意力贡献进行门控，防止遮挡节点污染邻域嵌入。使用可微Sinkhorn头联合交叉熵和三重损失进行端到端训练。

Result: 在VisDrone2019-MOT上，使用oracle检测时MOTA达94.51%、IDF1达97.24%，比SORT提升+5.0 MOTA点，身份切换减少81%；使用真实YOLOv8n检测时，身份切换比SORT减少49%。消融实验验证了各组件的独立贡献。

Conclusion: HDST-GNN通过异构动态时空图神经网络有效建模目标的异构生命周期状态，自适应处理高度变化，利用遮挡门控抑制噪声传播，显著降低了无人机航拍多目标跟踪中的身份切换，在各项指标上大幅超越基线方法。

Original Abstract: Multi-object tracking (MOT) from UAV imagery presents unique challenges: altitude varies across sequences, objects are small and densely packed, and frequent occlusion causes identity switches. Existing graph-based trackers assume fixed spatial context and treat all objects uniformly, ignoring the heterogeneous lifecycle states of detections, active tracklets, and lost targets. We propose HDST-GNN, a Heterogeneous Dynamic Spatiotemporal Graph Neural Network with three novel contributions. First, Altitude-Adaptive Edge Construction estimates a camera-altitude proxy from mean object area and adjusts the graph connectivity radius accordingly. Second, Heterogeneous Node Representation models detections (Type-D), confirmed tracklets (Type-T), and lost tracklets (Type-L) as distinct node types with dedicated projections and typed edge relations. Third, Occlusion-Gated Temporal Aggregation gates each node's attention contribution by its occlusion confidence, preventing occluded nodes from corrupting neighbour embeddings. HDST-GNN is trained end-to-end with a differentiable Sinkhorn head using joint cross-entropy and triplet loss. On VisDrone2019-MOT with oracle detections, HDST-GNN achieves 94.51% MOTA and 97.24% IDF1, outperforming SORT by +5.0 MOTA points and reducing identity switches by 81%. With real YOLOv8n detections, HDST-GNN reduces identity switches by 49% vs. SORT. Ablation studies confirm the independent contribution of each component.

中文摘要翻译: 无人机航拍影像的多目标跟踪（MOT）面临独特挑战：不同序列间高度变化，目标小且密集，频繁遮挡导致身份切换。现有基于图的跟踪器假设固定的空间上下文并统一对待所有对象，忽略了检测、活跃轨迹和丢失目标等异构生命周期状态。我们提出HDST-GNN，一种异构动态时空图神经网络，包含三项创新贡献。首先，高度自适应边构建通过平均目标面积估计相机高度代理，并相应调整图连接半径。其次，异构节点表示将检测（D型）、确认轨迹（T型）和丢失轨迹（L型）建模为不同节点类型，配备专用投影和类型化边关系。第三，遮挡门控时间聚合根据节点的遮挡置信度门控其注意力贡献，防止遮挡节点污染邻域嵌入。HDST-GNN使用可微Sinkhorn头，结合交叉熵和三重损失进行端到端训练。在配备oracle检测的VisDrone2019-MOT上，HDST-GNN实现94.51%的MOTA和97.24%的IDF1，比SORT提升+5.0 MOTA点，身份切换减少81%。使用真实YOLOv8n检测时，HDST-GNN相比SORT减少49%的身份切换。消融研究证实了每个组件的独立贡献。

</details>


### [29] [What's Under the Skin? Estimating Swine Body Condition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05611)
*Mk Bashar,Kuljit Bhatti,Gary Rohrer,Madonna Benjamin,Tami Brown-Brandl,Daniel Morris*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PigFormer 是一个端到端两阶段系统，利用天花板安装的 RGB-D 相机获取的原始深度帧，预测母猪最后肋骨处的皮下背膘厚度、腰肌深度和总组织厚度，实现了 2.43 mm 背膘 MAE 和 3.87 mm 总体 MAE，优于强基线模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 母猪体况对泌乳性能和后裔存活影响重大，但现有体况测量方法（如视觉评分和卡尺）与底层组织成分相关性差，超声扫描虽能直接测量但操作费力且无法规模化。因此需要一种非接触、自动化、可扩展的体况监测方法。

Method: PigFormer 包含两个阶段：阶段1是几何前端，通过 SAM3-to-MaskDINO 分割蒸馏、地面去除和方向归一化将原始深度转换为标准化高度图；阶段2是切片注意力编码器，将每个高度图视为一系列横截面切片，沿整个背侧表面捕捉空间关系。

Result: 在两个设施的 319 头母猪/后备母猪数据集上，PigFormer 达到 2.43 mm 背膘 MAE 和 3.87 mm 总体 MAE，优于单阶段 ResNet-18 和 ViT-small 基线。

Conclusion: PigFormer 为实现商业养猪生产中连续、自动化、非接触的体况监测提供了一条实用路径。

Original Abstract: Sow body condition is an important indicator for growers as it has a large impact on lactation performance and piglet survival. However, body condition measures used during production, such as visual scoring and calipers, correlate poorly with underlying tissue composition. Ultrasound scans can provide direct measurements of subcutaneous backfat thickness and loin muscle depth, but their operation is labor intensive and not scalable for production. We present PigFormer, an end-to-end two-stage system that takes raw depth frames from a ceiling-mounted RGB-D camera and predicts subcutaneous backfat thickness, loin muscle depth, and total tissue thickness at the last rib. Stage 1 is a geometric front-end that converts raw depth into a standardized height map via SAM3-to-MaskDINO segmentation distillation, ground-plane removal, and orientation normalization.
