Stage 2 is a Slice Attention Encoder that treats each height map as a sequence of cross-sectional slices and captures spatial relationships along the full dorsal surface. On a multi-site dataset of 319 sow and gilt instances from two facilities, PigFormer achieves 2.43 mm backfat MAE and 3.87 mm overall MAE. It outperforms strong single-stage ResNet-18 and ViT-small baselines. PigFormer offers a practical path toward continuous, automated, non-contact body condition monitoring in commercial swine production. Code is available at https://github.com/iambashar/Pigformer.

中文摘要翻译: 母猪体况是养殖者关注的重要指标，因为它对泌乳性能和后裔存活有重大影响。然而，生产中使用的体况测量方法（如视觉评分和卡尺）与底层组织成分相关性较差。超声扫描能直接测量皮下背膘厚度和腰肌深度，但操作费力且无法规模化生产。本文提出 PigFormer，一个端到端两阶段系统，它从天花板安装的 RGB-D 相机获取原始深度帧，预测最后肋骨处的皮下背膘厚度、腰肌深度和总组织厚度。阶段1是几何前端，通过 SAM3-to-MaskDINO 分割蒸馏、地面去除和方向归一化将原始深度转换为标准化高度图。阶段2是切片注意力编码器，将每个高度图视为一系列横截面切片，沿整个背侧表面捕捉空间关系。在两个设施的 319 头母猪和后备母猪的多场地数据集上，PigFormer 实现了 2.43 mm 的背膘 MAE 和 3.87 mm 的总体 MAE。它优于强单阶段 ResNet-18 和 ViT-small 基线。PigFormer 为实现商业养猪生产中连续、自动化、非接触的体况监测提供了一条实用路径。代码可在 https://github.com/iambashar/Pigformer 获取。

</details>


### [30] [KV-Control: Parameter-Efficient K/V Injection for Trajectory-Controlled Text-to-Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05624)
*Tengjiao Sun,Pengcheng Fang,Xiaoyu Zhan,Yanwen Guo,Dongjie Fu,Xiaohao Cai,Hansung Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: KV-Control 是一种轻量级注意力侧控制接口，用于冻结的掩码文本到运动Transformer模型，通过将几何约束作为记忆注入自注意力层，实现精确的轨迹控制而不破坏预训练的文本条件运动先验。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有文本到3D人体运动模型能从文本提示生成合理运动，但实际动画和具身智能工作流需要额外约束（如路径、末端效应器目标、多关节轨迹），而现有解决方案要么复制大量生成器以恢复逐层控制访问，要么将大量计算成本转移到测试时优化，缺乏一种精确且轻量的控制接口。

Method: 提出KV-Control，一种冻结的掩码文本到运动变压器的紧凑注意力侧控制接口。核心是：1) PartVQ：学习解剖学对齐的部分码本；2) T-Concat：将每个帧-部分token暴露为注意力可寻址位点；3) KV-Control：在每一自注意力层注入控制条件的键/值记忆，同时保留预训练的查询流、文本交叉注意力、FFN和所有骨干网络权重。

Result: 在继承的细化协议下，KV-Control跟踪根节点和多关节约束的精度达到亚厘米级，同时保持文本条件运动的质量。该适配器仅在共享轨迹编码器之上添加可训练注入参数。

Conclusion: KV-Control将轨迹条件化重新定义为轻量级记忆检索，为文本到运动生成提供了一个小巧、精确且透明的控制接口。

Original Abstract: Text-conditioned 3D human motion models now synthesize plausible motions from prompts, but practical animation and embodied-agent workflows rarely stop at text: a character may need to follow a sketched root path, hit an end-effector target, or satisfy a multi-joint trajectory while still preserving the gait, style, and intent described by language. This exposes a control trade-off. A trajectory controller should be precise without overwriting the pretrained text-conditioned motion prior, yet existing solutions either duplicate large portions of the generator to regain per-layer control access or move much of the cost to test-time optimization. We introduce KV-Control, a compact attention-side control interface for frozen masked text-to-motion transformers. The key idea is to make geometric constraints available as memory inside self-attention rather than injecting them through a global pose token or enforcing them only at the output side. To support this interface, we co-design a part-tokenized motion substrate and controller: \textbf{PartVQ} learns anatomy-aligned part codebooks, T-Concat exposes each frame--part token as an attention-addressable site, and KV-Control injects control-conditioned key/value memories at every self-attention layer while preserving the pretrained query stream, text cross-attention, FFN, and all backbone weights. The resulting adapter adds only trainable injection parameters atop a shared trajectory encoder, yet tracks root and multi-joint constraints with sub-centimeter accuracy under the inherited refinement protocol while retaining text-conditioned motion quality. KV-Control reframes trajectory conditioning as lightweight memory retrieval, providing a small, precise, and transparent control interface for text-to-motion generation.

中文摘要翻译: 文本条件驱动的3D人体运动模型现在能够根据提示合成合理的运动，但实际动画和具身智能体工作流很少仅止于文本：一个角色可能需要遵循一条手绘的根轨迹、击中一个末端效应器目标、或满足一个多关节轨迹，同时仍保留语言所描述的步态、风格和意图。这揭示了一个控制权衡问题。一个轨迹控制器应当精确，同时不覆盖预训练的文本条件运动先验，然而现有的解决方案要么复制生成器的大部分以重新获得逐层控制访问，要么将大量计算成本转移到测试时优化。我们引入了KV-Control，一种针对冻结的掩码文本到运动转换器的紧凑型注意力侧控制接口。其核心思想是将几何约束作为记忆放置在自注意力内部，而不是通过全局姿态标记注入或仅在输出侧强制执行。为了支持这一接口，我们共同设计了部分标记化的运动基元和控制器：PartVQ学习了解剖对齐的部分码本，T-Concat将每个帧-部分标记暴露为注意力可寻址位点，KV-Control在每个自注意力层注入控制条件的键/值记忆，同时保留预训练的查询流、文本交叉注意力、前馈网络以及所有骨干网络权重。最终得到的适配器仅在共享轨迹编码器之上添加了可训练的注入参数，但在继承的细化协议下，能够以亚厘米级的精度跟踪根节点和多关节约束，同时保持文本条件的运动质量。KV-Control将轨迹条件化重新定义为轻量级记忆检索，为文本到运动生成提供了一个小巧、精确且透明的控制接口。

</details>


### [31] [ShotCrop$^3$: Cropping Human-Centric Images into Cinematic Triple-Shot Compositions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05635)
*Dehong Kong,Lina Lei,Lingtao Zheng,Chenyang Wu,Ailing Zhang,Xinran Qin,Teng Ma,Jiaqi Xu,Zhixin Wang,Zhikai Chen,Xuecheng Qi,Renjing Pei,Fan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出三镜头构图任务（TSC），从单张人物图像生成远景、中景、特写三个镜头并配文字描述。提出ShotCrop方法，通过三阶段训练（CoT SFT、半监督微调、GRPO-S优化）实现该任务，在TSC-Bench基准上镜头定位精度平均提升2.82倍（vs GPT-5）。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有美学构图方法仅生成单一裁剪结果，忽视了实际创意工作流（如商业海报）中需要多镜头组合来呈现不同叙事重点（上下文、主体、情感/产品细节）的需求。

Method: 提出Triple-Shot Compositions（TSC）任务，以及ShotCrop方法。三步训练：(1) Chain-of-Thought监督微调建立基础推理和裁剪能力；(2) 半监督微调，用MLLM评分、美学评估和CLIP相似度联合筛选高置信度伪标签；(3) GRPO-S优化，使用针对ShotCrop设计的组合奖励函数。

Result: 在TSC-Bench（1.2k专家标注测试集）上，ShotCrop在镜头定位精度上平均提升2.82倍（对比GPT-5）。

Conclusion: 多镜头构图在视觉叙事中具有重要价值，所提三阶段训练策略结合伪标签和强化学习能有效实现该任务，大幅超越现有模型。

Original Abstract: Prior work on aesthetic composition typically produces a single aesthetically pleasing crop, overlooking the narrative value of composing multiple shots from one scene. In practice, multi-shot composition is critical for downstream creative workflows: commercial posters often require multiple crops with different emphases (e.g., context, subject, and emotion/product details) to present key story beats. Therefore, we propose \textbf{Triple-Shot Compositions (TSC)}, a composition task that generates a three-shot set -- establishing, medium, and close-up -- from a single human-centric image, each paired with a brief shot description to support visual narration. To learn TSC with limited expert annotations, we introduce \textbf{ShotCrop} which undergoes a three-stage training process: it first applies Chain-of-Thought supervised fine-tuning to establish basic reasoning and aesthetic shot-cropping skills, then performs semi-supervised fine-tuning with high-confidence pseudo labels to further enhance aesthetic capability, and is finally optimized with Group Relative Policy Optimization for \textbf{ShotCrop} (GRPO-S) using a composite reward tailored for it. Specifically, our pseudo-labeling strategy combines MLLM-based scoring, aesthetic assessment, and CLIP similarity to retain high-confidence training signals. In addition, we present TSC-Bench, a benchmark of 1.2k expert-annotated test cases. Notably, ShotCrop achieves an average improvement of \textbf{2.82} times over GPT-5 in shot localization accuracy.

中文摘要翻译: 先前关于美学构图的研究通常只生成单个美观的裁剪结果，忽视了从同一场景组合多个镜头的叙事价值。在实践中，多镜头构图对于下游创意工作流至关重要：商业海报通常需要多个具有不同侧重点（如上下文、主体、情感/产品细节）的裁剪结果来呈现关键故事节拍。因此，我们提出\textbf{三镜头构图（Triple-Shot Compositions, TSC）}，这是一个从单张人物图像生成三镜头集——远景、中景和特写——的构图任务，每个镜头配有简短的镜头描述以支持视觉叙事。为在有限专家标注下学习TSC，我们引入\textbf{ShotCrop}，它经历三阶段训练过程：首先应用思维链监督微调建立基本推理和美学裁剪技能，然后使用高置信度伪标签进行半监督微调以进一步增强美学能力，最后使用针对\textbf{ShotCrop}设计的组合奖励通过组相对策略优化（GRPO-S）进行优化。具体地，我们的伪标签策略结合了基于多模态大模型的评分、美学评估和CLIP相似度，以保留高置信度训练信号。此外，我们提出TSC-Bench，一个包含1.2k专家标注测试用例的基准。值得注意的是，ShotCrop在镜头定位精度上相比GPT-5实现了平均\textbf{2.82}倍的提升。

</details>


### [32] [Multi-Task Crack Foundation Model for Engineering-Reliable Crack Representation and Topology Preservation in Civil Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05641)
*Blessing Agyei Kyem,Joshua Kofi Asamoah,Eugene Denteh,Armstrong Aboah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CrackGeoFM多任务框架，结合视觉基础模型与裂缝专用适配模块，实现像素级分割、骨架重建和不确定性估计，在20个裂缝数据集上达到SOTA性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有分割模型在裂缝评估中存在碎片化分割、遗漏细枝裂缝、缺乏校准的不确定性估计等问题，无法满足域迁移下稳定可靠的裂缝几何与置信度评估需求。

Method: 提出CrackGeoFM多任务框架，包含三个核心模块：频率引导的裂缝增强模块（FCEM）增强高频裂缝线索、裂缝域特征适配模块（CFAM）适配冻结骨干特征、结构感知多任务解码器（SMTD）联合解码掩码、骨架和不确定性。

Result: 在20个裂缝数据集上，CrackGeoFM在分割精度、拓扑结构保持、不确定性校准方面达到SOTA，且仅需5张标注图像即可实现有效的少样本适配。

Conclusion: 该框架支持可靠、可泛化、面向工程的裂缝分析，适用于基础设施评估。

Original Abstract: Reliable crack assessment requires not only accurate pixel-level masks but also connected crack geometry and confidence estimates that remain stable under domain shift. However, existing segmentation models can achieve high overlap scores while fragmenting cracks, missing fine branches, and providing no calibrated uncertainty. To address this gap, this paper proposes CrackGeoFM, a multi-task framework that combines a frozen visual foundation backbone with crack-specific adaptation for mask prediction, skeleton reconstruction, and uncertainty estimation. The framework integrates a Frequency-Guided Crack Enhancement Module (FCEM) to enhance high-frequency crack cues, a Crack-Domain Feature Adaptation Module (CFAM) to adapt frozen backbone features to crack-domain patterns, and a Structure-Aware Multi-Task Decoder (SMTD) to jointly decode masks, skeletons, and uncertainty. Across 20 crack datasets, CrackGeoFM achieves state-of-the-art segmentation, improved topology preservation, calibrated uncertainty, and effective few-shot adaptation with only five labeled images. These results support reliable, generalizable, and engineering-oriented crack analysis for infrastructure assessment.

中文摘要翻译: 可靠的裂缝评估不仅需要精确的像素级掩码，还需要在域迁移下保持稳定的连通裂缝几何结构和置信度估计。然而，现有的分割模型在达到高重叠分数的同时，往往导致裂缝碎片化、遗漏细枝裂缝，且无法提供校准的不确定性。为解决这一问题，本文提出CrackGeoFM，一个多任务框架，将冻结的视觉基础骨干网络与裂缝专用适配相结合，用于掩码预测、骨架重建和不确定性估计。该框架集成了频率引导的裂缝增强模块（FCEM）以增强高频裂缝线索、裂缝域特征适配模块（CFAM）以将冻结的骨干特征适配到裂缝域模式，以及结构感知多任务解码器（SMTD）以联合解码掩码、骨架和不确定性。在20个裂缝数据集上，CrackGeoFM实现了最先进的分割性能、更好的拓扑结构保持、校准的不确定性估计，以及仅用五张标注图像即可进行的有效少样本适配。这些结果支持面向基础设施评估的可靠、可泛化且工程导向的裂缝分析。

</details>


### [33] [CoFi-UCGen: Coarse-to-Fine Unsupervised Conditional Generation without Label Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05652)
*Shengxi Li,Zhaokun Hu,Ce Zheng,Mai Xu,Jingyuan Xia,Si Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种粗到细的无监督条件图像生成框架（CoFi-UCGen），通过对抗语义互惠学习理论和比特码机制，在没有标签的情况下同时实现粗粒度和细粒度的条件生成，在图像质量、语义一致性和控制精度上优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 无监督条件图像生成（UCGen）旨在无需人工标注标签即可控制生成过程，但由于跨粒度的非结构化语义表示，该任务仍然极具挑战性。现有方法难以同时实现粗粒度和细粒度的条件控制。

Method: 1) 提出对抗语义互惠学习理论（Adversarial Semantic Reciprocal Learning），保证图像空间与潜空间之间的语义一致性和完整性；2) 基于该一致性，提出比特码（bit-codes）学习结构化的粗粒度潜空间，在保留独立噪声采样的同时证明比特码蕴含不同的全局语义；3) 在比特码基础上建立细粒度语义基，并在扩散模型中引入层次化调制机制（Hierarchical Modulation），通过从粗条件到细属性的逐层注入实现渐进式控制。

Result: 在无任何标签先验或预训练特征提取器的条件下，CoFi-UCGen 在图像质量、语义一致性和控制精度上始终优于现有 UCGen 方法，验证了显式粗到细语义分解在无监督条件生成任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作是首个成功实现无需任何标签即可进行粗粒度和细粒度条件生成的无监督框架，表明显式的粗到细语义分解是解决无监督条件图像生成挑战的有效途径。

Original Abstract: Unsupervised conditional image generation (UCGen) aims to control generation without relying on manually annotated labels, yet remains challenging due to unstructured semantic representations across granularities. To address this, we propose a novel coarse-to-fine UCGen framework (CoFi-UCGen) that explicitly disentangles global semantics from fine-grained variations, which to the best of our knowledge, sets out the first successful attempt for both coarse- and fine-grained conditional generation without any labels. More specifically, we first propose the adversarial semantic reciprocal learning theory to ensure the semantic consistency and completeness between images and latent spaces. Based on the consistency, we propose the bit-codes to learn a structured coarse-grained latent space, and further prove distinct global semantics inherent from our bit-codes while preserving independent noise sampling for generation. Building upon these bit-codes, we establish a fine-grained semantic basis and introduce a hierarchical modulation mechanism in diffusion models, by enabling layer-wise injection from coarse conditions to progressively control fine-grained attributes during generation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that without any label priors or pre-trained feature extractors, our CoFi-UCGen consistently outperforms existing UCGen methods in terms of image quality, semantic consistency, and control accuracy, verifying the effectiveness of explicit coarse-to-fine semantic decomposition for the challenging UCGen task.

中文摘要翻译: 无监督条件图像生成（UCGen）旨在无需依赖人工标注标签即可控制生成过程，但由于跨粒度的非结构化语义表示，该任务仍然具有挑战性。为解决这一问题，我们提出了一种新颖的从粗到细的UCGen框架（CoFi-UCGen），该框架显式地将全局语义与细粒度变化分离开来，据我们所知，这是首次成功实现无需任何标签即可同时进行粗粒度和细粒度条件生成的尝试。具体而言，我们首先提出对抗语义互惠学习理论，以确保图像空间与潜空间之间的语义一致性和完整性。基于该一致性，我们提出比特码（bit-codes）来学习结构化的粗粒度潜空间，并进一步证明比特码蕴含不同的全局语义，同时保留独立的噪声采样用于生成。在这些比特码的基础上，我们构建了细粒度语义基，并在扩散模型中引入层次化调制机制，通过从粗条件到细属性的逐层注入，在生成过程中渐进式地控制细粒度属性。大量实验表明，在没有任何标签先验或预训练特征提取器的情况下，我们的CoFi-UCGen在图像质量、语义一致性和控制精度方面始终优于现有的UCGen方法，验证了显式的粗到细语义分解对于具有挑战性的UCGen任务的有效性。

</details>


### [34] [V2V-Bench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Video-to-Video Generation Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05665)
*Tao Liu,Leela Krishna,Gouti Pavan Kumar,Sreeja K,Vishav Garg*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对视频到视频（V2V）生成任务缺乏专门评估基准的问题，提出了一个包含11个维度、5个类别的V2V-Bench基准，并对三个代表性模型进行了评测，发现不同模型具有互补优势，该基准在V2V特定维度上与人类判断的Spearman相关系数达0.905。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的文本到视频（T2V）和图像到视频（I2V）指标无法捕捉V2V生成任务中既要遵循编辑指令又要保持与源视频帧级对应关系的双重需求，因此亟需一个专门针对V2V任务的评估基准。

Method: 提出了V2V-Bench基准，包含11个评估维度，分为时间对齐、结构保真度、变换质量、视频质量和语义对齐五大类别；选取多样化的源视频和具有挑战性的编辑任务，对Grok Imagine、Gemini Veo3和Open Sora 2三个模型进行评测。

Result: Grok在编辑保真度上表现更好，Veo3在视觉质量上更强；在6个V2V特定维度上，V2V-Bench与人类判断的Spearman相关系数达到0.905。

Conclusion: V2V-Bench能够有效评估V2V生成任务，揭示了不同模型之间的互补优势，为V2V评估提供了可靠的自动化基准。

Original Abstract: Video-to-video (V2V) generation is difficult to evaluate because outputs must both follow editing instructions and preserve frame-level correspondence with the source video, which existing T2V and I2V metrics do not capture. We introduce V2V-Bench, a 11-dimension benchmark organized into five categories: temporal alignment, structural fidelity, transformation quality, video quality, and semantic alignment. V2V-Bench pairs diverse source videos with challenging editing tasks and evaluates two commercial models, Grok Imagine and Gemini Veo3, and one open-source model, Open Sora 2. Results show complementary model strengths: Grok performs better on editing fidelity, while Veo3 achieves stronger visual quality. On six V2V-specific dimensions, V2V-Bench reaches a Spearman correlation of 0.905 with human judgments.

中文摘要翻译: 视频到视频（V2V）生成任务难以评估，因为输出必须同时遵循编辑指令并保持与源视频的帧级对应关系，而现有的T2V和I2V指标无法捕捉这些要求。本文提出了V2V-Bench，一个包含11个维度的基准测试，分为五大类别：时间对齐、结构保真度、变换质量、视频质量和语义对齐。V2V-Bench将多样化的源视频与具有挑战性的编辑任务配对，并评估了两个商业模型（Grok Imagine和Gemini Veo3）以及一个开源模型（Open Sora 2）。结果表明不同模型具有互补优势：Grok在编辑保真度上表现更优，而Veo3在视觉质量上更胜一筹。在六个V2V特定维度上，V2V-Bench与人类判断的Spearman相关系数达到0.905。

</details>


### [35] [LongSpace: Exploring Long-Horizon Spatial Memory from Perception to Recall in Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05677)
*Shiqiang Lang,Jing Liu,Haoyang He,Peiwen Sun,Yuanteng Chen,Tao Liu,Lan Yang,Longteng Guo,Honggang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LongSpace-Bench基准和LongSpace记忆框架，用于评估和增强多模态大模型的长视频空间记忆与推理能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有MLLMs在处理长视频时难以记住和检索之前观察过的空间布局、路线、视角变化和物体状态，而长时程任务（如自动驾驶、机器人导航）恰恰需要这种空间记忆能力，因此需要相应的基准和方法来评估和提升这一能力。

Method: LongSpace将长视频建模为连续块，将3D结构线索注入早期解码器层，并构建分层感知的记忆用于问题引导检索。

Result: 在多个空间推理基准上，LongSpace提升了长视频空间理解能力，表明显式空间记忆是长视频多模态大模型的关键能力。

Conclusion: LongSpace通过显式的空间记忆框架有效提升了MLLMs的长视频空间推理性能，证明了空间记忆在长时程视频理解中的重要性。

Original Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have advanced image and video understanding and can increasingly handle longer visual inputs. Long-horizon tasks such as autonomous driving and robotic navigation require more than recognizing the current view, as models must remember and retrieve previously observed spatial layouts, routes, viewpoint changes, and object states. To evaluate this capability, we introduce LongSpace-Bench, a room-tour video benchmark for long-horizon spatial memory, covering scene perception, spatial relations, and spatial memory. In this work, we further propose LongSpace, a memory framework for long-video spatial reasoning. LongSpace models long videos as sequential chunks, incorporates 3D structural cues into early decoder layers, and constructs layer-aware memory for question-guided retrieval. Experiments on multiple spatial reasoning benchmarks show that LongSpace improves long-video spatial understanding, further demonstrating explicit spatial memory as a key capability for long-horizon video MLLMs.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态大语言模型在图像和视频理解方面取得了进展，并且能够处理越来越长的视觉输入。诸如自动驾驶和机器人导航等长时程任务不仅需要识别当前视图，模型还必须记住并检索之前观察到的空间布局、路线、视角变化和对象状态。为评估这一能力，我们引入了LongSpace-Bench，这是一个用于长时程空间记忆的室内漫游视频基准，涵盖了场景感知、空间关系和空间记忆。在此基础上，我们进一步提出了LongSpace，一个用于长视频空间推理的记忆框架。LongSpace将长视频建模为连续块，将3D结构线索注入早期解码器层，并构建分层感知的记忆用于问题引导检索。在多个空间推理基准上的实验表明，LongSpace提升了长视频空间理解能力，进一步证明了显式空间记忆是长时程视频多模态大模型的一项关键能力。

</details>


### [36] [T-SAR-JEPA: Self-Supervised Temporal Anomaly Detection in SAR Amplitude Stacks via Latent Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05700)
*Kerod Woldesenbet,Abem Woldesenbet*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: T-SAR-JEPA是一种基于潜在预测的自监督SAR时序异常检测框架。通过域自适应编码器和时序Transformer，在DFC 2026数据集上显著优于基线方法（ROC-AUC 77.0% vs ~50%），检测具有高度空间一致性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有SAR时序异常检测方法（如RX、PaDiM等）在复杂地表变化场景下表现不足，且依赖InSAR相干性等辅助数据。作者希望构建一种仅利用幅度信息、通过自监督学习实现鲁棒时序异常检测的方法。

Method: 首先将SAR-JEPA的ViT-Base/16编码器在39,300个Capella patches上进行域自适应，采用局部掩码重建与梯度特征预测。然后使用时序Transformer（含正弦时间编码）从K=7次采集中预测未来潜在状态，并采用渐进解冻策略降低验证损失。模型仅基于幅度信息，InSAR相干性仅作为独立伪标签进行评估。

Result: 在DFC 2026数据集（300个时间序列，三个感兴趣区域）上：针对夏威夷喷发窗口，ROC-AUC达77.0%，显著优于RX、PaDiM、线性AR和LSTM基线（约50%）；空间一致性达99.9%（p<0.001，排列检验），证实检测结果具有结构化模式而非随机噪声。

Conclusion: T-SAR-JEPA通过自监督潜在预测有效实现了SAR幅度时序异常检测，无需依赖InSAR相干性等辅助数据，在性能和空间一致性上大幅超越传统方法，为SAR时序分析提供了新范式。

Original Abstract: We present T-SAR-JEPA, a self-supervised framework for temporal anomaly detection in SAR amplitude stacks via latent prediction. A ViT-Base/16 encoder from SAR-JEPA is domain-adapted on 39,300 Capella patches using local masked reconstruction with gradient feature prediction. A temporal transformer with sinusoidal time encoding forecasts future latent states from K=7 acquisitions, with progressive unfreezing substantially reducing validation loss. The model operates on amplitude alone; InSAR coherence serves exclusively as independent pseudo-ground-truth. On the DFC 2026 dataset (300 time-series, three AOIs), T-SAR-JEPA achieves ROC-AUC of 77.0% on the Hawaii eruption window, outperforming RX, PaDiM, Linear AR, and LSTM baselines (~50%). Spatial coherence of 99.9% (p < 0.001, permutation test) confirms structured detections. Code: https://github.com/TerraLatent/t-sar-jepa

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了T-SAR-JEPA，一种通过潜在预测进行SAR幅度时序异常检测的自监督框架。来自SAR-JEPA的ViT-Base/16编码器在39,300个Capella patches上通过局部掩码重建与梯度特征预测进行域自适应。一个配有正弦时间编码的时序Transformer从K=7次采集中预测未来潜在状态，渐进解冻策略显著降低了验证损失。该模型仅基于幅度信息运行；InSAR相干性仅作为独立的伪真值进行评估。在DFC 2026数据集（300个时间序列，三个感兴趣区域）上，T-SAR-JEPA在夏威夷喷发窗口上达到77.0%的ROC-AUC，优于RX、PaDiM、线性AR和LSTM基线（约50%）。99.9%的空间一致性（p<0.001，排列检验）证实了结构化的检测结果。代码地址：https://github.com/TerraLatent/t-sar-jepa

</details>


### [37] [Parallel Jacobi Decoding for Fast Autoregressive Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05703)
*Boya Liao,Ying Li,Siyong Jian,Huan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需训练的并行雅可比解码方法（PJD），通过在二维空间域扩展草稿令牌并调整注意力掩码，有效加速自回归图像生成，实现4.8倍-6.4倍加速同时保持生成质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 自回归图像生成模型虽能生成高保真图像，但其顺序逐令牌预测导致推理速度极慢；现有雅可比式解码在一维序列中因误差传播导致加速很快饱和，利用率受限。

Method: 提出并行雅可比解码（PJD），在二维空间域扩展草稿令牌，利用图像的强局部空间相关性实现高效的空间并行精炼；同时调整注意力掩码以缓解误差累积并提高收敛稳定性。

Result: 在多个数据集上，PJD在多种自回归图像生成模型中实现了4.8倍-6.4倍的加速，同时保持了有竞争力的生成质量。

Conclusion: 通过二维空间并行策略和注意力掩码调整，PJD能够有效克服一维雅可比解码的误差传播瓶颈，实现自回归图像生成的无训练加速。

Original Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) models have demonstrated remarkable performance in generating high-fidelity images. However, their inherently sequential next-token prediction leads to significantly slower inference. Recent studies have introduced Jacobi-style decoding to accelerate autoregressive image generation. Extending the draft sequence initially improves efficiency, yet the acceleration quickly saturates as error propagation in the one-dimensional sequence hinders convergence. Observing that images exhibit strong local spatial correlations, we propose Parallel Jacobi Decoding (PJD), a training-free decoding approach that expands draft tokens in the two-dimensional spatial domain to enable efficient spatially parallel refinement. PJD adjusts the attention mask to mitigate error accumulation and improve convergence stability. Extensive experiments on diverse datasets show that PJD achieves 4.8x-6.4x acceleration across multiple autoregressive image generation models while maintaining competitive generation quality.

中文摘要翻译: 自回归（AR）模型在生成高保真图像方面展现了卓越的性能。然而，其固有序贯逐令牌预测的特性导致推理速度显著变慢。近期研究引入了雅可比式解码来加速自回归图像生成。扩展草稿序列初步提升了效率，但随着一维序列中的误差传播阻碍收敛，加速效果迅速饱和。观察到图像具有强局部空间相关性，我们提出并行雅可比解码（PJD），这是一种无需训练的解码方法，通过在二维空间域扩展草稿令牌，实现高效的空间并行精炼。PJD调整注意力掩码以缓解误差累积并提高收敛稳定性。在多个数据集上的大量实验表明，PJD在多种自回归图像生成模型上实现了4.8倍至6.4倍的加速，同时保持了有竞争力的生成质量。

</details>


### [38] [Real-Time Threat Detection from Surveillance Cameras using Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05708)
*Gajendra Mandal,J. P. Patra,Priyansh Mahant*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于YOLOv8的实时目标检测监控框架，用于检测枪支、刀具及印度监控场景中的钝器（铁棒、木棍、塑料棒），使用7,959张图像的数据集训练模型，在准确性和效率之间取得了良好平衡，适合部署于校园、公共空间和交通区域等真实监控环境。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统监控系统依赖人工监测，存在效率低下、易疲劳、响应延迟和观察错误等问题，难以保障密集城市环境中的公共安全。因此，需要部署智能化的自动视频监控系统来克服这些局限。

Method: 使用自建数据集（336张钝器图像，包含铁棒、木棍、塑料棒，由手机拍摄）与公开数据集（7,623张枪支和刀具图像）合并，形成共7,959张图像的三类数据集（枪、刀、钝器）。基于该数据集训练YOLOv8目标检测模型，实现实时检测。

Result: 实验结果表明，增加训练时长能显著提升钝器类别的召回率和平均精度，且未出现过拟合现象。整体上，该框架在准确性与效率之间取得了有效平衡。

Conclusion: 该框架适合部署于校园、公共空间和交通区域等真实监控环境，能够有效提升公共安全保障能力。

Original Abstract: Ensuring public safety in densely populated urban environments remains a critical challenge, necessitating the deployment of intelligent and automated video surveillance systems. Traditional surveillance approaches rely heavily on manual monitoring, which is inefficient and susceptible to human fatigue, delayed response, and observational errors. To overcome these limitations, this work presents a real-time object detection-based surveillance framework. The proposed system focuses on detecting guns, knives, and region-specific blunt objects commonly involved in violent activities in Indian surveillance scenarios. A key contribution of this work is the use of a custom-created dataset collected using a mobile camera, consisting of 336 labeled images of blunt objects such as iron rods, wooden sticks, and plastic rods. This dataset is combined with a publicly available dataset of 7,623 images of guns and knives, forming a consolidated dataset of 7,959 images across three classes: gun, knife, and blunt object. The combined dataset is used to train a YOLOv8-based object detection model for real-time performance. Experimental evaluation shows that increasing the training duration significantly improves recall and average precision for the blunt object class without signs of overfitting. Overall, the proposed framework achieves an effective balance between accuracy and efficiency, making it suitable for deployment in real-world surveillance environments such as campuses, public spaces, and transportation areas.

中文摘要翻译: 在人口密集的城市环境中确保公共安全仍然是一项严峻挑战，需要部署智能化和自动化的视频监控系统。传统的监控方法严重依赖人工监测，这种方式效率低下，且容易受到人员疲劳、响应延迟和观察错误的影响。为了克服这些限制，本文提出了一种基于实时目标检测的监控框架。该系统专注于检测枪支、刀具以及印度监控场景中常出现在暴力活动中的区域性钝器。本文的一个关键贡献是使用手机摄像头采集并创建了自定义数据集，包含336张标注的钝器图像，如铁棒、木棍和塑料棒。该数据集与一个包含7,623张枪支和刀具图像的公开数据集合并，形成了包含7,959张图像、涵盖枪、刀和钝器三个类别的合并数据集。利用该合并数据集训练基于YOLOv8的目标检测模型以实现实时性能。实验评估表明，增加训练时长能够显著提升钝器类别的召回率和平均精度，且未出现过拟合现象。总体而言，所提出的框架在准确性和效率之间取得了有效平衡，使其适合部署于校园、公共空间和交通区域等真实监控环境中。

</details>


### [39] [ViCuR: Visual Cues as Recoverable Privilege for Multimodal On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05718)
*Kanghui Tian,Siyuan Liu,Ziang Yan,Sheng Xia,Shuai Dong,Yi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ViCuR提出了一种基于视觉线索的特权教师蒸馏框架，通过在输入中引入查询相关视觉证据作为特权信号，解决了多模态推理中答案侧特权导致的训练-测试不匹配问题，在七个基准测试上显著优于基于答案的在线自蒸馏和强教师在线蒸馏方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有在线策略蒸馏（OPD）在多模态推理中使用特权教师时，依赖参考答案或推理过程等答案侧特权信号，这导致训练-测试不匹配：教师的监督依赖于学生无法获取的线索，鼓励捷径模仿而非基于视觉的合理推理。

Method: ViCuR框架用视觉线索（输入中与查询相关的证据）替代答案侧特权，并引入轻量级线索恢复模块，在预填充阶段通过专属sink-token交叉注意力将任务相关的视觉证据聚合并内部表示，不改变推理接口，也不需要额外的线索生成损失。

Result: 在七个基准测试上，Qwen3-VL-2B和8B学生模型上，ViCuR相比基于答案的在线自蒸馏平均提升+1.19和+1.24；相比强教师OPD基线平均提升+0.64和+1.08，并在8B规模上取得一致的域外泛化收益。

Conclusion: 在多模态在线策略蒸馏中，教师特权信号的设计与教师模型本身的强度同等重要，基于视觉线索的特权设计能够有效缓解训练-测试不匹配问题，促进更合理的视觉推理。

Original Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) improves reasoning by training a student on trajectories sampled from its own policy under supervision from a teacher. In multimodal reasoning, a common extension is to use a privileged teacher that observes training-time-only signals such as reference answers or rationales. However, such answer-side privilege creates a train-test mismatch: the teacher's supervision may depend on signals unavailable to the student, encouraging shortcut imitation rather than visually grounded reasoning. We propose ViCuR, a visually grounded privileged-teacher distillation framework that replaces answer-side privilege with visual cues (query-related evidence in the input). Because these cues are derived from the same visual input available at inference, their evidence is recoverable by the student. To support this, ViCuR introduces a lightweight cue recovery module that uses dedicated sink-token cross-attention during prefill to aggregate task-relevant visual evidence into an internal representation, without changing the inference interface or requiring auxiliary cue-generation losses. Across seven benchmarks with Qwen3-VL-2B and 8B students, ViCuR consistently improves over answer-based on-policy self-distillation by +1.19 and +1.24 on overall average performance. It also extends naturally to stronger-teacher OPD, surpassing OPD baselines by +0.64 and +1.08, with consistent out-of-domain gains at the 8B scale. These results show that, in multimodal on-policy distillation, the design of teacher privilege is as important as teacher strength.

中文摘要翻译: 在线策略蒸馏（OPD）通过训练学生模型在其自身策略采样的轨迹上接受教师监督，从而提升推理能力。在多模态推理中，一种常见的扩展是使用特权教师，该教师可以观察到仅在训练时可用的信号，例如参考答案或推理过程。然而，这种答案侧特权造成了训练-测试不匹配：教师的监督可能依赖于学生无法获取的信号，从而鼓励捷径模仿而非基于视觉的合理推理。我们提出ViCuR，一个基于视觉引导的特权教师蒸馏框架，用视觉线索（输入中与查询相关的证据）替代答案侧特权。由于这些线索来源于推理阶段同样可获得的视觉输入，其证据可以被学生模型恢复。为此，ViCuR引入了一个轻量级线索恢复模块，在预填充阶段通过专属sink-token交叉注意力将任务相关的视觉证据聚合为内部表示，无需改变推理接口或引入额外的线索生成损失。在七个基准测试上，使用Qwen3-VL-2B和8B学生模型，ViCuR在整体平均性能上分别比基于答案的在线自蒸馏持续提升+1.19和+1.24。该方法还能自然地扩展到更强教师的OPD场景，分别超越OPD基线+0.64和+1.08，并在8B规模上取得一致的域外泛化收益。这些结果表明，在多模态在线策略蒸馏中，教师特权信号的设计与教师模型的强度同等重要。

</details>


### [40] [TextWand: A Unified Framework for Scene Text Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05730)
*Shuyu Wang,Zhile Guan,Hongxiu Chen,Yule Duan,Weiqi Li,Xin Shan,Ronggang Wang,Jian Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TextWand是一个统一的场景文本编辑通用框架，通过渲染和擦除的原子原语分解，结合ORPE和RAS两大创新设计，在文本移除、生成和替换三项任务上均超越现有开源和闭源模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法通常针对场景文本移除、生成或替换中的单一任务设计，缺乏能够统一处理多项编辑任务的通用框架；同时缺乏针对通用场景文本编辑的综合评测基准。

Method: 提出TextWand框架，将复杂编辑任务分解为渲染（rendering）和擦除（erasure）两个原子原语；引入Overlay-Reference Positional Encoding (ORPE)实现像素级布局保真度和基于示例的风格控制；提出Region-Adaptive Suppression (RAS)策略确保干净的文本擦除；并构建了TextWand-Bench综合评测基准。

Result: 在文本移除、生成和替换三项任务上，TextWand在文本内容准确度、布局与风格一致性以及整体图像质量方面均优于现有的领先开源和闭源模型。

Conclusion: 通过将复杂编辑任务分解为渲染和擦除原语，并设计ORPE和RAS两项关键技术，TextWand实现了通用、精准的场景文本编辑，为场景文本处理领域提供了一个统一的解决方案。

Original Abstract: We propose TextWand, a general-purpose framework that unifies scene text removal, generation, and replacement into a single model. By decomposing complex editing tasks into the atomic primitives of rendering and erasure, TextWand achieves precise control over both text appearance and background integrity. Specifically, we introduce a novel design, Overlay-Reference Positional Encoding (ORPE), to enforce pixel-level layout fidelity and exemplar-driven style control, alongside a new strategy, Region-Adaptive Suppression (RAS), to ensure clean text erasure. To address the absence of a comprehensive benchmark for general-purpose scene text editing among existing single-task datasets, we construct TextWand-Bench. Extensive experiments demonstrate that TextWand outperforms existing leading open-source and closed-source models by delivering superior text content accuracy, layout and style consistency, and overall image quality across scene text removal, generation and replacement tasks.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了TextWand，一个能够将场景文本移除、生成和替换统一到单一模型中的通用框架。通过将复杂的编辑任务分解为渲染和擦除两个原子原语，TextWand实现了对文本外观和背景完整性的精确控制。具体而言，我们引入了一种新颖设计——覆盖参考位置编码（ORPE），以强制执行像素级布局保真度和示例驱动的风格控制；同时提出了一种新策略——区域自适应抑制（RAS），以确保干净的文本擦除。为了解决现有单任务数据集缺乏通用场景文本编辑综合基准的问题，我们构建了TextWand-Bench。大量实验表明，TextWand在场景文本移除、生成和替换任务中，在文本内容准确度、布局和风格一致性以及整体图像质量方面均优于现有的领先开源和闭源模型。

</details>


### [41] [VTI-CoT: Visual-Textual Interleaved Chain of Thought for Video Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05736)
*Shufan Zhang,Ziyue Lin,Bairun Wang,Lei Jin,Xuanding Ding,Xinzhu Ma,Kunlin Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VTI-CoT框架，通过交错文本与视觉帧的多模态思维链推理，并采用OCR压缩技术提升长视频推理效率，在同等参数量下达到SOTA性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于CoT的视频推理方法仅依赖文本信息进行逻辑推理，忽略了推理过程中关键的视觉信息。受人类在推理时回顾视觉片段的认知机制启发，需要引入视觉-文本交错的推理方式。

Method: 提出VTI-CoT框架：(1) 将文本推理步骤与对应的视觉帧交错结合；(2) 开发自动化标注流水线构建高质量多模态CoT数据；(3) 针对长视频推理中CoT token序列过长的问题，采用基于OCR的压缩技术将CoT监督信号压缩到单一画布上。

Result: 在同等参数规模模型中达到SOTA性能，同时显著提升了训练效率。

Conclusion: 视觉-文本交错的CoT推理范式有效弥补了纯文本推理缺失视觉信息的不足，OCR压缩技术缓解了长序列带来的训练收敛和效率问题，验证了多模态CoT在视频推理中的优势。

Original Abstract: Video reasoning aims to understand complex temporal events and causal relationships within videos. Recently, Chain-of-Thought (CoT) has been introduced to this field to enhance reasoning accuracy. However, existing CoT-based video reasoning methods primarily rely on text-only information for logical deduction, overlooking critical visual information during the inference process. Inspired by the human cognitive mechanism of reviewing visual segments during inference, we propose VTI-CoT, a Visual-Textual Interleaved CoT framework. VTI-CoT integrates textual reasoning steps with corresponding visual frames. Given the scarcity of visual-textual interleaved CoT in existing datasets, we develop an automated annotation pipeline to construct high-quality multimodal CoT data. Further, reasoning over long-form videos entails increasingly long CoT token sequences, which severely hinders training convergence and efficiency. To address this, we employ Optical Character Recognition (OCR)-based compression techniques to compress CoT supervision signals into a single canvas. Experimental results demonstrate that VTI-CoT achieves state-of-the-art performance among models of the same parameter scale while significantly improving training efficiency.

中文摘要翻译: 视频推理旨在理解视频中复杂的时间事件和因果关系。近期，思维链（Chain-of-Thought, CoT）被引入该领域以提升推理准确性。然而，现有基于CoT的视频推理方法主要依赖纯文本信息进行逻辑推理，在推理过程中忽略了关键的视觉信息。受人类在推理时回顾视觉片段的认知机制启发，我们提出了VTI-CoT，一种视觉-文本交错的思维链框架。VTI-CoT将文本推理步骤与相应的视觉帧进行整合。针对现有数据集中缺乏视觉-文本交错CoT的问题，我们开发了自动化标注流水线来构建高质量的多模态CoT数据。此外，对长视频进行推理会导致CoT token序列越来越长，严重阻碍了训练的收敛和效率。为解决这一问题，我们采用基于光学字符识别（OCR）的压缩技术，将CoT监督信号压缩到单一画布上。实验结果表明，VTI-CoT在同等参数规模的模型中达到了最先进的性能，同时显著提升了训练效率。

</details>


### [42] [Let It Be Simple: One-Step Action Generation for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05737)
*Yitong Chen,Shiduo Zhang,Jingjing Gong,Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现VLA策略的动作生成与图像生成具有不同的条件-目标结构，通过简单偏置训练时的高噪声状态分布，无需教师模型、蒸馏或辅助目标，即可实现与十步解码相当的单步动作生成性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于扩散的VLA模型沿用了图像生成的观点，即通过迭代去噪生成动作。但本文指出VLA动作生成具有不同的条件-目标结构：策略以丰富的观测、语言和状态为条件，仅预测紧凑的低维动作片段。在这种不对称结构下，强单步动作生成不应必然需要为图像合成开发的先进单步方法。

Method: 保持标准的速度预测，不添加教师模型、蒸馏阶段或辅助目标，仅通过偏置训练时间分布朝向高噪声状态（即采用高噪声偏置的训练调度）来训练单步策略。

Result: 在标准LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus和LIBERO-Pro上，高噪声偏置训练的单步策略通常匹配同设置下的十步解码性能；在标准LIBERO上甚至能超越均匀时间分布训练的十步策略。在1.4B VLM模型上，单步解码在LIBERO-Long上达到95.6%。真实机器人双臂YAM RSS评估提供了小样本跨架构验证。

Conclusion: 强单步VLA动作生成可以源于标准扩散训练，无需引入为图像生成开发的完整少步扩散机制。

Original Abstract: Diffusion-based vision-language-action (VLA) models often inherit the image-generation view: actions are generated by iterative denoising. We argue that VLA action generation has a different condition-target structure: the policy is conditioned on rich observations, language, and state, but predicts only a compact, low-dimensional action chunk. Under this asymmetry, strong one-step action generation should not necessarily require the advanced one-step methods developed for image synthesis. We keep standard velocity prediction and add no teacher model, distillation stage, or auxiliary objective; in our main recipe, we simply bias the training time distribution toward high-noise states. We first isolate the effect in a controlled MNIST grid-to-sequence task, then test it with extensive robot-policy experiments. Across standard LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, and LIBERO-Pro, one-step policies trained with high-noise biased schedules generally match ten-step decoding under the same recipe, and on standard LIBERO can exceed ten-step policies trained with a uniform time distribution. A real-robot bimanual YAM RSS evaluation gives a small-sample cross-architecture check of the same sampler trend. On a 1.4B VLM model with a 30M action head, one-step decoding reaches 95.6\% on LIBERO-Long. These results show that strong one-step VLA action generation can emerge from standard diffusion training, without importing the full few-step diffusion machinery developed for image generation.

中文摘要翻译: 基于扩散的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型通常继承了图像生成的观点：动作通过迭代去噪生成。我们认为VLA动作生成具有不同的条件-目标结构：策略以丰富的观测、语言和状态为条件，但仅预测紧凑的低维动作片段。在这种不对称结构下，强单步动作生成不应必然需要为图像合成开发的先进单步方法。我们保持标准的速度预测，不添加教师模型、蒸馏阶段或辅助目标；在主要方案中，我们仅将训练时间分布偏置朝向高噪声状态。我们首先在受控的MNIST网格到序列任务中隔离该效应，然后通过大量机器人策略实验进行验证。在标准LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus和LIBERO-Pro上，采用高噪声偏置调度训练的单步策略通常匹配同设置下的十步解码性能，并且在标准LIBERO上可以超越使用均匀时间分布训练的十步策略。真实机器人双臂YAM RSS评估提供了相同采样器趋势的小样本跨架构验证。在具有30M动作头的1.4B VLM模型上，单步解码在LIBERO-Long上达到95.6%。这些结果表明，强单步VLA动作生成可以源于标准扩散训练，无需引入为图像生成开发的完整少步扩散机制。

</details>


### [43] [Cosine Misleads: Auxiliary Losses Reshape Vision Language Models, Not Their Latents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05753)
*XiuYu Zhang,Junfeng Fang,Zhenkai Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现视觉语言模型中的潜在视觉推理（LVR）存在一个反直觉现象：监督式潜在表示与视觉目标的对齐程度（余弦相似度）与模型准确率呈强负相关（r=-0.94），表明更好的对齐反而损害性能。作者提出PRISM诊断工具，揭示监督式潜在token在很大程度上被旁路，辅助目标通过共享参数而非潜在变量本身重塑语言模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有LVR方法假设潜在表示与视觉目标的对齐越好，最终答案质量越高，但该假设未经验证。作者希望检验这一核心假设是否成立，并解释对齐与准确率之间的关系。

Method: 设计五种LVR变体的矩阵进行对照实验；提出PRISM诊断框架，包含两个推理时诊断方法：线性探针（解码答案的位置）和损坏测试（潜在变量是否承载关键信息）。

Result: 余弦对齐与准确率呈显著负相关（r=-0.94）；损坏潜在变量最多导致准确率下降4个百分点；答案可在潜在变量下游解码但在潜在变量本身位置不可解码，该解码差距预测各变体对潜在变量的依赖程度。

Conclusion: LVR中的监督式潜在token在很大程度上被旁路，辅助目标函数实际上通过共享参数间接重塑语言模型，而非通过潜在变量本身；这符合信息瓶颈理论的解释。

Original Abstract: Latent visual reasoning (LVR) inserts supervised latent tokens between perception and answer generation in vision-language models (VLMs). The field uses alignment between these latents and their visual targets, i.e., cosine similarity or mean squared error (MSE), as both the training loss and the quality metric, assuming that better alignment yields a better answer. We test this with a designed matrix of five LVR variants and find the assumption inverted: cosine alignment is negatively correlated with accuracy across all five (r=-0.94). To explain this, we introduce PRISM, a pair of inference-time diagnostics: a linear probe that asks where the answer is decodable, and a corruption test that asks whether the latent is load-bearing. The supervised latents are largely bypassed. Corrupting them shifts accuracy by at most four points. The answer is decodable downstream of the latent but not at it, and the size of this decodability gap predicts how much each variant relies on its latent under perturbation. Consistent with an Information Bottleneck reading of the loss, the auxiliary objective reshapes the language model via shared parameters rather than via the latent variable it nominally optimizes.

中文摘要翻译: 潜在视觉推理（LVR）在视觉语言模型（VLM）的感知与答案生成之间插入监督式潜在标记。该领域使用这些潜在表示与其视觉目标之间的对齐（即余弦相似度或均方误差）作为训练损失和质量指标，假设更好的对齐能带来更好的答案。我们通过设计五种LVR变体的矩阵来验证这一假设，却发现该假设被反转：余弦对齐与所有五种变体的准确率呈负相关（r=-0.94）。为解释这一现象，我们引入PRISM——一组推理时诊断工具：一个线性探针用于探查答案在何处可解码，以及一个损坏测试用于检验潜在变量是否承载关键信息。结果表明，监督式潜在标记在很大程度上被旁路，损坏它们最多使准确率变化四个百分点。答案可在潜在变量下游解码，但在潜在变量本身位置不可解码，且这一解码差距的大小可预测各变体在扰动下对潜在变量的依赖程度。与损失函数的信息瓶颈解读一致，辅助目标函数通过共享参数而非名义上优化的潜在变量来重塑语言模型。

</details>


### [44] [DRIFT: A Residual Flow Adapter for Decoding Continuous Outputs in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05758)
*Zhuoming Liu,Jinhong Lin,Kwan Man Cheng,Lin Zhang,Shayok Bagchi,Yin Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DRIFT是一个通用框架，通过流匹配的生成式精炼模块，使预训练的视觉-语言模型（VLM）能够处理需要连续输出的任务（如时间边界定位和机器人控制），在感知和规划任务上均优于回归和生成式基线方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视觉-语言模型基于离散token的自回归解码，在文本输出任务上表现优异，但难以处理需要精确连续输出的问题（如事件时间边界定位、机器人控制动作生成）。

Method: DRIFT结合了一个提供粗估计的基础预测器与一个基于流匹配（flow matching）的生成式精炼模块，通过残差形式将生成建模从学习全局输出分布转化为在强先验周围建模局部残差分布，从而简化优化过程。

Result: 在视觉定位和机器人控制等多个感知与规划任务上，涵盖MLLM、VLA和WAM等多种架构，DRIFT consistently优于强回归和生成式基线方法。

Conclusion: DRIFT是一种有效的通用框架，能够将预训练的VLM适配到连续解码任务上，通过残差流匹配生成式精炼实现性能提升。

Original Abstract: Many modern vision-language models (VLMs) build on autoregressive decoding of discrete tokens. While text-based output interfaces enable scalable pretraining and strong zero-shot generalization across diverse tasks, they are poorly suited for problems that require precise continuous outputs, such as localizing temporal boundaries of events or generating robotic control actions. To address this challenge, we propose DRIFT, a general framework for adapting pretrained VLMs to continuous decoding tasks. DRIFT combines a base predictor, which provides a coarse estimate of the target output, with a generative refinement module based on flow matching that iteratively improves the prediction. This residual formulation transforms the generative modeling problem from learning a global output distribution to modeling a localized residual distribution around a strong prior, substantially simplifying optimization. We evaluate DRIFT on both perception and planning tasks, including visual grounding and robotic control. Across multiple tasks and architectures spanning MLLMs, VLAs, and WAMs, DRIFT consistently outperforms a strong set of regression- and generative-based solutions.

中文摘要翻译: 许多现代视觉-语言模型（VLM）建立在离散token的自回归解码之上。虽然基于文本的输出接口支持可扩展的预训练和跨多种任务的强零样本泛化能力，但它们难以处理需要精确连续输出的问题，例如定位事件的时间边界或生成机器人控制动作。为了解决这一挑战，我们提出了DRIFT，一个将预训练VLM适配到连续解码任务的通用框架。DRIFT结合了一个提供目标输出粗估计的基础预测器，以及一个基于流匹配（flow matching）的生成式精炼模块，该模块通过迭代方式逐步改进预测结果。这种残差形式将生成建模问题从学习全局输出分布转变为在强先验周围建模局部残差分布，从而大幅简化了优化过程。我们在感知和规划任务上评估了DRIFT，包括视觉定位和机器人控制。在跨越多模态大语言模型（MLLM）、视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）和世界动作模型（WAM）等多种架构的多个任务上，DRIFT consistently优于一组强回归和生成式基线方法。

</details>


### [45] [Physics-Guided Deep Unfolding for Blind Cross-Sensor Spectral Super-Resolution via Learning the Spectral Transformation Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05759)
*Zhaolin Li,Jinsong Chen,Shanxin Guo,Tuo Zhang,Xinglong Zhang,Pan Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对跨传感器场景下光谱响应函数未知导致的盲光谱超分辨率问题，提出物理引导的深度展开网络PGU-Net，通过联合估计高光谱图像和可学习的光谱变换函数，在多个数据集上取得优于现有方法的重建性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 高光谱成像提供丰富光谱信息但传感器成本高昂，在无人机平台部署有限。现有光谱超分辨率方法大多假设光谱响应函数固定已知，仅适用于单传感器场景；而实际跨传感器场景中，从高光谱到多光谱的退化过程未知且随传感器特性和场景内容变化，使得高光谱重建问题高度病态。

Method: 提出物理引导的深度展开网络PGU-Net，将交替优化过程展开为端到端可训练的多阶段架构，每个阶段依次更新高光谱图像和光谱变换函数。两个模块均结合可学习近端网络与可微闭式求解器，兼具物理可解释性与强表示能力。

Result: 在CAVE和NTIRE 2022基准数据集上使用多种SRF进行实验，结果表明PGU-Net能够准确恢复光谱变换函数（退化算子），且重建性能优于现有最先进方法。在真实无人机跨传感器数据集（Headwall Nano HSI与DJI P4 Multispectral MSI）上的评估验证了该方法在真正盲条件下的有效性和鲁棒性，且估计的STF可能表现出与土地覆盖相关的差异。

Conclusion: PGU-Net通过物理引导的深度展开框架有效解决了盲跨传感器光谱超分辨率问题，能够联合估计高光谱图像和未知的光谱变换函数，在合成数据和真实跨传感器数据上均展现出优越性能。

Original Abstract: Hyperspectral imaging provides rich spectral information for quantitative remote sensing, yet hyperspectral sensors remain costly and thus unavailable in many UAV deployments. Spectral super-resolution (SSR) seeks to reconstruct hyperspectral images (HSIs) from multispectral images (MSIs). Most existing SSR methods assume a fixed and known spectral response function (SRF) and are therefore limited to single-sensor settings. In practical cross-sensor scenarios, the spectral degradation from HSI to MSI is unknown and varies with sensor characteristics and scene content, which renders HSI reconstruction ill-posed. This paper proposes a physics-guided deep unfolding network, termed PGU-Net, to address blind cross-sensor SSR by jointly estimating the HSI and a learnable spectral transformation function (STF). PGU-Net unrolls an alternating optimization procedure into an end-to-end trainable architecture with stages, where each stage sequentially updates the HSI and the STF. Both modules combine learnable proximal networks with differentiable closed-form solvers, enabling physical interpretability while retaining strong representation capacity. Experiments on benchmark datasets (CAVE and NTIRE 2022) with multiple SRFs demonstrate accurate recovery of the STF (degradation operator) and improved reconstruction performance over state-of-the-art SSR methods. Furthermore, evaluations on a real UAV cross-sensor dataset (Headwall Nano HSI and DJI P4 Multispectral MSI) verify the effectiveness and robustness of PGU-Net under truly blind conditions, and suggest that the estimated STF may exhibit land-cover-related differences.

中文摘要翻译: 高光谱成像为定量遥感提供了丰富的光谱信息，但高光谱传感器成本高昂，因此在许多无人机部署中难以获得。光谱超分辨率（SSR）旨在从多光谱图像（MSI）重建高光谱图像（HSI）。现有大多数SSR方法假设光谱响应函数（SRF）固定且已知，因此局限于单传感器场景。在实际跨传感器场景中，从HSI到MSI的光谱退化过程是未知的，并随传感器特性和场景内容而变化，这使得HSI重建问题高度病态。本文提出一种物理引导的深度展开网络PGU-Net，通过联合估计HSI和可学习的光谱变换函数（STF）来解决盲跨传感器SSR问题。PGU-Net将交替优化过程展开为端到端可训练的多阶段架构，每个阶段依次更新HSI和STF。两个模块均结合了可学习近端网络与可微闭式求解器，在保持物理可解释性的同时具备强大的表示能力。在多个SRF下的基准数据集（CAVE和NTIRE 2022）上的实验表明，该方法能够准确恢复STF（退化算子），并在重建性能上优于现有最先进的SSR方法。此外，在真实无人机跨传感器数据集（Headwall Nano HSI和DJI P4 Multispectral MSI）上的评估验证了PGU-Net在真正盲条件下的有效性和鲁棒性，并表明估计的STF可能表现出与土地覆盖相关的差异。

</details>


### [46] [ExpSpeech-Net: Multimodal Fusion of Expression and Speech for Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05760)
*Ruchika Sharma,Rudresh Dwivedi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种轻量级多模态深度伪造检测模型ExpSpeech-Net (SqN-R-DFD)，结合SqueezeNet和RNN，通过智能特征选择和融合面部表情与语音模式，实现了94.5%的准确率，适用于实时检测场景。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有深度伪造视频检测方法多依赖复杂、资源密集型模型，限制了实际应用中的部署，因此需要一种轻量级、高效且适用于实时场景的检测框架。

Method: 以SqueezeNet和RNN（循环神经网络）为骨干网络，提取ISLBT图像特征和MPNCC信号特征，并采用SASMA（鲣鸟辅助黏菌算法）进行智能特征选择，实现多模态（面部表情与语音模式）融合检测。

Result: 准确率94.5%，精确率99.3%，F-measure 96.8%，优于传统检测方法。

Conclusion: 集成多模态信号与智能预处理及特征选择，能够实现适用于日常应用的高效、实时的深度伪造检测。

Original Abstract: Deepfake videos are increasingly challenging the credibility of online content. Many existing detection methodology relies on complex, resource-intensive models, which limit their practical use. The study introduces the ExpSpeech-Net deepfake detection (SqN-R-DFD) model, which utilizes SqueezeNet and RNN (Recurrent Neural Network) as its backbone, providing a lightweight and efficient deepfake detection framework that simultaneously analyzes facial expressions and speech patterns. The approach incorporates advanced feature extraction, such as ISLBT-based features for image and MPNCC for signals, along with a smart feature-selection strategy using SASMA (Sandpiper-Assisted Slime Mould Algorithm), ensuring optimal and balanced input to the detection models. By combining SqueezeNet and an RNN, subtle inconsistencies in deepfake videos are captured effectively. The framework achieves 94.5% accuracy, precision of 99.3%, and F-measure of 96.8%, outperforming conventional methods. This demonstrates that integrating multiple modalities with intelligent preprocessing and feature selection enables practical, real-time deepfake detection suitable for everyday applications.

中文摘要翻译: 深度伪造视频正日益挑战在线内容的可信度。许多现有的检测方法依赖于复杂且资源密集型的模型，限制了其实际应用。本研究提出了ExpSpeech-Net深度伪造检测模型（SqN-R-DFD），该模型以SqueezeNet和循环神经网络（RNN）为骨干网络，提供了一个轻量且高效的深度伪造检测框架，能够同步分析面部表情和语音模式。该方法引入了先进的特征提取技术，包括基于ISLBT的图像特征和基于MPNCC的信号特征，并采用SASMA（鲣鸟辅助黏菌算法）实现智能特征选择策略，确保检测模型获得最优且均衡的输入。通过结合SqueezeNet和RNN，有效捕捉了深度伪造视频中细微的不一致性。该框架实现了94.5%的准确率、99.3%的精确率和96.8%的F-measure，优于传统方法。这表明，将多模态信号与智能预处理及特征选择相结合，能够实现适用于日常应用场景的实用化实时深度伪造检测。

</details>


### [47] [Imagine Before You Predict: Interleaved Latent Visual Reasoning for Video Event Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05769)
*Tianxiang Jiang,Linquan Wu,Sheng Xia,Songze Li,Ziang Yan,Haoyu Yang,Yu Qiao,Yi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Future-L1框架，通过在自回归解码中交错使用语言token和连续潜在视觉跨度（latent visual spans），让多模态大语言模型在潜在空间而非文本空间中进行视频事件预测的中间推理，解决了现有方法因文本化视觉线索导致细节丢失和幻觉的问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频MLLMs通常将中间推理过程在文本空间中进行言语化，导致细粒度的运动、几何和交互线索丢失，产生看似合理但缺乏视觉依据的幻觉。因此，需要一种能保留中间视觉语义的推理方式。

Method: 提出Future-L1框架，采用交错潜在视觉推理机制，在自回归解码中交替生成语言token和连续潜在视觉跨度；构建Future-L1-50K数据集，选取未来视觉线索有助于预测的样本，并将潜在状态对齐到未来帧嵌入；进一步使用LA-DAPO（潜在感知的强化学习目标，结合结果对比和时序多样性奖励）优化采样的潜在轨迹。

Result: 在FutureBench上，将Qwen3-VL-8B从61.0提升至85.4，超过先前最佳Video-CoE 10.4个百分点；在TwiFF-Bench上，平均得分从2.44提升至3.04。

Conclusion: 面向未来的视频推理受益于在潜在空间中保留中间视觉语义，而非将每个推理步骤都翻译成文本。

Original Abstract: Video event prediction (VEP) requires models to infer unobserved future states from partial video evidence. Existing video MLLMs usually verbalize intermediate future reasoning in text space: once visual evidence is verbalized, fine-grained motion, geometry, and interaction cues can be lost, leading to plausible but visually ungrounded hallucinations. We introduce Future-L1, an interleaved latent visual reasoning framework that lets an MLLM alternate between language tokens and continuous latent visual spans during autoregressive decoding. To train this capability, we construct Future-L1-50K by selecting examples where future visual hints help prediction and align latent states to future-frame embeddings, then further optimize sampled latent trajectories with LA-DAPO, a latent-aware RL objective with outcome-contrastive and temporal-diversity rewards. Future-L1 achieves new state-of-the-art results on both benchmarks: on FutureBench, it improves Qwen3-VL-8B from 61.0 to 85.4 and exceeds the previous best Video-CoE by 10.4 points; on TwiFF-Bench, it improves the average score from 2.44 to 3.04. These results suggest that future-oriented video reasoning benefits from preserving intermediate visual semantics in latent space rather than translating every reasoning step into text.

中文摘要翻译: 视频事件预测要求模型从部分视频证据中推断未观察到的未来状态。现有视频多模态大语言模型通常将中间未来推理在文本空间中进行言语化：一旦视觉证据被言语化，细粒度的运动、几何和交互线索就可能丢失，导致看似合理但缺乏视觉依据的幻觉。我们提出Future-L1，一个交错潜在视觉推理框架，允许多模态大语言模型在自回归解码过程中交替使用语言token和连续潜在视觉跨度。为了训练这种能力，我们构建了Future-L1-50K数据集，选取未来视觉线索有助于预测的样本，并将潜在状态对齐到未来帧嵌入；然后使用LA-DAPO（一种潜在感知的强化学习目标，结合结果对比奖励和时序多样性奖励）进一步优化采样的潜在轨迹。Future-L1在两个基准上均取得了新的最优结果：在FutureBench上，将Qwen3-VL-8B从61.0提升至85.4，超过先前最佳Video-CoE 10.4个百分点；在TwiFF-Bench上，平均得分从2.44提升至3.04。这些结果表明，面向未来的视频推理受益于在潜在空间中保留中间视觉语义，而非将每个推理步骤都翻译成文本。

</details>


### [48] [LiAuto-GeoX: Efficient Grounded Driving Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05774)
*Jiawei Lian,Haoyi Sun,Yang Wu,Lifu Mu,Siyuan Wang,Le Hui,Ning Mao,Tao Wei,Pan Zhou,Kun Zhan,Jian Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LiAuto-GeoX 是一个高效的可部署驾驶Transformer，通过几何保持蒸馏框架将大规模驾驶几何模型压缩为155M参数的轻量模型，在KITTI上达到220 FPS，实现实时高保真稠密3D重建，并成功迁移至轨迹预测、占据预测等下游任务。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有大规模视觉几何模型计算资源需求大，且缺乏长距离几何保真度、环视一致性和实时效率，无法满足自动驾驶动态环境对实时机载稠密3D重建的要求。

Method: 首先利用大规模环视数据和稀疏LiDAR先验学习高容量驾驶几何模型；然后通过几何保持蒸馏框架将其压缩为155M参数的机载模型。蒸馏框架包含：1) 掩码引导的深度感知蒸馏，通过强调几何信息区域保留细粒度度量结构；2) 相对姿态关系蒸馏，通过姿态诱导的几何关系强制跨视角空间一致性。

Result: 在KITTI上达到220 FPS；轨迹预测PDMS 90.6，占据预测mIoU 24.63，未来帧预测IoU 47.67。

Conclusion: 高效的稠密3D重建可以超越传统的感知目标角色，作为一种可扩展的基础几何表征服务于下一代自动驾驶。

Original Abstract: Dense 3D reconstruction has demonstrated immense potential for spatial understanding, yet its viability as a real-time, onboard representation for autonomous driving remains an open challenge. Existing large-scale visual geometry models typically require substantial computational resources and lack the long-range geometric fidelity, surround-view consistency, and real-time efficiency demanded by dynamic driving environments. To bridge this gap, we present \textbf{LiAuto-GeoX}, an efficient grounded driving transformer designed for deployable, ego-centric 3D scene understanding. Our approach begins by learning a high-capacity driving geometry model from large-scale surround-view data, utilizing sparse LiDAR priors to provide robust geometric grounding in distant, ambiguous, or structure-sparse regions. We then instantiate this capability into a highly compact 155M-parameter onboard model through a novel geometry-preserving distillation framework. This framework employs mask-guided depth-aware distillation to retain fine-grained metric structures by emphasizing geometrically informative regions, and relative-pose relational distillation to enforce cross-view spatial consistency through pose-induced geometric relations. Extensive evaluations reveal that \textbf{LiAuto-GeoX} runs at 220 FPS on KITTI while maintaining high-fidelity dense reconstruction, enabling real-time deployment. The learned geometry transfers seamlessly to downstream autonomy tasks, achieving 90.6 PDMS in trajectory prediction, 24.63 mIoU in occupancy prediction, and 47.67 IoU in future-frame prediction. These all demonstrate that efficient dense 3D reconstruction can transcend its traditional role as a perception target to serve as a scalable, foundational geometric representation for next-generation autonomous driving.

中文摘要翻译: 稠密三维重建在空间理解方面展现出巨大潜力，但其作为自动驾驶实时机载表示的可行性仍然是一个悬而未决的挑战。现有的大规模视觉几何模型通常需要大量计算资源，且缺乏动态驾驶环境所要求的长距离几何保真度、环视一致性和实时效率。为弥合这一差距，我们提出\textbf{LiAuto-GeoX}——一种高效的可部署接地驾驶Transformer，用于以自我为中心的三维场景理解。我们的方法首先从大规模环视数据中学习高容量驾驶几何模型，利用稀疏LiDAR先验在遥远、模糊或结构稀疏区域提供稳健的几何接地。随后，通过一种新颖的几何保持蒸馏框架，将该能力实例化为高度紧凑的155M参数机载模型。该框架采用掩码引导的深度感知蒸馏，通过强调几何信息丰富的区域来保留细粒度度量结构；以及相对姿态关系蒸馏，通过姿态诱导的几何关系强制跨视角空间一致性。大量评估表明，\textbf{LiAuto-GeoX}在KITTI上以220 FPS运行，同时保持高保真稠密重建，实现了实时部署。所学习的几何表征无缝迁移到下游自主驾驶任务，在轨迹预测中达到90.6 PDMS，在占据预测中达到24.63 mIoU，在未来帧预测中达到47.67 IoU。这些结果共同证明，高效的稠密三维重建可以超越其作为感知目标的传统角色，作为下一代自动驾驶中可扩展的基础几何表征。

</details>


### [49] [Beyond Absolute Scores: Relative Edit-induced Difference for Generalizable Image Aesthetic Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05778)
*Qifei Jia,Xintong Yao,Minghao Li,Yajie Chai,Qiming Lu,Baoyue Shen,Yasen Zhang,Runyu Shi,Ying Huang,Yue Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RED-Aes框架，利用可控图像编辑模型模拟人类审美推理过程，通过相对审美差异学习而非绝对MOS回归，实现更好的泛化性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统IAA方法依赖回归绝对MOS分数，忽视了人类审美感知的动态比较特性，缺乏对审美差异的因果推理能力，导致模型难以学习可泛化的审美原则。

Method: 提出RED-Aes框架，包含三部分：(1) RED-20k数据集，包含编辑图像对、定量审美差异和CoT推理；(2) 利用可控图像编辑模型模拟人类审美推理；(3) 三阶段训练策略，由相对排序一致性奖励引导，仅通过相对监督优化模型。

Result: 在多个公开基准上达到最先进性能，展现出优异的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过相对编辑诱导的差异学习范式替代传统绝对分数回归，能够更有效地学习可泛化的审美原则，显著提升模型在多样化场景下的表现。

Original Abstract: Traditional Image Aesthetic Assessment (IAA) methods mainly rely on regressing absolute Mean Opinion Scores (MOS). However, such a paradigm overlooks the inherently dynamic nature of human aesthetic perception, which relies on subconscious comparison against implicit visual references. Consequently, the lack of causal reasoning regarding aesthetic differences prevents models from learning generalizable aesthetic principles, thus limiting their generalization across diverse scenarios. In this work, we rethink the IAA task and propose Relative Edit-induced Difference Aesthetic learning (RED-Aes), a novel framework that leverages controllable image editing models to simulate the human aesthetic reasoning process. Instead of fitting absolute score distributions, RED-Aes explicitly learns the visual factors that drive aesthetic changes. To support this paradigm, we construct the RED-20k dataset, which comprises editing-based image pairs, quantitative aesthetic differences, and Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning. Furthermore, we introduce a three-stage training strategy guided by a relative ranking consistency reward, optimizing the model solely via relative supervision. Extensive experiments demonstrate that RED-Aes achieves state-of-the-art performance on multiple public benchmarks, exhibiting superior generalization capabilities.

中文摘要翻译: 传统的图像美学评估（IAA）方法主要依赖于回归绝对平均意见分数（MOS）。然而，这种范式忽视了人类审美感知固有的动态特性，即它依赖于针对隐含视觉参考的无意识比较。因此，缺乏对美学差异的因果推理能力使得模型无法学习可泛化的审美原则，从而限制了其在多样化场景中的泛化能力。本文重新思考IAA任务，提出了一种新颖框架——相对编辑诱导差异美学学习（RED-Aes），该框架利用可控图像编辑模型来模拟人类审美推理过程。RED-Aes不拟合绝对分数分布，而是显式学习驱动美学变化的视觉因素。为支持这一范式，我们构建了RED-20k数据集，包含基于编辑的图像对、定量美学差异和思维链（CoT）推理。此外，我们引入了一种由相对排序一致性奖励引导的三阶段训练策略，仅通过相对监督来优化模型。大量实验表明，RED-Aes在多个公开基准上取得了最先进的性能，展现出优异的泛化能力。

</details>


### [50] [Next-Generation Parallel Decoder for LPDR: Architectural Optimization and Class-Balanced GAN-Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05785)
*Shawaiz Obaid,Nida Chandio,Neha Jamil,Muhammad Khuram Shahzad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出交叉空间混合注意力（CSHA）和类平衡合成增强（CBSA）方法，有效解决了YOLOV5-PDLPR模型中空间字符不匹配和数据不平衡问题，将少数省份车牌识别率从78.2%提升至91.5%，并保持152 FPS的实时处理速度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: YOLOV5-PDLPR模型虽然通过并行解码器提升了系统效率，但其性能仍受训练集中空间字符不匹配和数据不平衡问题的制约，导致少数省份车牌识别率较低。

Method: 提出交叉空间混合注意力（CSHA）机制和类平衡合成增强（CBSA）方法。CSHA用于解决空间字符不匹配问题，CBSA通过数据增强缓解训练集中的类别不平衡。利用75,000个合成样本进行广泛实验。

Result: 在CCPD、CLPD、PKU及应用特定数据集四个基准上，少数省份车牌识别率从78.2%提升至91.5%，同时保持152 FPS的实时处理性能。

Conclusion: 空间感知的并行解码结合类平衡增强为高速车牌识别系统提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Real-Time License Plate Detection and Recognition (LPDR) forms the backbone of modern smart cities. Although the YOLOV5-PDLPR model substantially improved system efficiency through a parallel decoder approach, its performance is still affected by spatial character mismatches and data imbalance within the training set. This paper addresses these limitations by introducing Cross-Spatial Hybrid Attention (CSHA) and Class-Balanced Synthetic Augmentation (CBSA). An extensive study involving 75,000 synthetic samples is conducted and evaluated on four benchmarks: CCPD, CLPD, PKU, and an application-specific dataset. Experimental results demonstrate a substantial improvement in the recognition rate of minority provincial license plates from 78.2% to 91.5% while maintaining real-time processing performance of 152 FPS. The results indicate that spatially-aware parallel decoding combined with class-balanced augmentation provides an effective solution for high-speed license plate recognition systems.

中文摘要翻译: 实时车牌检测与识别（LPDR）构成了现代智慧城市的基石。尽管YOLOV5-PDLPR模型通过并行解码器方法显著提升了系统效率，但其性能仍然受到训练集中空间字符不匹配和数据不平衡的影响。本文通过引入交叉空间混合注意力（CSHA）和类平衡合成增强（CBSA）来解决这些局限性。基于75,000个合成样本的广泛研究在四个基准数据集上进行了评估：CCPD、CLPD、PKU和一个应用特定数据集。实验结果表明，少数省份车牌识别率从78.2%显著提升至91.5%，同时保持152 FPS的实时处理性能。研究结果表明，空间感知的并行解码结合类平衡增强为高速车牌识别系统提供了一种有效的解决方案。

</details>


### [51] [Emotion-Aware Image Generation from Korean Diary Text via LLM-based Prompt Translation and LoRA Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05816)
*Jihun Cho,Soo-Yeon Jeong,Sun-Young Ihm*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对现有T2I模型忽视文本情感的问题，提出一个结合Qwen3-8B情感识别与Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium（LoRA微调）的情感感知文本到图像生成流水线，用于将韩语短日记生成为儿童手绘风格图像，并研究了情感触发词的影响及CLIP Score作为评估指标的局限性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有T2I模型主要关注与视觉对象相关的模式，而非上下文情感理解，无法有效捕捉日记等多种类型文本中的情感。因此需要一种能够识别隐含情感并生成情感感知图像的流水线。

Method: 提出情感感知文本到图像生成流水线：使用Qwen3-8B从短韩语日记中识别隐含情感；使用基于LoRA微调的Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium，在儿童手绘图像上以情感触发词进行微调，用于图像生成。

Result: 通过实验研究了情感触发词对生成图像的影响，并指出CLIP Score作为情感感知图像生成评估指标的局限性。

Conclusion: 该流水线能够从短韩语日记中生成带有情感的儿童手绘风格图像，但CLIP Score在评估情感感知图像生成方面存在不足，需要更合适的评估方法。

Original Abstract: T2I models cannot effectively capture sentiment from various types of text, including diaries, as they primarily focus on visual object-related patterns rather than contextual emotional understanding. This paper proposes an emotion-aware text-to-image pipeline that generates children's hand drawing style images from short Korean diary entries. The proposed pipeline employs Qwen3-8B for recognising implicit sentiment from short diaries, and Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium fine-tuned with LoRA on children's drawing images with emotion-based trigger words for image generation. Additionally, this paper presents experiments examining the effect of emotion trigger words on generated images and discusses the limitations of CLIP Score as an evaluation metric for emotion-aware image generation.

中文摘要翻译: T2I模型无法有效捕捉来自日记等多种类型文本的情感，因为它们主要关注与视觉对象相关的模式，而非上下文情感理解。本文提出了一种情感感知的文本到图像生成流水线，用于从短韩语日记条目生成儿童手绘风格的图像。该流水线采用Qwen3-8B识别短日记中的隐含情感，并使用基于LoRA在儿童手绘图像上以情感触发词进行微调的Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium生成图像。此外，本文通过实验考察了情感触发词对生成图像的影响，并讨论了CLIP Score作为情感感知图像生成评估指标的局限性。

</details>


### [52] [Gender Artifacts from Art History to Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05829)
*Piera Riccio,Miriam Doh,Benedikt Höltgen,Noa Garcia,Nanne van Noord*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次提出研究历史图像与AI生成图像中艺术风格与性别表征的相互影响，构建了包含74k图像的数据集StyleGender，并提出两种性别伪影度量指标（PixelSGA和MaskSGA），揭示性别表征在艺术风格中的编码模式，并发现生成模型会放大历史来源中的性别伪影。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 艺术风格根植于社会历史语境，编码了包括性别建构在内的社会等级制度，但AI研究长期将风格仅视为表面视觉属性（颜色、笔触、纹理等）。作者旨在填补这一空白，系统探究艺术风格与性别表征之间的相互作用。

Method: (1) 构建StyleGender数据集，包含74k图像，涵盖19种艺术风格；(2) 提出两种Set Gender Artifact (SGA)度量指标：PixelSGA（像素级性别信号）和MaskSGA（构图结构中的性别信号）；(3) 进行文本到图像（T2I）生成实验，在受控风格和性别提示下生成图像，并与历史图像进行语义对齐比较。

Result: (1) 性别表征塑造了跨艺术风格的视觉特征；(2) 风格关键词将这些模式带入T2I生成过程；(3) 生成模型倾向于放大历史来源中观察到的性别伪影。

Conclusion: 艺术风格并非性别中立的表面视觉属性，而是编码了性别表征模式；生成模型不仅继承了这些模式，还进一步放大了它们，这提示在AI生成内容中需要关注性别偏见的加剧问题。

Original Abstract: Artistic styles are rooted in specific socio-historical contexts that encode social hierarchies, including distinct constructions of gender. Yet in AI research, style has long been treated as a surface-level visual property: a filter of color, brushstroke, and texture applied to otherwise content-neutral scenes. We introduce the first dataset to investigate the interplay between gender representation and style in both historical and generated images. StyleGender comprises 74k images spanning 19 artistic styles, comprising art historical images with style and gender annotations, T2I-generated images under controlled style and gender prompts, and a semantically aligned set enabling direct art history-to-generation comparison. By proposing two Set Gender Artifact (SGA) metrics (PixelSGA and MaskSGA), capturing gender signals at the pixel level and in compositional structure, we show that (1) gender representation shapes visual features across artistic styles, (2) style keywords carry these patterns into T2I generation, and (3) generative models tend to amplify gender artifacts beyond what is observed in historical sources.

中文摘要翻译: 艺术风格根植于特定的社会历史语境，这些语境编码了包括不同性别建构在内的社会等级制度。然而在AI研究中，风格长期被视为一种表面视觉属性：即应用于内容中性场景的颜色、笔触和纹理滤镜。我们首次提出一个用于探究历史图像与生成图像中性别表征与风格相互作用的数据集。StyleGender包含跨越19种艺术风格的74k幅图像，涵盖带有风格和性别标注的艺术史图像、在受控风格和性别提示下由T2I生成的图像，以及一组实现艺术史与生成直接对比的语义对齐集合。通过提出两种集合性别伪影（SGA）度量指标（PixelSGA和MaskSGA），分别从像素层面和构图结构层面捕获性别信号，我们发现：(1) 性别表征塑造了跨艺术风格的视觉特征；(2) 风格关键词将这些模式带入T2I生成过程；(3) 生成模型倾向于放大历史来源中观察到的性别伪影。

</details>


### [53] [Learning Geometric Representations from Videos for Spatial Intelligent Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05833)
*Haibo Wang,Lifu Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoVR提出了一种利用纯2D视频序列为多模态大模型（MLLM）学习几何表征的框架，通过从预训练3D基础模型中蒸馏几何知识并结合多目标学习（相机位姿、深度图、度量尺度因子、多尺度3D特征），重塑MLLM内部表征以解锁空间智能，在空间推理基准上达到SOTA。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 多模态大模型在2D语义理解上表现出色，但缺乏内在的3D感知能力，导致跨视频帧的表征无法保持几何和空间一致性。同时，大规模3D数据稀缺，因此需要一种仅依赖2D数据就能赋予模型3D空间智能的方法。

Method: GeoVR通过从预训练3D基础模型中蒸馏几何知识来重塑MLLM的内部表征。采用多目标学习策略，包含四个互补的几何目标：(1)估计帧间相机位姿以建模视角动态变化；(2)回归稠密深度图以锚定物理距离；(3)预测度量尺度因子用于真实世界校准；(4)蒸馏多尺度3D特征以对齐中间特征空间。

Result: 在空间推理基准上的大量实验表明，GeoVR取得了最先进的性能，验证了该框架能有效赋予基础模型空间智能。

Conclusion: GeoVR通过纯2D视频序列学习几何表征，成功为多模态大模型注入了3D意识，为赋予基础模型空间智能建立了新范式。

Original Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) excel at 2D semantic understanding but lack intrinsic 3D awareness, resulting in representations that fail to maintain geometric and spatial consistency across video frames. Given the scarcity of large-scale 3D data, we present GeoVR, a novel framework that learns geometric representations using purely 2D video sequences. This approach effectively restructures the semantic latent space within MLLMs to unlock spatial intelligence. Rather than employing superficial feature mixing, GeoVR reshapes the internal representations of the MLLM by distilling geometry knowledge from pre-trained 3D foundation models. This is accomplished through a multi-objective learning strategy driven by four complementary geometric targets: (1) estimating inter-frame camera poses to embed varying viewpoint dynamics, (2) regressing dense depth maps to anchor physical distances, (3) predicting a metric scale factor for real-world calibration, and (4) distilling multi-scale 3D features to align the intermediate feature space. Guided by these explicit physical and geometric constraints, the model's internal representations naturally develop strong 3D awareness. Extensive experiments on spatial reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that GeoVR achieves state-of-the-art performance, establishing a new paradigm for endowing foundation models with spatial intelligence.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态大语言模型在二维语义理解方面表现出色，但缺乏内在的三维感知能力，导致其表征无法在视频帧之间保持几何和空间一致性。鉴于大规模三维数据的稀缺性，我们提出了GeoVR——一个仅利用纯二维视频序列来学习几何表征的新型框架。该方法有效重构了多模态大语言模型内部的语义潜空间，以解锁空间智能。GeoVR并非采用浅层的特征混合，而是通过从预训练的三维基础模型中蒸馏几何知识来重塑多模态大语言模型的内部表征。这是通过一个由四个互补几何目标驱动的多目标学习策略实现的：(1)估计帧间相机位姿以嵌入变化的视角动态；(2)回归稠密深度图以锚定物理距离；(3)预测度量尺度因子用于真实世界校准；(4)蒸馏多尺度三维特征以对齐中间特征空间。在这些显式的物理和几何约束的引导下，模型的内部表征自然发展出强大的三维感知能力。在空间推理基准上的大量实验表明，GeoVR取得了最先进的性能，为赋予基础模型空间智能建立了新范式。

</details>


### [54] [Geometry-Aware Dataset Condensation for Diffusion Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05883)
*Xiao Cui,Yulei Qin,Mo Zhu,Wengang Zhou,Hongsheng Li,Houqiang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于几何感知分布对齐的数据集浓缩方法（GADC），通过单向部分最优传输选择实子集以保留数据几何结构，结合轻量特征统计与语义一致性正则化，实现高效的扩散模型训练。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有数据集浓缩方法不适用于扩散模型训练：合成数据生成常产生低保真样本，而实子集选择无法保留扩散模型似然目标所需的分布几何结构。

Method: 将实子集选择重新形式化为几何感知的分布对齐问题，利用单向部分最优传输选择性对齐紧凑子集与完整数据分布，允许低密度区域未匹配质量，并辅以特征统计和语义一致性正则化，采用两阶段离散优化策略求解。

Result: 在多种扩散变体、子集大小、图像分辨率和训练轮次下的实验表明，该方法在扩散模型训练中实现了优越的保真度和分布覆盖。

Conclusion: 所提出的几何感知分布对齐方法（GADC）有效解决了现有数据集浓缩方法在扩散模型训练中的不足，通过保留关键几何结构实现了更好的训练效果。

Original Abstract: Dataset condensation aims to construct compact datasets from real data via synthesis or selection. However, existing approaches are ill-suited for diffusion model training: synthetic data generation often yields low-fidelity samples unsuitable for authentic modeling, while real subset selection typically fails to preserve the distributional geometry required by diffusion likelihood objectives. To address this, we propose to reformulate real subset selection as a geometry-aware distribution alignment problem. By incorporating one-sided partial optimal transport, our method selectively aligns a compact subset with the full data distribution while allowing unmatched mass in low-density regions, ensuring the preserved geometric structure necessary for effective diffusion model training. To further ensure distributional fidelity, we complement geometric alignment with lightweight feature-statistics and semantic consistency regularization. An efficient two-stage discrete optimization strategy is proposed to achieve this alignment objective. Extensive experiments across diffusion variants, subset sizes, image resolutions, and training rounds show that our method achieves superior fidelity and distributional coverage in diffusion model training. Codes are available at https://github.com/2018cx/GADC.

中文摘要翻译: 数据集浓缩旨在通过合成或选择的方式，从真实数据构建紧凑数据集。然而，现有方法不适合扩散模型训练：合成数据生成常产生低保真样本，无法用于真实建模；而真实子集选择通常无法保留扩散模型似然目标所需的分布几何结构。为解决这一问题，我们提出将真实子集选择重新形式化为几何感知的分布对齐问题。通过引入单向部分最优传输，我们的方法选择性地将紧凑子集与完整数据分布对齐，同时允许低密度区域中存在未匹配质量，从而确保保留扩散模型有效训练所需的几何结构。为进一步保证分布保真度，我们用轻量级特征统计和语义一致性正则化来补充几何对齐。我们提出了一种高效的两阶段离散优化策略来实现这一对齐目标。在多种扩散变体、子集大小、图像分辨率和训练轮次上的大量实验表明，我们的方法在扩散模型训练中实现了优越的保真度和分布覆盖。代码可在 https://github.com/2018cx/GADC 获取。

</details>


### [55] [Resonant Minds: Closed-Loop Social Avatars with Theory of Mind](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05896)
*Jianxu Shangguan,Jing Xu,Hang Ye,Xiaoxuan Ma,Yizhou Wang,Wentao Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个闭环双智能体框架，将感知、社会推理（心理理论）和多模态表达（对话+双人视频生成）统一到连续交互循环中，在人设-场景数据集上表现出优于或媲美现有方法的对话质量和视频生成效果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法将认知推理与多模态生成割裂：LLM擅长对话但缺乏具身表达，基于扩散的说话头模型视觉保真度高但忽视社会认知，亟需一个统一框架来生成具有真实社会智能的数字人。

Method: 提出闭环双智能体框架：(1) 感知模块从视频分析对方的 multimodal 行为；(2) 社会推理模块通过心理理论推断隐藏心理状态，并利用集成机制选择回应；(3) 表达模块生成情感可控的双智能体视频，合成说话者的言语表情和听者的反应行为，捕捉双向动态。

Result: 在自建的人设-场景数据集上，该方法在对话质量和视频生成指标上达到或超越现有方法；尤其在关键对话质量维度上超越了全信息脚本模式，表明不确定性下的显式心理状态推断能激发更周到的对话。

Conclusion: 将认知推理与多模态生成统一在闭环交互框架中是可行的，显式建模心理状态不仅能提升社交智能，还能在信息不对称场景下生成比充分信息更优质的对话。

Original Abstract: Creating lifelike digital humans with genuine social intelligence requires unifying cognitive reasoning and multimodal generation within a coherent framework. Current approaches treat these as separate tasks: Large Language Models excel at dialogue but lack embodied expression, while diffusion-based talking head models achieve visual fidelity but ignore social cognition. To bridge this gap, we propose a closed-loop dual-agent framework integrating perception, social reasoning, and expression into a continuous interaction cycle. The perception module analyzes partners' multimodal behaviors from video, while the social reasoning module infers hidden mental states through Theory of Mind and selects responses via an ensemble mechanism. The expression module then generates emotion-controllable dual-agent videos synthesizing both speaker speech and expression alongside listener reactive behaviors, capturing bidirectional dynamics absent in prior work. We construct a hierarchical Persona-Scenario dataset with psychologically grounded personas and private social goals to support evaluation under information asymmetry. Experiments on this dataset demonstrate competitive or superior performance on both dialogue quality and video generation metrics. Notably, our method surpasses even the full-information Script mode on key dialogue quality dimensions, suggesting that explicit mental state inference under uncertainty can elicit more thoughtful dialogue than unrestricted information access.

中文摘要翻译: 创建具有真实社会智能的数字人需要将认知推理与多模态生成统一在一个连贯的框架内。现有方法将二者视为分离任务：大语言模型擅长对话但缺乏具身表达，而基于扩散的说话头模型实现了视觉保真度但忽视了社会认知。为弥合这一差距，我们提出了一个闭环双智能体框架，将感知、社会推理和表达整合到连续的交互循环中。感知模块从视频中分析对方的多模态行为，社会推理模块通过心理理论推断隐藏的心理状态，并利用集成机制选择回应。表达模块随后生成情感可控的双智能体视频，同时合成说话者的言语表情和听者的反应行为，捕捉先前工作中缺失的双向动态。我们构建了一个分层的人设-场景数据集，包含基于心理学的角色设定和私有社交目标，以支持信息不对称下的评估。在该数据集上的实验表明，我们的方法在对话质量和视频生成指标上均表现出竞争性或优越的性能。值得注意的是，我们的方法在关键对话质量维度上甚至超越了全信息脚本模式，这表明不确定性下显式的心理状态推断比无限制的信息访问能激发更周到的对话。

</details>


### [56] [Self-Learning Expression Deformations for Data-Efficient Gaussian Avatars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05912)
*Jiahao Yang,Xiaohang Yang,Qing Wang,Yilan Dong,Gregory Slabaugh,Shanxin Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了SAGE框架，通过自监督学习高斯形变，从极小数据输入（单帧多视图、仅头部旋转的单目、无需预训练的一次性）即可构建高保真、可动画化的3D高斯虚拟人，数据需求降低数个数量级。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有3D高斯虚拟人方法依赖大量多视图和时序表情数据，可扩展性和可访问性受限，难以从稀疏输入中建模动态表情。

Method: 联合优化2D高斯面元和符号距离场(SDF)以约束表面对齐的高斯分布；设计自监督表情学习阶段，通过几何和外观一致性约束替代长序列训练；支持多视图（单帧）、单目（仅头部旋转）、一次性（无预训练）三种重建模式。

Result: 在重建和动画质量上与最先进方法相当，但数据需求降低数个数量级。

Conclusion: 自监督高斯形变学习是实现数据高效、易获取的虚拟人创建的有前景的方向。

Original Abstract: Modeling dynamic facial expressions using 3D Gaussian representations remains challenging due to their unstructured nature. Conventional Gaussian avatar pipelines require extensive multiview and sequential expression data, limiting scalability and accessibility. In this work, we introduce Self-Adaptive Gaussian Expression (SAGE), a framework for self-learning expression-induced Gaussian deformations that enables high-fidelity, animatable avatars from minimal input data. Our method jointly optimizes 2D Gaussian surfels and a Signed Distance Field (SDF) to enforce compact, surface-aligned Gaussian distributions, while a self-supervised expression learning phase replaces long training sequences with geometric and appearance consistency constraints. This design allows flexible deployment across multiple reconstruction regimes: in the multiview setting, only a single frame (timestep) is required instead of thousands; in the monocular setting, only head rotations are needed without expression sequences; and in the one-shot setting, no pretraining or priors are necessary. Experiments demonstrate that our approach achieves reconstruction and animation quality comparable to state-of-the-art methods, while reducing data requirements by several orders of magnitude. Our results highlight the potential of self-supervised Gaussian deformation learning as a step toward accessible, data-efficient avatar creation.

中文摘要翻译: 使用3D高斯表示建模动态面部表情由于其非结构化特性仍具挑战性。传统的虚拟人高斯管线需要大量多视图和时序表情数据，限制了可扩展性和可访问性。本文提出自适应高斯表情（SAGE）框架，一种自学习表情诱导高斯形变的方法，能够从极小输入数据中生成高保真、可动画化的虚拟人。我们的方法联合优化2D高斯面元和符号距离场（SDF），以强制实现紧凑、表面对齐的高斯分布；同时，自监督表情学习阶段通过几何和外观一致性约束替代了长序列训练。该设计允许在多种重建场景下灵活部署：在多视图设置下，仅需单帧（时间步）而非数千帧；在单目设置下，仅需头部旋转而无需表情序列；在一次性设置下，无需预训练或先验知识。实验表明，我们的方法在重建和动画质量上可与最先进方法媲美，同时将数据需求降低数个数量级。我们的结果凸显了自监督高斯形变学习作为迈向数据高效、易获取的虚拟人创建步骤的潜力。

</details>


### [57] [CamFlow+: Hybrid Motion Bases for 2D Camera Motion Estimation with Stabilization Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05915)
*Haipeng Li,Zhen Liu,Zhanglei Yang,Hai Jiang,Tianhao Zhou,Zhengzhe Liu,Ping Tan,Bing Zeng,Shuaicheng Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CamFlow+提出了一种混合基框架，直接在密集光流空间中表示2D相机运动，结合物理基、随机基和深度平移基，放松了单平面约束，在相机运动估计和视频稳像任务上取得了更好效果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于单应性的方法在处理相机平移、深度变化和局部视差时表现不佳；局部单应性和网格模型虽然提升了灵活性，但仍依赖分片平面假设。因此需要一种能直接表示连续深度区域中相机运动且不受平面约束的方法。

Method: CamFlow+提出混合基框架，包含三种基：从单应性导出的物理基、从单应性流中采样的随机基、以及从深度和相机内参导出的深度平移基。同时引入深度感知平滑项，在连续深度区域正则化平移引起的视差，同时在深度边界附近保留运动变化。

Result: 在GHOF-Cam基准上，CamFlow+在稀疏和密集相机运动估计上均有提升。在数字视频稳像任务中，CamFlow+改善了全局和局部稳定性，在盲测用户研究中获得了最高的top-1偏好率。

Conclusion: CamFlow+通过混合基框架有效放松了单平面约束，更准确地建模了相机运动，在相机运动估计和视频稳像两个任务上均取得了先进结果。

Original Abstract: Estimating 2D camera motion is fundamental to computer vision and computational photography. Existing homography-based methods work well for planar scenes or pure rotation, but struggle with camera translation, depth variation, and local parallax; local homography and mesh-based models improve flexibility but still rely on piecewise planar assumptions. We introduce CamFlow+, a hybrid-basis framework that represents 2D camera motion directly in dense-flow space. CamFlow+ combines homography-derived physical bases, stochastic bases sampled from homography flows, and depth-translational bases derived from depth and camera intrinsics, relaxing the single-plane constraint while preserving camera-motion regularity. A depth-aware smoothness term further regularizes translation-induced parallax in continuous-depth regions while preserving motion changes near depth boundaries. We evaluate CamFlow+ on GHOF-Cam, a camera-motion benchmark that masks out dynamic objects and ill-posed occlusion regions in an optical-flow benchmark to isolate camera-induced motion. Experiments show that CamFlow+ improves sparse and dense camera-motion estimation. In digital video stabilization, CamFlow+ also improves global and local stability, achieving the best top-1 preference rate in a blind user study. Code and datasets will be available on the project page: https://lhaippp.github.io/CamFlow+.

中文摘要翻译: 估计二维相机运动是计算机视觉和计算摄影的基础。现有的基于单应性的方法在平面场景或纯旋转下表现良好，但在处理相机平移、深度变化和局部视差时存在困难；局部单应性和网格模型提升了灵活性，但仍依赖于分片平面假设。我们提出CamFlow+，一种混合基框架，直接在密集光流空间中表示二维相机运动。CamFlow+结合了从单应性导出的物理基、从单应性流中采样的随机基、以及从深度和相机内参导出的深度平移基，在保持相机运动规律的同时放松了单平面约束。一个深度感知平滑项进一步在连续深度区域正则化平移引起的视差，同时在深度边界附近保留运动变化。我们在GHOF-Cam上评估CamFlow+，这是一个相机运动基准，通过掩蔽光流基准中的动态物体和不适定遮挡区域来分离相机诱导的运动。实验表明，CamFlow+提升了稀疏和密集相机运动估计。在数字视频稳像中，CamFlow+也改善了全局和局部稳定性，在盲测用户研究中取得了最高的top-1偏好率。代码和数据集将在项目页面提供：https://lhaippp.github.io/CamFlow+。

</details>


### [58] [Unveiling the Unknown: Open Vocabulary Object Detection with Scene Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05916)
*Yi Chen,Yinghao Lu,Zhehao Li,Chenchen Yan,Jiafei Wu,Chong Wang,Jiangbo Qian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种场景引导的关系建模检测框架（Scene-guided Relational Modeling），通过场景图显式建模候选区域与上下文对象之间的结构语义和空间关系，并引入关系注意力模块隐式增强关键关系线索，结合基于场景的文本对齐分支，显著提升了开放词汇目标检测中 novel 类别的检测性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于知识蒸馏的开放词汇目标检测方法忽略了目标之间结构化的、图像特定的关系（如交互和空间布局），这严重限制了新类别的检测效果。

Method: 提出场景引导关系建模检测框架：1）利用场景图捕获候选区域与上下文对象间的结构化语义和空间关系；2）显式建模相邻区域间的交互；3）引入关系注意力模块隐式增强从场景图中提取的关键关系线索；4）提出基于场景的文本对齐分支，从描述文本中蒸馏类别知识以引导关系对齐。

Result: 在 COCO 和 LVIS 数据集上，该方法在 novel 类别的 AP 指标上优于其他开放词汇目标检测方法。

Conclusion: 通过显式与隐式相结合的关系建模以及场景文本对齐，能够有效融合视觉关系与语义信息，显著提升开放词汇目标检测中未知类别的识别性能。

Original Abstract: Open-vocabulary object detection seeks to identify novel object categories that were not part of the training data. Many knowledge distillation-based approaches have shown promising performance by transferring knowledge from pre-trained vision-language models to object detection. However, these methods often overlook structured, image-specific relationships between objects, such as interactions and spatial arrangements. This oversight can significantly restrict the effectiveness of detecting novel categories. To address this issue, we propose a Scene-guided Relational Modeling detection framework. This framework utilizes scene graphs to capture structured semantic and spatial relationships between candidate regions and their contextual objects. It explicitly models interactions among neighboring regions and incorporates a Relation Attention Module to implicitly amplify the key relational cues extracted from the scene graph. Furthermore, we present a scene-based textual alignment branch that distills category knowledge from captions to guide relational alignment. This approach facilitates a seamless integration of visual relations with semantic information for enhanced detection performance. Comprehensive experiments show that our model achieves superior performance compared to other OVOD methods, improving the AP for novel categories on COCO and LVIS datasets.

中文摘要翻译: 开放词汇目标检测旨在识别训练数据中未包含的新对象类别。许多基于知识蒸馏的方法通过将预训练的视觉-语言模型的知识迁移到目标检测中，已展现出良好的性能。然而，这些方法往往忽略了对象之间结构化的、图像特定的关系，例如交互和空间布局。这一疏忽会显著限制新类别的检测效果。为解决该问题，我们提出一种场景引导的关系建模检测框架。该框架利用场景图来捕获候选区域与其上下文对象之间结构化的语义和空间关系。它显式地建模相邻区域之间的交互，并引入关系注意力模块来隐式地增强从场景图中提取的关键关系线索。此外，我们提出一个基于场景的文本对齐分支，从描述文本中蒸馏类别知识以引导关系对齐。该方法促进了视觉关系与语义信息的无缝融合，从而提升了检测性能。大量实验表明，与其它开放词汇目标检测方法相比，我们的模型在 COCO 和 LVIS 数据集上针对新类别的平均精度（AP）取得了更优的性能。

</details>


### [59] [MemoryCard: Topic-Aware Multi-Modal Clue Compression for Long-Video Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05917)
*Qing Yang,Pengcheng Huang,Xinze Li,Zhenghao Liu,Yukun Yan,Yu Gu,Ge Yu,Gang Li,Maosong Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MemoryCard是一种基于视频记忆增强的框架，通过将长视频组织为自包含的“记忆卡”（Memory Cards），以事件级语义单元替代传统逐帧处理方式，显著提升长视频问答性能，在可比视觉token预算下准确率最高提升21.8%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于帧的方法将孤立、零散的帧作为基本证据单元，导致VLMs难以有效捕获连贯的事件级语义，无法应对长视频中答案相关证据稀疏、短暂且时间上分散的挑战。

Method: 首先对视频和对齐文本执行自读过程，将视频切分为语义连贯的单元（每个对应一个主题或事件）；然后为每个单元生成事件级视频摘要并选取代表性视觉时刻；最后将这些信息渲染为统一的记忆卡，用于检索和问答。

Result: 在可比视觉token预算下，MemoryCard一致提升了长视频问答性能，准确率相对提升最高达21.8%。

Conclusion: MemoryCard通过构建事件级语义单元（记忆卡）替代分散帧作为证据单元，有效增强了VLMs在长视频问答中的表现。

Original Abstract: Long-video question answering remains challenging for Vision-Language Models (VLMs), as answer-relevant evidence is often sparse, transient, and temporally dispersed across lengthy video contexts. Existing frame-centric approaches improve efficiency through uniform sampling, query-aware frame selection, visual-token compression, and adaptive resolution strategies. However, they still rely on isolated and fragmented frames as the fundamental evidence units, limiting VLMs' ability to effectively capture coherent event-level semantics. To address this limitation, we propose MemoryCard, a video-memory-based augmentation framework that organizes long videos into self-contained Memory Cards. Specifically, MemoryCard first performs a self-reading process over videos and aligned utterances to segment the video into semantically coherent units, each corresponding to a distinct topic or event. For each unit, it generates an event-level video gist and selects representative visual moments, which are then rendered into unified Memory Cards for retrieval and question answering. Experimental results demonstrate that MemoryCard consistently improves long-video QA performance under comparable visual-token budgets, achieving up to a 21.8% relative improvement in accuracy. All code is available at https://github.com/NEUIR/MemoryCard.

中文摘要翻译: 长视频问答对视觉-语言模型（VLMs）仍然具有挑战性，因为答案相关的证据通常稀疏、短暂且分散在冗长的视频上下文中。现有的基于帧的方法通过均匀采样、查询感知的帧选择、视觉token压缩和自适应分辨率策略来提高效率。然而，它们仍然依赖孤立且零散的帧作为基本证据单元，限制了VLMs有效捕获连贯事件级语义的能力。为了解决这一局限，我们提出了MemoryCard，一种基于视频记忆增强的框架，将长视频组织为自包含的记忆卡。具体而言，MemoryCard首先对视频和对齐文本执行自读过程，将视频切分为语义连贯的单元，每个单元对应一个不同的主题或事件。对于每个单元，它生成事件级视频摘要并选取代表性视觉时刻，然后将这些信息渲染为统一的记忆卡，用于检索和问答。实验结果表明，在可比视觉token预算下，MemoryCard一致提升了长视频问答性能，准确率相对提升最高达21.8%。所有代码已开源在https://github.com/NEUIR/MemoryCard。

</details>


### [60] [Faithful, Enriched, and Precise: Benchmarking Natural-Science Illustration Generation by T2I models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05949)
*Yifan Chang,Jiaxin Ai,Jianwen Sun,Yuandong Pu,Siqi Luo,Liangliang Zhao,Yuchen Ren,Minghao Liu,Yunfei Yu,Yu Qiao,Kaipeng Zhang,Yihao Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FEPBench，一个面向科学插图的细粒度文本到图像生成评估基准，从指令遵循、推理丰富性和语义精度三个维度系统评估T2I模型，揭示当前SOTA模型在文本渲染、推理增强及生成丰富度与精度平衡方面仍存在瓶颈。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有科学插图生成评估基准多停留在整体层面，忽略细粒度元素，且缺乏对科学推理能力和输出简洁性的量化评估。

Method: 构建涵盖多学科和多布局类型的高质量科学插图基准FEPBench，结合多模态大语言模型和人类专家提供细粒度原子集标注，从指令遵循、推理丰富性、语义精度三个维度评估T2I模型，并进一步分解视觉、文本、关系和布局元素分析模型表现。

Result: 即使是GPT Image 2和Nano Banana Pro等SOTA闭源模型，仍存在文本渲染瓶颈、推理丰富性有限以及生成丰富度与精度难以平衡的问题。

Conclusion: FEPBench为科学插图生成中T2I模型的改进和部署提供了实用指导，揭示当前模型在细粒度科学可视化任务上的不足。

Original Abstract: Scientific illustrations are essential tools for communicating research findings, especially in natural science, where they visualize complex concepts and processes. As Text-to-Image (T2I) models become increasingly capable, researchers have started to use them for scientific illustration generation. However, existing benchmarks often assess outputs at a holistic level, overlooking fine-grained elements, while scientific reasoning ability and output conciseness remain under-quantified. We introduce FEPBench, a benchmark built from carefully selected high-quality scientific illustrations across multiple disciplines and layout types. With the assistance of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) and human experts, we provide fine-grained atom set annotations and systematically evaluate T2I models along three dimensions: instruction faithfulness, reasoning enrichment, and semantic precision. Our evaluation further decomposes model performance across visual, textual, relation, and layout elements. Results show that even state-of-the-art (SOTA) closed-source models, such as GPT Image 2 and Nano Banana Pro, still suffer from text-rendering bottlenecks, limited reasoning enrichment, and difficulty balancing generation richness with precision. These findings provide practical guidance for improving and deploying T2I models in scientific illustration generation. Benchmark data, atom set annotations, and evaluation code will be released by us.

中文摘要翻译: 科学插图是传达研究发现的重要工具，尤其是在自然科学领域，它们能够可视化复杂的概念和过程。随着文本到图像（T2I）模型能力的不断增强，研究人员已开始将其用于科学插图生成。然而，现有基准通常仅在整体层面评估输出，忽略了细粒度元素，同时科学推理能力和输出简洁性仍缺乏量化评估。我们提出了FEPBench，一个基于精心挑选的多学科、多布局类型高质量科学插图构建的基准。借助多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）和人类专家的辅助，我们提供了细粒度原子集标注，并从指令遵循、推理丰富性和语义精度三个维度系统评估T2I模型。我们的评估进一步将模型表现分解为视觉、文本、关系和布局元素。结果表明，即使是GPT Image 2和Nano Banana Pro等最先进的闭源模型，仍然存在文本渲染瓶颈、推理丰富性有限以及生成丰富度与精度难以平衡的问题。这些发现为在科学插图生成中改进和部署T2I模型提供了实用指导。基准数据、原子集标注和评估代码将由我们公开发布。

</details>


### [61] [T-FunS3D: Task-Driven Hierarchical Open-Vocabulary 3D Functionality Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05975)
*Jingkun Feng,Reza Sabzevari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: T-FunS3D是一种任务驱动的分层开放词汇3D功能分割方法，通过构建场景图并利用视觉-语言模型，在保持与最先进方法相当性能的同时，显著提升运行速度并降低内存消耗。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有开放词汇3D分割方法要么聚焦于物体级识别，要么对整个场景进行穷尽式分割，导致资源消耗高、耗时长，难以在细粒度、准确性和速度之间取得平衡。

Method: 以室内场景的3D点云和带有位姿的RGB-D图像为输入，通过提取实例及其视觉嵌入构建开放词汇场景图；给定任务描述后，识别场景图中最相关的实例，并利用视觉-语言模型定位其功能组件。

Result: 在SceneFun3D数据集上的实验表明，T-FunS3D在开放词汇3D功能分割任务上达到与最先进方法相当的性能，同时实现了更快的运行时间和更少的内存使用。
