We establish novel pseudo-dimension bounds, non-asymptotic approximation guarantees, and Hölder-norm bounds for the approximators of these models. Leveraging these results, we derive non-asymptotic uniform convergence rates for smooth DNN estimators across multiple statistical contexts, including Huber, least-squares, quantile, and logistic regression. We prove that smooth DNNs can mitigate the {curse of dimensionality} in uniform convergence by adaptively exploiting the low-dimensional hierarchical composition structure of the target function. Supported by both simulation studies and a real-world application, our results position smooth DNNs as a theoretically grounded and practically viable alternative to ReLU networks for statistical learning tasks requiring uniform guarantees.

中文摘要翻译: 本文建立了光滑激活深度神经网络估计器一致收敛的理论框架。虽然标准ReLU网络在L^2(P)范数下对各类非参数回归任务能达到极小化最优收敛率，但我们建立了一个理论下界，证明最小二乘ReLU估计器在其一致收敛行为中可能遭受维数诅咒。受下游任务中对最坏情况可靠性的需求驱动，我们通过分析光滑激活DNN（包括前馈和残差结构）来解决这一局限性。我们为这些模型的逼近器建立了新的伪维数界、非渐近逼近保证和Hölder范数界。利用这些结果，我们推导了光滑DNN估计器在多种统计场景（包括Huber回归、最小二乘回归、分位数回归和逻辑回归）中的非渐近一致收敛率。我们证明光滑DNN能够通过自适应利用目标函数的低维层次组合结构来缓解一致收敛中的维数诅咒。仿真研究和真实数据应用均支持了我们的结果，将光滑DNN定位为在需要一致保证的统计学习任务中，ReLU网络在理论上可靠且实践上可行的替代方案。

</details>


### [150] [From Prediction to Self: Developmental Conditions for Agency in Minimal Neural Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05605)
*Evan Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过40个受控实验组成的发育序列，发现一个仅预测世界的系统（192维GRU）要区分自身因果影响与外界变化，必须按严格顺序满足四个条件：持久状态形成稳定吸引子、因果行动循环连接输出到输入、本体感受反馈使隐性因果知识显性化、异步觉醒（感知学习先于行动学习巩固）。提出代理增益（A=Err_world-Err_self）作为追踪该过程的指标，实验验证了该指标的有效性以及12个被证伪的假设。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 探索一个仅能预测世界的系统如何发展出区分自身因果影响与外部世界变化的能力，即自我意识或自我表征的起源问题。

Method: 在192维GRU上设计40个受控实验构成发育序列，逐步添加组件并追踪系统能否区分自致变化与世界所致变化。引入代理增益（A=Err_world-Err_self）作为量化指标，在周期（正弦）和混沌（Lorenz）环境中比较自我感知与自我盲预测器的表现。

Result: 发现自我区分能力必须按严格顺序满足四个条件；代理增益指标在两种环境中一致有效，且能经受辅助组件的消融实验；仅有前向采样动作选择产生有意义的代理增益，两种基于梯度的方法失效；识别出12个被证伪的假设，表明纯预测编码不足以产生自我表征。

Conclusion: 系统的自我意识能力并非单一机制的结果，而是多个条件按严格发育顺序共同作用的结果；代理增益是一个有效的量化追踪指标；前向采样是产生自我区分能力的关键动作选择机制。

Original Abstract: How does a system that merely predicts the world come to distinguish its own causal influence from everything else? We trace this transition in a minimal 192-dimensional GRU through 40 controlled experiments arranged as a developmental sequence, adding components one at a time and tracking whether the system can distinguish self-caused from world-caused changes.
  The developmental path reveals four conditions that must be satisfied in strict order: (1) persistent state forming stable attractors, (2) a causal action loop linking output to input, (3) proprioceptive feedback that makes implicit causal knowledge explicit, and (4) asynchronous awakening - perceptual learning must consolidate before action learning begins. We propose agency gain (A = Err_world - Err_self), the predictive advantage of knowing one's own action, as a metric to track this process. The self-aware predictor consistently outperforms the self-blind predictor across periodic (sinusoidal) and chaotic (Lorenz) environments, and the metric survives ablation of all auxiliary components. Only forward-sampled action selection produces meaningful agency gain; two gradient-based alternatives degenerate.
  Equally significant are 12 falsified hypotheses mapping where development stalls: predictive coding alone does not produce self-represent

中文摘要翻译: 一个仅能预测世界的系统如何学会区分自身因果影响与外界变化？我们通过40个受控实验构成的发育序列，在一个最小化的192维门控循环单元中追踪这一转变过程。实验逐步添加组件，并监控系统能否区分自致变化与世界所致变化。该发育路径揭示了四个必须按严格顺序满足的条件：（1）持久状态形成稳定吸引子；（2）因果行动循环连接输出与输入；（3）本体感受反馈使隐性因果知识显性化；（4）异步觉醒——感知学习必须先于行动学习巩固。我们提出代理增益（A=Err_world-Err_self），即知晓自身动作所带来的预测优势，作为追踪该过程的指标。自我感知预测器在周期（正弦）和混沌（Lorenz）环境中均一致优于自我盲预测器，该指标能经受所有辅助组件的消融实验。仅前向采样动作选择产生有意义的代理增益，两种基于梯度的方法均告失效。同样重要的是，我们识别出12个被证伪的假设，揭示了发育停滞的位置：纯预测编码本身不足以产生自我表征。

</details>


### [151] [Cross-Epoch Adaptive Rollout Optimization for RL Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05606)
*Yiming Zong,Yige Wang,Jiashuo Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CERO方法，通过贝叶斯后验估计和Fenchel对偶优化，实现自适应rollout预算分配，在固定全局预算下提升LLM后训练的样本效率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有LLM后训练的强化学习方法对每个prompt使用固定的rollout预算，忽略了不同prompt提供的训练信号存在巨大差异，导致样本效率低下。

Method: CERO方法：（1）为每个prompt的成功概率维护Beta后验分布，利用后验期望Bernoulli方差估计额外rollout的价值；（2）构建凹饱和效用函数，使跨prompt和跨epoch的决策通过全局预算耦合；（3）由于目标函数时间不可分，推导Fenchel对偶重写，通过投影在线梯度下降更新prompt级和预算级对偶变量。

Result: 在数学推理问题上的实验表明，CERO在多个开源LLM和基准测试中一致优于GRPO；理论上，在固定prompt效用下证明了相对于离线分配基准的O(√K)遗憾界。

Conclusion: 自适应rollout预算分配能够有效提升样本效率，CERO方法在理论和实践上都验证了这一思路的有效性。

Original Abstract: LLM post-training often relies on reinforcement learning methods that sample multiple rollouts per prompt, yet most existing approaches use a fixed rollout budget for every prompt, despite large differences in the training signal different prompts provide. In this paper, we study adaptive rollout allocation under a fixed global budget and formulate the problem as online resource allocation with prompt-level diminishing returns. Our method, CERO, maintains a Beta posterior over each prompt's success probability and uses the posterior expected Bernoulli variance as a Bayesian estimate of the value of additional rollouts. We use this estimate to construct a concave, saturating utility over cumulative allocations, yielding an objective in which decisions across prompts and epochs are coupled by the global budget. Since the resulting objective is temporally nonseparable, we derive a Fenchel-dual reformulation and update both prompt-level and budget-level dual variables via projected online gradient descent. Under fixed prompt utilities, we prove an $O(\sqrt{K})$ regret bound against the offline allocation benchmark. Experiments on mathematical-reasoning problems show that CERO consistently outperforms GRPO across multiple open-weight LLMs and benchmarks, demonstrating that adaptive rollout budgeting can improve sample efficiency.

中文摘要翻译: LLM后训练通常依赖于对每个提示采样多个rollout的强化学习方法，然而尽管不同提示提供的训练信号差异巨大，大多数现有方法仍为每个提示使用固定的rollout预算。在本文中，我们研究在固定全局预算下的自适应rollout分配，并将该问题形式化为具有提示级递减回报的在线资源分配问题。我们的方法CERO为每个提示的成功概率维护一个Beta后验，并使用后验期望Bernoulli方差作为额外rollout价值的贝叶斯估计。我们利用该估计构建一个关于累积分配量的凹饱和效用函数，从而得到一个目标函数，其中跨提示和跨epoch的决策通过全局预算耦合。由于所得目标函数在时间上不可分离，我们推导了Fenchel对偶重写，并通过投影在线梯度下降同时更新提示级和预算级对偶变量。在固定提示效用下，我们证明相对于离线分配基准的O(√K)遗憾界。在数学推理问题上的实验表明，CERO在多个开源LLM和基准测试中一致优于GRPO，证明自适应rollout预算分配能够提升样本效率。

</details>


### [152] [StableRCA: Robust Graph-Agnostic Mechanism-Level Root Cause Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05636)
*Xiaoyu Lin,Nicholas Tagliapietra,Kehan Li,Lavdim Halilaj,Juergen Luettin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: StableRCA提出了一种基于局部马尔可夫边界和独立因果机制原理的根因分析方法，避免全局因果图发现，在多种场景下显著优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有根因分析方法存在关键瓶颈：基于图的因果方法需要已知或精确估计的因果图，而无图统计方法只能定位边缘异常而非结构原因，或依赖严格的图结构和函数形式假设。

Method: 提出StableRCA框架，通过估计局部马尔可夫边界并检测其中的条件分布偏移来实现机制级根因分析，利用独立因果机制原理证明干预目标可以以指数收敛概率被识别。

Result: 在合成基准和五个真实数据集上，StableRCA对图错误指定具有鲁棒性，在多个干预目标下有效，可扩展至大规模系统，并在多种应用领域表现可靠。

Conclusion: StableRCA通过避免全局因果图发现，提供了一种鲁棒、高效且可扩展的根因分析方法，在理论和实验上均优于现有方法。

Original Abstract: Root-Cause Analysis (RCA) seeks to identify the variables responsible for abnormal system behavior in complex domains such as manufacturing, cloud computing, and healthcare. Existing approaches face a critical bottleneck: graph-based causal methods can identify intervention targets but typically require a known or accurately estimated causal graph, while graph-free statistical methods either localize marginal anomalies rather than structural causes, or rely on restrictive assumptions about graph structure or functional form. We propose StableRCA, a local mechanism-level RCA framework that avoids global graph discovery by estimating local Markov boundaries and detecting conditional distribution shifts within them. Leveraging the Independent Causal Mechanism principle, we show that intervention targets can be identified with probability converging exponentially in sample size under faithful Markov boundary recovery and non-degenerate mechanism shifts. Experiments on synthetic benchmarks and five real-world datasets demonstrate that StableRCA is robust to graph misspecification, effective under multiple intervention targets, scalable to large systems, and reliable across diverse application domains. Code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/StableRCA-E362

中文摘要翻译: 根因分析旨在识别复杂领域（如制造业、云计算和医疗保健）中导致系统行为异常的变量。现有方法面临关键瓶颈：基于图的因果方法可以识别干预目标，但通常需要已知或准确估计的因果图；而无图统计方法要么定位的是边缘异常而非结构原因，要么依赖于关于图结构或函数形式的严格假设。我们提出StableRCA，一种局部机制级根因分析框架，它通过估计局部马尔可夫边界并检测其中的条件分布偏移来避免全局图发现。利用独立因果机制原理，我们证明在忠实马尔可夫边界恢复和非退化机制偏移下，干预目标可以以样本量指数收敛的概率被识别。在合成基准和五个真实数据集上的实验表明，StableRCA对图错误指定具有鲁棒性，在多个干预目标下有效，可扩展至大规模系统，并在多种应用领域表现可靠。代码可在以下网址获取：https://anonymous.4open.science/r/StableRCA-E362

</details>


### [153] [Q-GNN: Query-Conditioned Graph Neural Networks with Type Awareness for Knowledge Graph Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05639)
*Dongxiao He,Ruqiong Zhang,Zhizhi Yu,Ling Ding,Di Jin,Guangquan Xu,Zhiyong Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Q-GNN，通过在推理过程中同时利用查询实体和查询关系的信息（结构上下文和语义类型）来改进基于GNN的知识图谱补全方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于GNN的KGC方法仅以查询关系作为引导信号，忽略了查询实体本身携带的推理信息（实体仅作为子图提取的结构锚点），导致推理不够充分。

Method: 从两个角度引入查询实体信息：(1) 结构上下文：通过专用上下文编码器编码实体周围的邻接结构和关系模式，用于调制消息传递；(2) 语义类型：利用大语言模型推断实体的语义类型，融入注意力计算和最终评分，提供类型级先验约束。

Result: 在标准基准数据集上的实验结果表明了Q-GNN的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过在推理中同时利用查询关系和查询实体的信息（结构上下文和语义类型），可以显著提升知识图谱补全的性能。

Original Abstract: Knowledge Graph Completion (KGC) aims at predicting missing triplets from incomplete knowledge graphs, which is crucial for downstream applications. Recently, Graph Neural Network (GNN)-based methods have achieved remarkable success by performing message passing over query-centered local subgraphs. However, in practice, a query is jointly defined by both the entity and the relation, with both carrying information indispensable for reasoning, yet these methods rely solely on the query relation as the guiding signal, while the information inherent in the query entity is not leveraged to guide inference - the entity serves merely as a structural anchor for subgraph extraction. To this end, we incorporate query entity information into the reasoning process from two perspectives: the first is structural context, i.e., the neighboring structure and relation patterns around the entity, which is encoded by a dedicated context encoder and used to modulate messages; the second is semantic type of the entity, inferred by a large language model, which is incorporated into attention computation and final scoring to provide type-level prior constraints. Together, these two sources of information enable the reasoning process to be guided by both the query relation and the query entity. Experimental results on standard benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed Q-GNN.

中文摘要翻译: 知识图谱补全（KGC）旨在从不完整的知识图谱中预测缺失的三元组，这对下游应用至关重要。近年来，基于图神经网络（GNN）的方法通过在以查询为中心的局部子图上执行消息传递取得了显著成功。然而，在实践中，查询是由实体和关系共同定义的，两者都携带推理不可或缺的信息，但这些方法仅依赖查询关系作为引导信号，而查询实体本身蕴含的信息未被用于指导推理——实体仅作为子图提取的结构锚点。为此，我们从两个角度将查询实体信息融入推理过程：第一是结构上下文，即实体周围的邻接结构和关系模式，通过专用上下文编码器编码并用于调制消息；第二是实体的语义类型，由大语言模型推断，融入注意力计算和最终评分，以提供类型级先验约束。这两个信息源使得推理过程能够同时由查询关系和查询实体共同引导。在标准基准上的实验结果证明了所提出的Q-GNN的有效性。

</details>


### [154] [Two-Way Is Better Than One: Bidirectional Alignment with Cycle Consistency for Exemplar-Free Class-Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05675)
*Hongye Xu,Bartosz Krawczyk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种双向投影器对齐方法BiCyc，通过循环一致性目标联合优化新旧映射，缓解原型漂移导致的灾难性遗忘问题，在无样本类增量学习中取得显著效果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有原型类增量学习中的单向投影存在系统性偏差：要么扭曲当前特征几何结构，要么仅局部对齐旧类别，导致跨任务循环不一致性累积，无法有效补偿原型漂移。

Method: 提出BiCyc双向投影器对齐方法，包含旧到新和新到旧两个映射，采用循环一致性目标联合优化，并引入停止梯度门控机制，使传输与表示共同演化。通过理论分析证明循环损失在白化空间中收缩奇异谱，改善类均值和协方差的传输，从而减小分类对数几率扰动。

Result: 在标准无样本类增量学习基准上，BiCyc显著减少了遗忘并提高了从头训练的准确率，在预训练细粒度场景中也具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 双向循环一致性投影能有效克服单向投影的系统性偏差，在无样本类增量学习中实现更好的知识保留与遗忘缓解。

Original Abstract: Continual learning (CL) seeks models that acquire new skills without erasing prior knowledge. In exemplar-free class-incremental learning (EFCIL), this challenge is amplified because past data cannot be stored, making representation drift for old classes particularly harmful. Prototype-based EFCIL is attractive for its efficiency, yet prototypes drift as the embedding space evolves; therefore, projection-based drift compensation has become a popular remedy. We show, however, that existing one-directional projections introduce systematic bias: they either retroactively distort the current feature geometry or align past classes only locally, leaving cycle inconsistencies that accumulate across tasks. We introduce BiCyc, a bidirectional projector alignment approach with a cycle-consistency objective. BiCyc jointly optimizes two maps, old-to-new and new-to-old, with stop-gradient gating so that transport and representation co-evolve. Analytically, we show that the cycle loss contracts the singular spectrum toward unity in whitened space, and that improved transport of class means and covariances yields smaller perturbations of classification log-odds, preserving old-class decisions and mitigating catastrophic forgetting. Empirically, across standard EFCIL benchmarks, BiCyc substantially reduces forgetting and improves accuracy in from-scratch settings, while remaining competitive in the pretrained fine-grained regime.

中文摘要翻译: 持续学习（CL）旨在构建能够获取新技能而不抹除先前知识的模型。在无样本类增量学习（EFCIL）中，由于无法存储过去数据，这一挑战被进一步放大，旧类别的表示漂移尤为有害。基于原型的EFCIL因其高效性而具有吸引力，但随着嵌入空间的演化，原型也会发生漂移；因此，基于投影的漂移补偿已成为一种流行的补救方法。然而，我们表明现有的单向投影引入了系统性偏差：它们要么追溯性地扭曲当前特征几何结构，要么仅局部地对齐旧类别，导致跨任务累积循环不一致性。我们提出BiCyc，一种采用循环一致性目标的双向投影器对齐方法。BiCyc联合优化两个映射——旧到新和新到旧，并引入停止梯度门控机制，使得传输与表示共同演化。理论分析表明，循环损失在白化空间中将奇异谱向单位值收缩，并且类均值和协方差的改善传输能减小分类对数几率的扰动，从而保留旧类别决策并缓解灾难性遗忘。在标准EFCIL基准上的实验表明，BiCyc显著减少了遗忘并提高了从头训练设置下的准确率，同时在预训练细粒度场景中保持竞争力。

</details>


### [155] [Causal Modeling of Selection in Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05689)
*Haoyue Dai,Zeyu Tang,Peter Spirtes,Kun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文区分了静态选择和进化选择两种数据选择机制，指出现有方法将两者混为一谈导致进化场景下的错误发现，并提出了专门针对进化选择的因果发现模型与识别方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有因果发现方法将数据选择统一建模为静态选择机制，忽略了进化选择（如免疫适应、抗生素耐药性、社会规范涌现）中反复轮次的选择过程，导致在进化场景下产生虚假发现。

Method: 提出了一种专门刻画进化选择机制的新因果模型，并开发了一个完备且可靠的程序，用于从单个或多个环境/世代的数据中识别该模型。

Result: 实验验证了该方法能够从数据中有效揭示进化背后的相关机制，纠正了传统方法在进化场景下的错误发现。

Conclusion: 静态选择与进化选择是两种本质不同的数据选择机制，需要分别建模；本文提出的进化选择模型和识别方法为进化场景下的因果发现提供了有效工具。

Original Abstract: Understanding potential selection in data is crucial for causal discovery; we argue that "selection" in common narratives takes two forms, which we term static and evolutionary selection, respectively. Static selection refers to a one-shot filtering process where observed data consist of a subset of the population of interest, as in survey volunteer bias. Evolutionary selection, in contrast, operates through repeated rounds of differential fitness in reproduction, where observed data constitute the latest generation shaped by a historical trajectory, as in immune adaptation, antibiotic resistance, and social norm emergence. Existing methods largely conflate these two forms and rely on an identical graphical model of selection. We show that this model is valid for static settings but fails to characterize data under evolution, yielding false discovery results. To address this, we introduce a new model that specifically characterizes evolutionary selection, and develop a sound and complete procedure for identifying such models from data across one or multiple environments or generations. Experimental results validate the method's ability to uncover the relevant mechanisms underlying evolution from data.

中文摘要翻译: 理解数据中的潜在选择对于因果发现至关重要。我们认为，通常叙述中的"选择"存在两种形式，分别称为静态选择和进化选择。静态选择指一次性的过滤过程，观测数据由目标人群的子集构成，如调查志愿者偏差。相比之下，进化选择通过生殖过程中差异适应性的反复轮次运作，观测数据构成了由历史轨迹塑造的最新世代，如免疫适应、抗生素耐药性和社会规范涌现。现有方法大多混淆了这两种形式，并依赖相同的图模型来描述选择。我们证明该模型在静态设置下有效，但无法刻画进化下的数据特征，导致错误发现。为解决此问题，我们引入了一个专门刻画进化选择的新模型，并开发了一个完备且可靠的程序，用于从单个或多个环境/世代的数据中识别该模型。实验结果验证了该方法从数据中揭示进化背后相关机制的能力。

</details>


### [156] [Benchmarking Counterfactual Prediction in Epidemic Time Series with Time-Varying Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05692)
*Wenhao Mu,Facundo Yan,Anik Mumssen,Marisa Eisenberg,Alexander Rodríguez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个大规模流行病时间序列反事实预测基准，支持静态/时变治疗和单/多策略干预，并基于校准的智能体模型在150多个美国县生成真实反事实轨迹，评估了多种因果推断方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有时间序列因果推断数据集要么缺乏真实反事实标签，要么基于过于简化的模拟，无法反映复杂因果动态，因此亟需一个具备可观测反事实结果的真实感基准。

Method: 基于校准的智能体模型（Agent-Based Model），结合真实的人口、流动性、流行病学和政策数据，为150多个美国县生成反事实轨迹；基准支持静态与时变治疗、单策略与多策略干预设置。

Result: 评估多种主流和最新因果推断方法后发现各方法性能差异显著，凸显了真实时间序列因果推理的挑战性。

Conclusion: 该基准填补了缺乏真实感反事实时序数据集的空白，为时间序列因果推断提供了更贴近实际需求的评估平台。

Original Abstract: Deep learning has enabled significant advances in time-series causal inference, yet progress remains constrained by the lack of realistic benchmarks with observable counterfactual outcomes. Existing datasets either rely on real-world observations without ground-truth counterfactuals or on simplified simulations that fail to capture complex causal dynamics. To address this gap, we develop a large-scale benchmark for counterfactual prediction in epidemic time series under dynamic interventions. Unlike existing benchmarks, it supports static and time-varying treatments, as well as both single-policy and multi-policy intervention settings, enabling evaluation of causal inference methods across a broad range of causal inference scenarios. Leveraging a calibrated agent-based model grounded in real-world demographic, mobility, epidemiological, and policy data, we generate realistic counterfactual trajectories across more than 150 U.S. counties. Using this benchmark, we evaluate widely used and state-of-the-art causal inference methods, revealing substantial performance differences and highlighting the challenges of realistic time-series causal reasoning.

中文摘要翻译: 深度学习推动了时间序列因果推断的显著进展，但由于缺乏具有可观测反事实结果的真实感基准，相关研究仍然受到制约。现有数据集要么依赖于缺乏真实反事实标签的真实世界观测数据，要么基于无法捕捉复杂因果动态的简化模拟。为弥补这一空白，我们开发了一个大规模基准，用于动态干预下流行病时间序列的反事实预测。与现有基准不同，它支持静态和时变治疗设置，以及单策略和多策略干预场景，从而能够在广泛的因果推断场景中评估因果推断方法。利用基于真实人口、流动性、流行病学及政策数据校准的智能体模型，我们为150多个美国县生成了真实的反事实轨迹。基于该基准，我们评估了广泛使用和最新的因果推断方法，发现各方法性能差异显著，凸显了真实时间序列因果推理的挑战性。

</details>


### [157] [MolE-RAG: Molecular Structure-Enhanced Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05693)
*Joey Chan,Wonbin Kweon,Ashley Shin,Niharika Bhattacharjee,Pengcheng Jiang,Yue Guo,Jiawei Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种免训练的、以分子为中心的检索增强生成框架（MolE-RAG），通过整合文献、分子信息和结构相似分子三种上下文来源，提升大语言模型在分子性质预测任务上的表现，无需微调即可显著提升预测性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 大语言模型在分子性质预测任务中表现出一定潜力，但分子表示（如SMILES）与自然语言存在显著语义鸿沟，LLM难以直接推理化学结构信息，现有方法缺乏有效的化学知识注入机制。

Method: 提出MolE-RAG框架，是一种免训练的检索增强生成方法。在推理阶段，为每个分子预测任务补充三类上下文信息：（1）检索相关化学文献；（2）分子特定信息（同义词、标识符、官能团注释、物理化学描述符）；（3）从训练集中检索结构相似的分子。将这些上下文与原始SMILES一起输入LLM进行预测。

Result: 在9个分子性质预测任务上，使用专有、化学专用和开源LLM进行评估。对通用LLM，MolE-RAG在分类任务上ROC-AUC最高提升28个百分点，回归任务RMSE相较仅使用SMILES的基线降低67%。不同模型对不同上下文来源的受益程度存在差异。

Conclusion: 以分子为中心的检索增强生成可以在不进行模型微调的情况下有效提升LLM的分子性质预测能力，并提供了一个灵活的框架，用于在推理时整合异构化学知识。

Original Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown promise for molecular property prediction, but their ability to reason over chemical structures remains limited, as molecular representations such as SMILES differ substantially from the natural language on which LLMs are primarily trained. To bridge this semantic and chemical knowledge gap, we propose MolE-RAG, a training-free, molecule-centric retrieval-augmented generation framework for LLM-based molecular property prediction. MolE-RAG augments each prediction with three complementary sources of inference-time context: retrieved chemistry literature, molecule-specific information including compound synonyms, identifiers, functional group annotations, and physicochemical descriptors, and structurally similar molecules retrieved from the training set. We evaluate MolE-RAG across nine molecular property prediction tasks using proprietary, chemistry-specialized, and open-source LLMs. Across general-purpose LLMs, MolE-RAG improves ROC-AUC by up to 28 percentage points on classification tasks and reduces regression RMSE by up to 67% relative to a SMILES-only baseline. We further find that the utility of each context source varies across models and tasks, with different models benefiting most from textual retrieval, molecular context, or structural retrieval. These results suggest that molecule-centric retrieval can improve LLM-based molecular property prediction without model fine-tuning while providing a flexible framework for integrating heterogeneous chemical knowledge at inference time.

中文摘要翻译: 大语言模型（LLM）在分子性质预测方面展现出一定潜力，但其对化学结构的推理能力仍然有限，因为分子表示（如SMILES）与LLM主要训练所基于的自然语言存在显著差异。为弥合这一语义与化学知识鸿沟，我们提出了MolE-RAG——一种免训练的、以分子为中心的检索增强生成框架，用于基于LLM的分子性质预测。MolE-RAG为每次预测补充三类互补的推理时上下文信息：检索到的化学文献、分子特定信息（包括化合物同义词、标识符、官能团注释和物理化学描述符），以及从训练集中检索到的结构相似分子。我们使用专有模型、化学专用模型和开源LLM，在九个分子性质预测任务上评估了MolE-RAG。在通用LLM中，与仅使用SMILES的基线相比，MolE-RAG在分类任务上将ROC-AUC提升了最多28个百分点，在回归任务中将RMSE降低了最多67%。我们进一步发现，每种上下文来源的效用因模型和任务而异，不同模型分别从文本检索、分子上下文或结构检索中受益最多。这些结果表明，以分子为中心的检索增强生成可以在不进行模型微调的情况下提升基于LLM的分子性质预测能力，同时提供了一个灵活的框架，用于在推理时整合异构化学知识。

</details>


### [158] [Revisiting Prototype Rehearsal for Exemplar-Free Continual Learning: Manifold-Aware Boundary Sampling with Adaptive Class-Balanced Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05695)
*Hongye Xu,Bartosz Krawczyk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种流形感知的原型回放方法，通过边界感知过采样和自适应类别平衡损失，解决了无范例类增量学习中原型回放效果不如漂移补偿方法的问题，并在多个基准上达到最先进性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有无范例类增量学习中，原型回放策略效果始终不如漂移补偿方法。作者认为差距并非来自原型回放思想本身，而是其实现方式存在两个缺陷：1）将原型视为孤立类别摘要，忽略邻近敌对类别信息；2）未能纠正合成旧类样本与真实新类样本之间的类别不平衡问题。

Method: 1）约束扩展过采样（CEOS）：将旧类原型向新类中最近敌特征方向插值，生成边界感知的回放样本，更好地沿数据流形分布并保持类别间分离。2）自适应类别平衡损失（ACBL）：基于时间进行类别加权，在旧原型信息量最大时放大其梯度，随后续任务丰富监督信号逐渐衰减其影响。

Result: 所提出的流形感知原型回放方法缩小甚至反超了与近期漂移补偿方法的性能差距，在多个EFCIL基准测试中达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: 原型回放本身并非根本受限，通过合理设计（边界感知采样和自适应类别平衡）可以使其成为具有漂移鲁棒性和不平衡感知能力的有效机制，在无范例类增量学习任务中取得领先性能。

Original Abstract: Exemplar-free class-incremental learning (EFCIL) aims to acquire new classes over time without storing raw data. Historically, prototype rehearsal, which samples around stored class prototypes and mixes them with current-task data, has been a popular strategy to reduce catastrophic forgetting. However, recent drift-compensation methods that explicitly realign prototypes in the evolving feature space consistently outperform prototype-based rehearsal, raising the question of whether rehearsal itself is fundamentally limited. We argue that the performance gap stems not from the idea of prototype rehearsal per se, but from how it is typically instantiated: existing approaches treat prototypes as isolated class summaries that ignore information from nearby enemy classes, and fail to correct the emerging class imbalance between a handful of synthetic old-class samples and hundreds of real instances from newly introduced classes. Building on this hypothesis, we revisit prototype rehearsal and propose a manifold-aware variant that restores its competitiveness in EFCIL. First, we introduce Constrained Expansive Over-Sampling, which interpolates each old-class prototype toward its nearest enemy features from new classes, generating boundary-aware rehearsal samples that better follow the underlying data manifold while preserving inter-class separation. Second, we design an Adaptive Class-Balanced loss that performs time-based class weighting, amplifying gradients from older prototypes when they are most informative and gradually annealing their influence as richer supervision from later tasks accumulates. Together, these components turn prototype rehearsal into a drift-resilient, imbalance-aware mechanism that closes, and often reverses, the gap to recent drift-compensation methods, achieving state-of-the-art performance across multiple EFCIL benchmarks.

中文摘要翻译: 无范例类增量学习（EFCIL）旨在随时间推移学习新类别而无需存储原始数据。历史上，原型回放（即围绕存储的类别原型采样并与当前任务数据混合）一直是减少灾难性遗忘的常用策略。然而，近期那些在演化特征空间中显式重新对齐原型的漂移补偿方法持续优于基于原型的回放策略，这引发了一个问题：回放本身是否从根本上存在局限性。我们认为，性能差距并非源自原型回放这一思想本身，而是源自其典型的实现方式：现有方法将原型视为孤立的类别摘要，忽略了邻近敌对类别的信息，并且未能纠正少量合成旧类样本与数百个新引入类别真实样本之间日益严重的类别不平衡问题。基于这一假设，我们重新审视原型回放，并提出一种流形感知的变体，使其在EFCIL中恢复竞争力。首先，我们引入约束扩展过采样（Constrained Expansive Over-Sampling），该方法将每个旧类原型向其新类中最近敌特征方向插值，生成边界感知的回放样本，这些样本能更好地沿底层数据流形分布，同时保持类别间分离。其次，我们设计了一种自适应类别平衡损失（Adaptive Class-Balanced Loss），该方法执行基于时间的类别加权，在旧原型信息量最大时放大其梯度，并随着后续任务积累更丰富的监督信号而逐渐衰减其影响。这两个组件共同将原型回放转变为一种具有漂移鲁棒性和不平衡感知能力的机制，缩小甚至反超了与近期漂移补偿方法的性能差距，在多个EFCIL基准测试中实现了最先进的性能。

</details>


### [159] [Intercomparison of Machine Learning Algorithms for Remote Sensing-based In-season Crop Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05731)
*August Posch,Jitendra Kumar,Forrest M. Hoffman,Auroop R. Ganguly*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究结合HLS遥感时间序列与作物轮作历史，利用支持向量机在早期六月以30米分辨率高精度绘制玉米和杏仁种植图，并量化物候与分布不确定性，填补了收获前作物类型实时监测的空白。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 面对日益极端的气候威胁，作物收获前及时获取高精度作物类型分布图对粮食安全和应急响应至关重要。现有产品（如USDA Cropland Data Layer）在收获后次年二月才可用，缺乏收获前的高精度作物类型映射产品，且此前没有研究系统评估多种算法在年际变化下的相对优势。

Method: 利用HLS（Harmonized Landsat-Sentinel）地表反射率时间序列与作物轮作历史信息，在30米分辨率下，对爱荷华州玉米和加利福尼亚州杏仁进行早期六月映射。通过10种机器学习算法的数千种模型配置，采用逐年交叉验证和一套度量指标进行比较，并通过超参数搜索确定最优算法。

Result: 支持向量机（SVM）是整体上最成功的算法，在加利福尼亚州杏仁（爱荷华州玉米）的五个未见验证年中，早期六月的平均F1分数达到0.74（0.59）。年际变化是主要的不确定性来源，但集成方法或辅助数据有进一步改进潜力。

Conclusion: 本研究首次系统评估了多种算法在年际变化下的表现，证明利用SVM结合遥感和轮作信息可在收获前准确绘制主要作物类型。未来工作可扩展至多类别全美作物类型图及季内作物产量预测。

Original Abstract: In-season crop type mapping is critical for food security in the face of increasingly extreme climate-related threats to crops. Currently, the USDA Cropland Data Layer provides crop type labels at 30m resolution and is available the February after harvest, but no product exists that maps crop types before harvest with satisfactory accuracy that would allow emergency managers to respond to crop threats in near real time. Furthermore, the relative advantages of a wide range of algorithms have not been evaluated in a way that accounts for interannual variability, until this study. Here, Harmonized Landsat-Sentinel surface reflectance imagery time series and crop rotation history information are combined to map corn in Iowa and almonds in California at 30m resolution accurately by early June in unseen years, with robust quantification of uncertainty due to phenology and crop distribution. Thousands of model configurations across ten machine learning algorithms were compared using a year-wise cross-validation and a suite of metrics. Hyperparameter search revealed Support Vector Machines to be the most successful algorithm overall, with a mean F1 score of 0.74 (0.59) across five unseen validation years for almonds by early June in California (corn by early June in Iowa). Interannual variation was a large source of uncertainty, but patterns showed the potential to further improve performance with ensemble approaches or ancillary data. Future work may extend these methods to include multiclass maps of all crop types, CONUS-wide maps, and in-season crop yield forecasting.

中文摘要翻译: 面对日益极端的气候对作物造成的威胁，季内作物类型制图对粮食安全至关重要。目前，美国农业部作物数据层提供30米分辨率的作物类型标签，并在收获后次年二月可用，但尚无产品能在收获前以令人满意的精度绘制作物类型地图，从而使应急管理人员能够近乎实时地应对作物威胁。此外，直至本研究，尚未有以考虑年际变化的方式评估广泛算法相对优势的研究。本文结合HLS（Harmonized Landsat-Sentinel）地表反射率时间序列与作物轮作历史信息，在30米分辨率下，准确地在早期六月绘制爱荷华州玉米和加利福尼亚州杏仁的分布图，并对物候和作物分布引起的不确定性进行了稳健量化。通过逐年交叉验证和一套度量指标，比较了涵盖十种机器学习算法的数千种模型配置。超参数搜索显示，支持向量机是整体上最成功的算法，在加利福尼亚州杏仁（爱荷华州玉米）的五个未见验证年中，早期六月的平均F1分数达到0.74（0.59）。年际变化是很大的不确定性来源，但模式表明结合集成方法或辅助数据有进一步改进性能的潜力。未来工作可扩展这些方法以包含所有作物类型的多类别地图、全美范围的地图以及季内作物产量预测。

</details>


### [160] [Zero-Copy Semantic Contagion: An In-Memory Streaming Architecture for Evolving Attention Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05733)
*Kabir Murjani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种异构Rust-Python流式架构，通过连续时间图注意力机制建模跨公司新闻传播，实现低延迟（每篇新闻~13 ms）且精度显著优于随机和同行业基线，图拓扑结构是跨公司信号的唯一驱动力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有单资产预测模型忽略跨公司传播效应（如台湾代工厂中断对苹果股价的影响），无法在新闻事件发生初期捕获跨公司信号。

Method: 采用异构Rust-Python流式架构：输入侧用零拷贝Rust边缘解析新闻（~100 ns/条，扫描资产池~1.2 μs）；推理侧用多元神经霍克斯过程，包含逐节点连续时间LSTM状态和双线性隐式投影来传播有向激励，并引入自适应剪枝规则控制动态邻域更新的计算开销。

Result: 在FNSPID语料库（638篇文章，47个标的）上，系统在90分位数次日回报阈值下比随机提升1.70×精度，比同行业基线提升3.36×；去除图拓扑后精度降为零，证实动态注意力网络是跨公司信号的唯一来源。

Conclusion: 基于文本驱动的连续时间图注意力机制可以高效捕获跨公司价格传播信号，且图结构本身不可或缺。

Original Abstract: Per-ticker forecasting models dominate financial time-series work yet remain blind to cross-company propagation: a foundry disruption in Taiwan does not register in a single-asset model until Apple's own price has already moved. To address this limitation, we introduce a heterogeneous Rust-Python streaming architecture that maps cross-company attention as a continuous-time graph driven directly from text. We show that on the ingestion side, a zero-copy Rust edge parses news records in $\sim$100 ns and scans the target equity universe in $\sim$1.2 $μ$s. On the inference end, a multivariate Neural Hawkes Process featuring per-node continuous-time LSTM states and a bilinear latent projection propagates directed excitation, while an adaptive pruning rule bounds the computational cost of dynamic neighborhood updates. Combining these stages, we demonstrate an end-to-end processing latency of $\sim$13 ms per incoming news record on a single commodity CPU. Evaluated on a one-month temporal holdout of the FNSPID corpus (638 articles across 47 tickers), the system delivers a $1.70\times$ precision lift over random at the 90th-percentile next-day return threshold, and $3.36\times$ over a same-sector baseline. Crucially, removing the graph topology collapses precision to zero, confirming that the dynamic attention network is the sole driver of cross-company signal in this architecture.

中文摘要翻译: 单资产预测模型主导金融时间序列研究，但始终无法感知跨公司传播效应：台湾代工厂发生中断时，单资产模型要等到苹果股价自身已经变动后才能捕捉到该信号。为解决这一局限，我们提出一种异构Rust-Python流式架构，将跨公司注意力建模为直接由文本驱动的连续时间图。我们证明，在输入侧，零拷贝Rust边缘解析新闻记录约需100 ns，扫描目标股票池约需1.2 μs。在推理侧，一个多元神经霍克斯过程采用逐节点连续时间LSTM状态和双线性隐式投影来传播有向激励，同时一个自适应剪枝规则约束了动态邻域更新的计算成本。结合这些阶段，我们展示了在单颗商用CPU上每条入站新闻记录的端到端处理延迟约为13 ms。在FNSPID语料库（638篇文章，47个标的）的一个月时间留出集上评估，系统在90分位数次日回报阈值下比随机提升1.70倍的精度，比同行业基线提升3.36倍。至关重要的是，移除图拓扑结构后精度降为零，这证实了动态注意力网络是该架构中跨公司信号的唯一驱动力。

</details>


### [161] [Beyond Soft Masks: Hard-Perturbation Mixup Explainer for Robust GNN Explainability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05756)
*Jialiang Yin,Zheng Zhao,Linsey Pang,Bo Dong,Bin Shi,Jiaxing Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于硬扰动混合解释框架HPME，通过图池化提取离散解释性子图，并结合结构级替换的混合策略生成分布内解释，有效解决了GNN解释中的分布外问题，提升了解释保真度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有GNN事后解释方法使用软掩码无法完全消除标签无关信息，导致冗余结构泄漏到混合过程中，无法解决分布外（OOD）问题，从而降低了解释保真度。

Method: 提出HPME框架，基于广义图信息瓶颈理论，利用图池化提取离散解释性子图并获得信息容量界限以彻底压缩标签无关成分；同时引入结构级替换的混合策略，生成分布内解释来缓解分布偏移。

Result: 在多种任务上的大量实验表明，HPME在合成和真实数据集上均取得了最先进的性能，能够生成鲁棒且可解释的解释。

Conclusion: HPME通过硬扰动和结构级混合策略有效解决了软掩码方法的固有问题，显著提升了GNN解释的保真度和鲁棒性。

Original Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have demonstrated remarkable performance across a range of applications involving graph-structured data, particularly in high-stakes domains. However, the opaque nature of their decision-making processes limits their trustworthiness and broader adoption. Existing post-hoc explanation methods aim to improve explainability by identifying subgraphs that influence GNN predictions and adopt mixup strategies to alleviate the out-of-distribution (OOD) issue caused by using subgraphs for prediction. Yet, these approaches typically rely on soft masks, which are inherently unable to fully eliminate label-irrelevant information, allowing redundant structures to leak into the mixup process and hindering the resolution of the OOD problem, thereby degrading explanation fidelity. In this work, we propose HPME, a Hard-Perturbation Mixup Explanation framework grounded in a generalized Graph Information Bottleneck, which leverages graph pooling to extract discrete explanatory subgraphs and to yield an information-capacity bound to thoroughly compress label-irrelevant components. Furthermore, we introduce a novel mixup strategy built upon structure-level replacement, generating in-distribution explanations to effectively mitigate the distribution shift. Extensive experiments on diverse tasks demonstrate that HPME achieves state-of-the-art performance in generating robust and interpretable explanations across both synthetic and real-world datasets.

中文摘要翻译: 图神经网络（GNNs）在一系列涉及图结构数据的应用中展现出卓越性能，尤其是在高风险领域。然而，其决策过程的不透明性限制了其可信度和更广泛的采用。现有的事后解释方法通过识别影响GNN预测的子图来提高可解释性，并采用混合策略来缓解使用子图进行预测时引起的分布外（OOD）问题。然而，这些方法通常依赖于软掩码，其本质上无法完全消除标签无关信息，导致冗余结构泄漏到混合过程中，阻碍了OOD问题的解决，从而降低了解释保真度。本文提出HPME——一种基于广义图信息瓶颈的硬扰动混合解释框架，该框架利用图池化提取离散解释性子图，并产生信息容量界限以彻底压缩标签无关成分。此外，我们引入了一种基于结构级替换的新型混合策略，生成分布内解释以有效缓解分布偏移。在多种任务上的大量实验表明，HPME在合成和真实数据集上生成鲁棒且可解释的解释方面均达到了最先进的性能。

</details>


### [162] [Domain-Adapted Small Language Models with Hybrid Post-Processing: Achieving Cost-Efficient, Low-Latency Multi-Label Structured Prediction via LoRA Fine-Tuning on Scarce Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05781)
*Srinivasan Manoharan,Dilipkumar Nallusamy,Sachin Kumar,Haifeng Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合框架，将LoRA微调的小语言模型（LLaMA 3.1 8B，仅2.05%可训练参数）与确定性规则后处理层结合，仅用219条标注样本即可在对话转录的多标签合规评估任务中达到83.0%准确率和100%关键字段准确率，同时降低46-76%成本和2-5倍延迟。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 部署前沿大语言模型进行领域特定的结构化评估任务时，存在显著的延迟、成本和数据隐私开销问题，亟需一种更高效、低成本和保护隐私的替代方案。

Method: 采用混合神经-符号分解框架：1) 使用LoRA微调LLaMA 3.1 8B小语言模型（仅2.05%可训练参数），在219条精选样本上训练；2) 结合确定性规则后处理层确保输出结构合法性；3) 引入定向硬负样本增强策略以改善关键决策边界的分类性能。

Result: 在53条未见生产数据上：100% JSON结构有效性，83.0%人工验证总体准确率，关键分类字段100%准确率；单张A100 GPU推理约2秒，比前沿模型API快2-5倍；每次评估成本仅$0.013，比专有方案（$0.025-$0.055）节省46-76%成本。

Conclusion: 经领域适配的小语言模型结合确定性后处理，能够在结构化合规评估任务中达到与前沿大模型相当的准确率，同时显著降低运营成本、延迟和隐私风险。

Original Abstract: Deploying frontier large language models (LLMs) for domain-specific structured evaluation tasks often incurs substantial latency, cost, and data privacy overhead. We present a hybrid framework that combines a fine-tuned small language model (LLaMA 3.1 8B, with only 2.05% trainable parameters via LoRA) and a deterministic rule-based post-processing layer. Trained on just 219 curated examples, the system is applied to multi-label compliance evaluation of conversational transcripts spanning 18 heterogeneous output fields. In blind evaluation on 53 previously unseen production transcripts, it achieves 100% JSON structural validity, 83.0% human-validated overall accuracy, and 100% accuracy on the most critical classification field. The proposed approach formalizes a hybrid neural-symbolic decomposition and introduces targeted hard-negative augmentation to improve performance on critical decision boundaries. Running on a single NVIDIA A100 GPU, inference completes in approximately 2 seconds, which is 2-5x faster than frontier-model APIs. The system costs only $0.013 per evaluation compared with $0.025-$0.055 for proprietary alternatives, resulting in 46-76% cost savings. These results demonstrate that domain-adapted small language models, when combined with deterministic post-processing, can match frontier-model accuracy for structured compliance evaluation while substantially reducing operational cost, latency, and privacy risk.
  Keywords: small language models, parameter-efficient fine-tuning, LoRA, domain adaptation, hybrid inference, compliance evaluation, structured output.

中文摘要翻译: 部署前沿大语言模型用于领域特定的结构化评估任务时，往往会产生显著的延迟、成本和数据隐私开销。本文提出一种混合框架，将经过微调的小语言模型（LLaMA 3.1 8B，通过LoRA仅训练2.05%的可训练参数）与确定性规则后处理层相结合。该系统仅在219个精选样本上训练，用于对覆盖18个异构输出字段的对话转录进行多标签合规评估。在53条未见生产数据的盲测中，该系统实现了100%的JSON结构有效性、83.0%的人工验证总体准确率，以及最关键分类字段上100%的准确率。提出的方法形式化了一种混合神经-符号分解，并引入了定向硬负样本增强以改善关键决策边界的性能。在单张NVIDIA A100 GPU上运行时，推理完成时间约为2秒，比前沿模型API快2-5倍。每次评估成本仅为$0.013，而专有替代方案为$0.025-$0.055，节省了46-76%的成本。这些结果证明，经领域适配的小语言模型结合确定性后处理，能够在结构化合规评估任务中达到与前沿模型相当的准确率，同时大幅降低运营成本、延迟和隐私风险。

</details>


### [163] [Causal Longitudinal Prior-Fitted Networks for Counterfactual Outcome Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05797)
*Amirhossein Zare,Amirhessam Zare,Herlock Rahimi,Reza Salarikia,Mohammad Kashkooli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CausalLongPFN，一种基于先验拟合的上下文预测器，通过在广泛的结构因果模型先验上预训练，实现无需梯度更新或倾向性模型拟合的纵向因果预测，在多个基准上达到与领域内训练模型竞争的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有纵向因果估计方法通常需要为每个队列或模拟器训练新模型，成本高昂且难以推广；而实际应用中存在时变混杂、异质性患者动态、领域数据有限等挑战，亟需一种无需重复领域训练即可进行纵向因果预测的通用方法。

Method: CausalLongPFN基于Prior-Fitted Networks框架，在涵盖治疗-混杂反馈、潜在异质性、非线性状态演化、延迟效应和累积治疗反应的广泛时间结构因果模型先验上，通过完全合成的episode进行预训练。测试时模型冻结，以支持轨迹、查询历史和未来治疗序列为条件，直接输出未来结局的预测分布，无需梯度更新或倾向性模型拟合。多步预测通过在指定治疗序列下递归应用单步预测器实现。

Result: 在可分支癌症、HIV和华法林基准（具有真实反事实标签）以及MIMIC-III ICU轨迹的仅事实滚动原点预测上评估。CausalLongPFN在反事实基准上与领域训练的纵向基线方法具有竞争力，在事实MIMIC-III预测上表现强劲。

Conclusion: 广泛的合成因果先验预训练可以提供一种有用的冻结替代方案，适用于重复领域训练成本高昂或不可行的场景。

Original Abstract: Longitudinal treatment decisions require predicting potential outcomes under future treatment sequences in the presence of time-varying confounding, heterogeneous patient dynamics, and limited domain-specific data. Existing longitudinal causal estimators typically train a new model for each cohort or simulator. We introduce Causal Longitudinal Prior-Fitted Networks (CausalLongPFN), a prior-fitted in-context predictor for longitudinal causal prediction. The model is pretrained entirely on synthetic episodes sampled from a broad prior over temporal structural causal models, exposing it to treatment-confounder feedback, latent heterogeneity, nonlinear state evolution, delayed effects, and cumulative treatment responses. At test time, CausalLongPFN is frozen: it conditions on support trajectories, a query history, and a proposed future treatment sequence, and returns a predictive distribution over future outcomes without gradient updates or propensity-model fitting. Multi-step predictions are obtained by recursively applying the one-step predictor under the specified treatment sequence. We evaluate on branchable cancer, HIV, and warfarin benchmarks with ground-truth counterfactual labels, and on factual-only rolling-origin prediction in MIMIC-III ICU trajectories. CausalLongPFN is competitive with domain-trained longitudinal baselines on counterfactual benchmarks and performs strongly on factual MIMIC-III prediction, suggesting that broad synthetic causal pretraining can provide a useful frozen alternative when repeated domain-specific training is costly or impractical.

中文摘要翻译: 纵向治疗决策需要预测未来治疗序列下的潜在结果，同时面对时变混杂、异质性患者动态以及有限的领域特定数据。现有的纵向因果估计器通常需要为每个队列或模拟器训练新模型。本文提出因果纵向先验拟合网络（CausalLongPFN），一种用于纵向因果预测的先验拟合上下文预测器。该模型完全在从广泛的时间结构因果模型先验中采样的合成episode上进行预训练，使其暴露于治疗-混杂反馈、潜在异质性、非线性状态演化、延迟效应和累积治疗反应。在测试时，CausalLongPFN被冻结：它以支持轨迹、查询历史和提出的未来治疗序列为条件，返回未来结果的预测分布，无需梯度更新或倾向性模型拟合。多步预测通过在指定治疗序列下递归应用单步预测器获得。我们在具有真实反事实标签的可分支癌症、HIV和华法林基准上，以及在MIMIC-III ICU轨迹的仅事实滚动原点预测上进行评估。CausalLongPFN在反事实基准上与领域训练的纵向基线方法具有竞争力，在事实MIMIC-III预测上表现强劲，表明广泛的合成因果先验预训练可以在重复领域训练成本高昂或不可行时提供一种有用的冻结替代方案。

</details>


### [164] [CaliDist: Calibrating Large Language Models via Behavioral Robustness to Distraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05799)
*Mohammad Anas Jawad,Cornelia Caragea*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 CaliDist，一种通过衡量 LLM 对语义干扰的稳定性来进行后验校准的方法，在 7 个 NLU 基准测试上平均将 ECE 从 23% 降至 7%（相对提升 70%）。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有 LLM 校准方法忽略了行为鲁棒性这一关键维度——模型在面对无关或误导信息时应保持置信度的稳定性。

Method: CaliDist 是一种后验校准方法，通过向输入提示中添加语义干扰项来扰动模型，量化预测和不确定性的变化，并根据这种稳定性信号自适应地缩放模型初始置信度分数。

Result: 在 7 个 NLU 分类基准上使用 6 种不同 LLM 的实验表明，CaliDist 在 ECE 和 Brier Score 上均优于强基线，平均将 ECE 从 23% 降至 7%，相对提升 70%。

Conclusion: 行为稳定性是校准的有效信号，CaliDist 通过捕捉模型对干扰的敏感性显著提升了校准质量。

Original Abstract: Existing calibration methods for Large Language Models (LLMs) often overlook a critical dimension of trustworthiness: a model's {\em behavioral robustness} to irrelevant or misleading information. In this paper, we argue that a model's true confidence should reflect its stability under cognitive pressure. We introduce \textsc{CaliDist}, a novel post-hoc calibration approach that directly measures and penalizes a model's susceptibility to distraction. \textsc{CaliDist} quantifies how an LLM's predictions and uncertainty change when its input prompt is perturbed with semantic \textit{distractors}. This stability (or lack thereof) signal is then used to adaptively scale the model's initial confidence score. Our extensive experiments on seven Natural Language Understanding classification benchmarks using six distinct LLMs show that \textsc{CaliDist} consistently achieves lower Expected Calibration Error (ECE) and Brier Score compared with strong baselines. Remarkably, our method reduces the ECE from 23\% to 7\% on average--a relative improvement of 70\%--demonstrating that behavioral stability is a powerful signal for calibration. We make our code and datasets available at github.com/m-anas-j/CaliDist.

中文摘要翻译: 现有的大型语言模型（LLM）校准方法往往忽视了一个关键的可信度维度：模型在面对无关或误导信息时的行为鲁棒性。本文认为，模型真正的置信度应反映其在认知压力下的稳定性。我们提出 \textsc{CaliDist}，一种新颖的后验校准方法，直接测量并惩罚模型对干扰的易感性。\textsc{CaliDist} 量化了当输入提示被语义干扰项扰动时，LLM 预测和不确定性的变化程度，并利用这种稳定性（或缺乏稳定性）信号来自适应地缩放模型的初始置信度分数。我们在 7 个自然语言理解分类基准上使用 6 种不同的 LLM 进行了广泛实验，结果表明 \textsc{CaliDist} 在期望校准误差（ECE）和 Brier Score 上均一致地优于强基线。值得注意的是，我们的方法平均将 ECE 从 23\% 降至 7\%，相对提升了 70\%，这表明行为稳定性是校准的有力信号。我们已在 github.com/m-anas-j/CaliDist 公开代码和数据集。

</details>


### [165] [SALT: When More Rollouts Don't Help in Group-Based Policy Optimization and How to Make Them Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05800)
*Powei Chang,Jinpeng Zhang,Chaoqun Sun,MiniWell Tsao,Lianrui Li,Jianxiang Xiang,Chenyu Wang,Yukang Gao,Dongying Kong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SALT通过子空间自适应几何重加权机制，解决了GRPO风格组归一化下策略梯度特征低秩符号聚合导致的梯度抵消问题，提升了RLVR的学习效果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在RLVR中，GRPO风格的组归一化更新虽然采样多个rollout，但增加rollout数量并不能可靠地增强学习：组归一化下的策略梯度特征会集中到低秩符号几何结构中，导致聚合时大量抵消，削弱有效更新。

Method: SALT（子空间自适应几何插件组件）利用样本级梯度几何信息对组相对更新的系数进行重加权：从mini-batch Gram几何中估计主导共享子空间，将组相对系数分解为共享通道和残差通道，并在符号抵消严重时自适应放大残差通道。

Result: 在多种推理导向的RLVR基准和模型规模上，SALT有效改善了更新几何结构并提升了性能，且无需修改奖励模型或rollout采样过程。

Conclusion: SALT作为轻量插件组件，通过修复GRPO组归一化的梯度抵消缺陷，显著增强了RLVR训练效果，具有即插即用的实用性。

Original Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) often adopts GRPO-style group-relative updates, sampling multiple rollouts per prompt to construct normalized learning signals. However, merely increasing the number of rollouts does not reliably strengthen learning: under GRPO-style group normalization, per-rollout policy-gradient features can concentrate into a low-rank, signed geometry, causing substantial cancellation during aggregation and weakening the effective update. We address this failure mode with SALT, a Subspace-Adaptive geometry pLug-in componenT that uses sample-wise gradient geometry to reweight the coefficients of group-relative updates. SALT estimates a dominant shared subspace from the mini-batch Gram geometry, decomposes group-relative coefficients into shared and residual channels, and adaptively amplifies the residual channel when signed cancellation is severe. Across diverse reasoning-oriented RLVR benchmarks and model scales, SALT improves effective update geometry and performance without modifying the reward model or the rollout sampling procedure

中文摘要翻译: 基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）常采用GRPO风格的组相对更新，即对每个提示采样多个rollout以构建归一化学习信号。然而，仅增加rollout数量并不能可靠地增强学习：在GRPO风格的组归一化下，每个rollout的策略梯度特征会集中到一种低秩符号几何结构中，导致聚合时发生大量抵消，从而削弱有效更新。本文提出的SALT（子空间自适应几何插件组件）通过利用样本级梯度几何信息对组相对更新的系数进行重加权，以解决上述失效模式。SALT从mini-batch Gram几何中估计主导共享子空间，将组相对系数分解为共享通道和残差通道，并在符号抵消严重时自适应放大残差通道。在多种推理导向的RLVR基准和模型规模上，SALT在不修改奖励模型或rollout采样过程的情况下，有效改善了更新几何结构并提升了性能。

</details>


### [166] [Robust and sparse support vector machine via hybrid truncated loss for supervised classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05814)
*Yuliang Yang,Chen Chen,Yuxiang Liu,Huiru Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种混合截断损失函数L_ht，构建了单视图L_ht-SVM和多视图MvL_ht-SVM模型，兼顾稀疏性与有界性，在分类精度和噪声鲁棒性上优于多种现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有SVM的凸损失函数（如合页损失、最小二乘损失）对异常值敏感，而有界非凸损失函数的计算成本高，亟需一种既能抵抗异常值又计算高效的损失函数。

Method: 提出混合截断损失函数L_ht（兼具稀疏性和有界性），构建L_ht-SVM模型；引入P-稳定点建立一阶必要与充分最优性条件，基于此设计带工作集策略的交替方向乘子法（ADMM）实现全局收敛；进一步扩展为多视图MvL_ht-SVM，加入结构信息和视图权重，遵循共识与互补原则。

Result: 在合成、真实和图像数据集上，L_ht-SVM在更高精度、更少支持向量和更好噪声鲁棒性上优于五种单视图方法；MvL_ht-SVM在准确率、精确率、召回率和F1分数上优于六种多视图方法。

Conclusion: 提出的L_ht损失函数及其SVM模型能有效平衡鲁棒性与计算效率，单视图和多视图扩展均显著优于现有方法。

Original Abstract: The support vector machine (SVM) is a widely used classifier, but choosing an appropriate loss function remains difficult. Convex losses such as the hinge loss and least-squares loss are sensitive to outliers, while bounded non-convex losses often lead to high computational cost. To address this, we propose a hybrid truncated loss function ($L_{\mathrm{ht}}$) that is both sparse and bounded, and build the $L_{\mathrm{ht}}$-SVM model for single-view classification. We introduce the P-stationary point and use it to establish the first-order necessary and sufficient optimality conditions. Based on these conditions, we design an alternating direction method of multipliers with a working-set strategy that reduces computational cost and achieves global convergence. We further extend $L_{\mathrm{ht}}$-SVM to multi-view learning by adding structural information and view weights, resulting in Mv$L_{\mathrm{ht}}$-SVM, which follows both the consensus and complementarity principles. Experiments on synthetic, real-world, and image datasets show that $L_{\mathrm{ht}}$-SVM achieves higher accuracy with fewer support vectors and better noise robustness than five single-view methods, while Mv$L_{\mathrm{ht}}$-SVM outperforms six multi-view methods in accuracy, precision, recall, and F1-score.

中文摘要翻译: 支持向量机（SVM）是一种广泛使用的分类器，但选择合适的损失函数仍然困难。合页损失和最小二乘损失等凸损失对异常值敏感，而有界非凸损失通常导致计算成本高昂。为解决这一问题，我们提出了一种兼具稀疏性和有界性的混合截断损失函数（$L_{\mathrm{ht}}$），并构建了用于单视图分类的$L_{\mathrm{ht}}$-SVM模型。我们引入P-稳定点，并利用它建立一阶必要与充分最优性条件。基于这些条件，我们设计了一种带有工作集策略的交替方向乘子法，该策略降低了计算成本并实现了全局收敛。我们进一步将$L_{\mathrm{ht}}$-SVM扩展到多视图学习，通过添加结构信息和视图权重，得到Mv$L_{\mathrm{ht}}$-SVM，该方法同时遵循共识原则和互补原则。在合成数据集、真实数据集和图像数据集上的实验表明，$L_{\mathrm{ht}}$-SVM在更高的精度、更少的支持向量和更好的噪声鲁棒性方面优于五种单视图方法，而Mv$L_{\mathrm{ht}}$-SVM在准确率、精确率、召回率和F1分数上优于六种多视图方法。

</details>


### [167] [Consistency Training Along the Transformer Stack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05817)
*Sukrati Gautam,Neil Shah,Arav Dhoot,Bryan Maruyama,Caroline Wei,Rohan Kapoor,Robert Sidey,Prakhar Gupta,Zi Cheng Huang,David Demitri Africa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文扩展了一致性训练（Consistency Training）在安全对齐中的应用，提出了两种新的内部一致性目标（MLPCT和AttCT），并在多种安全威胁场景下验证了其有效性，发现跨威胁泛化能力及不同方法间的机制差异。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的一致性训练主要针对谄媚和越狱场景，本文希望将其推广到更广泛的模型病理（如角色上下文学习攻击、对抗性挫败、预填充攻击、条件性失调），探索一致性训练作为统一防御框架的潜力。

Method: 提出了两种新的一致性训练目标：MLP一致性训练（MLPCT，匹配后激活MLP状态）和注意力一致性训练（AttCT，匹配每头注意力分布）；并在四个新的安全威胁上应用一致性训练，同时分析其跨威胁泛化能力和机制差异。

Result: 一致性训练在多个模型和威胁场景下显著减少了失调，效果超越了仅针对谄媚和越狱的研究；发现了跨威胁泛化现象（对一种失败模式的训练能提升对另一种的鲁棒性）；揭示了ACT、MLPCT和AttCT共享残差流机制，而BCT机制不同。

Conclusion: 一致性训练是一个灵活可扩展的对齐框架，能够统一防御更广泛类别的模型病理行为。

Original Abstract: Consistency training encourages models to behave similarly across different contexts, and has shown promise for reducing misalignment. We broaden the scope of consistency training in two ways. First, we introduce two new internal consistency targets: MLP Consistency Training (MLPCT), which matches post-activation MLP states, and Attention Consistency Training (AttCT), which matches per-head attention distributions. Second, we apply consistency training to four additional safety threats: persona in-context learning attacks, adversarial frustration, prefill attacks, and conditional misalignment. Across several models and threat settings, we find that consistency training reduces misalignment well beyond the sycophancy and jailbreak settings studied in prior work. We also find cases of cross-threat generalization, where training against one failure mode improves robustness to another, and identify a shared residual-stream mechanism underlying ACT, MLPCT, and AttCT, while distinguishing BCT as mechanistically distinct. Our results suggest that consistency training is a flexible and extensible framework for alignment, capable of unifying defenses against a broader class of model pathologies.

中文摘要翻译: 一致性训练鼓励模型在不同上下文中表现相似，并在减少失调方面展现出潜力。我们从两个方向扩展了一致性训练的范围。首先，我们引入了两种新的内部一致性目标：MLP一致性训练（MLPCT），它匹配后激活的MLP状态；以及注意力一致性训练（AttCT），它匹配每头的注意力分布。其次，我们将一致性训练应用于四种额外的安全威胁：角色上下文学习攻击、对抗性挫败、预填充攻击和条件性失调。在多个模型和威胁场景下，我们发现一致性训练在减少失调方面的效果远超先前研究中仅针对谄媚和越狱设置的结果。我们还发现了跨威胁泛化的案例——针对一种失败模式的训练能提升对另一种模式的鲁棒性——并识别出ACT、MLPCT和AttCT背后共享的残差流机制，同时区分出BCT在机制上的独特性。我们的结果表明，一致性训练是一个灵活且可扩展的对齐框架，能够统一防御更广泛类别的模型病理行为。

</details>


### [168] [GenAutoML: An Agentic Framework for Dynamic Architecture Generation and Optimization in Time-Series Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05860)
*Oleeviya Babu Poikarayil,Cédric Schockaert,Abdulrahman Nahhas,Christian Daase,Mursal Dawodi,Jawid Ahmad Baktash*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GenAutoML 是一个利用大语言模型作为神经架构师的自动化机器学习框架，能够根据自然语言需求动态生成适配数据特征的超轻量级时序预测与异常检测网络，其中 WaveInterferenceNet 在 ETTh1、ETTm1 和 Weather 基准上实现了低于 0.01 ms/样本的推理延迟，适用于资源受限的边缘 AI 场景。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统 AutoML 系统依赖静态预定义的搜索空间，难以适应多样的数据特征，且设计时序预测与异常检测的神经架构需要大量领域专业知识与资源投入，缺乏高效、自适应的自动化方法。

Method: 提出 GenAutoML 框架，核心包括：(1) 利用大语言模型作为神经架构师，将自然语言需求转化为可执行的 PyTorch 代码；(2) Sandboxed Reflection Loop（沙盒反射循环）实现自主代码优化；(3) Signature-Aware Runtime（签名感知运行时）确保架构一致性与执行安全性；(4) 引入 Dynamic Reversible Instance Normalization (Dyn-RevIN) 包装器提升非平稳条件下的鲁棒性。

Result: 在 ETTh1、ETTm1 和 Weather 基准上，GenAutoML 能够动态生成适配数据特性的任务特定架构。其中 WaveInterferenceNet 在保持有竞争力预测性能的同时，推理延迟低于 0.01 ms/样本。

Conclusion: GenAutoML 通过强调计算效率、架构适应性和稳定的优化行为，能够创建适用于资源受限和延迟敏感的边缘 AI 部署的超轻量级神经网络，有效解决了传统 AutoML 在时序任务中的适应性不足问题。

Original Abstract: Designing neural architectures for time-series forecasting and anomaly detection remains a resource-intensive task that often requires substantial domain expertise. Traditional Automated Machine Learning (AutoML) systems typically rely on static, predefined search spaces, limiting their ability to adapt to diverse data characteristics. We present GenAutoML, an agentic framework that leverages Large Language Models (LLMs) as neural architects to bridge natural-language requirements and executable PyTorch implementations. The framework incorporates a Sandboxed Reflection Loop for autonomous code refinement and a Signature-Aware Runtime that enforces architectural consistency and execution safety. To improve robustness under non-stationary conditions, we further introduce a Dynamic Reversible Instance Normalization (Dyn-RevIN) wrapper. Experiments on the ETTh1, ETTm1, and Weather benchmarks demonstrate that GenAutoML can dynamically generate task-specific neural architectures tailored to dataset characteristics. Among the generated models, WaveInterferenceNet achieves inference latency below 0.01 ms per sample while maintaining competitive predictive performance. By emphasizing computational efficiency, architectural adaptability, and stable optimization behavior, GenAutoML enables the creation of ultra-lightweight neural networks suitable for resource-constrained and latency-sensitive Edge AI deployments.

中文摘要翻译: 设计用于时间序列预测和异常检测的神经架构仍然是一项资源密集型的任务，通常需要大量领域专业知识。传统的自动机器学习系统通常依赖静态、预定义的搜索空间，限制了其适应多样化数据特征的能力。我们提出 GenAutoML，一个基于智能体的框架，利用大语言模型作为神经架构师，在自然语言需求与可执行的 PyTorch 实现之间建立桥梁。该框架包含一个沙盒反射循环用于自主代码优化，以及一个签名感知运行时用于保障架构一致性和执行安全。为进一步提升非平稳条件下的鲁棒性，我们引入了一种动态可逆实例归一化包装器。在 ETTh1、ETTm1 和 Weather 基准上的实验表明，GenAutoML 能够动态生成针对数据集特性定制的任务特定神经架构。在生成的模型中，WaveInterferenceNet 在保持有竞争力预测性能的同时，实现了低于每样本 0.01 毫秒的推理延迟。通过强调计算效率、架构适应性和稳定的优化行为，GenAutoML 能够创建适用于资源受限和延迟敏感的边缘 AI 部署的超轻量级神经网络。

</details>


### [169] [Deciphering Two Training Clocks in Grokking via Deep Linear Network Theory with Conditional ReLU Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05863)
*Hu Tan,Kuo Gai,Shihua Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"两个训练时钟"框架，形式化地描述了深度学习中的"Grokking"现象——分类损失的快速衰减与表征的缓慢简化发生在不同的时间尺度上。在线性网络和ReLU网络中分别证明了这一分离机制，并通过模加法实验进行了验证。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: Grokking现象表明，拟合训练数据与学习底层简单规则可能发生在不同的时间尺度上，但缺乏形式化的理论解释。作者希望从理论上分离并刻画这两个过程的时间尺度差异。

Method: 1) 提出"两个训练时钟"框架，用停时对分别表示分类损失快速衰减和表征简化的时间尺度。2) 对深度线性网络，分析后间隔间隙增长或一步尾收缩条件，证明交叉熵损失在对数时间尺度上降至ε。3) 引入层间权重衰减，证明其正则化效应等价于Schatten型惩罚，在Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz尾部条件下结构能量在多项式时间尺度上衰减。4) 对ReLU MLP，在训练集激活模式固定时网络退化为活跃坐标上的线性模型。5) 对两层ReLU嵌入模型，通过链式法则估计证明分类头比嵌入块获得更大的有效梯度。

Result: 1) 深度线性网络中，分类损失在对数时间尺度上快速衰减，而表征简化（结构能量）在多项式时间尺度上缓慢进行，两者时间尺度分离。2) ReLU网络中，当激活模式固定时，网络退化为线性模型，支持两阶段机制。3) 两层ReLU嵌入模型中，分类头接收的有效梯度大于嵌入块，支持"分类器先拟合，表征后简化"的两阶段机制。4) 模加法实验验证了理论分析。

Conclusion: 深度学习中的Grokking现象可以归结为"两个训练时钟"——拟合与表征简化发生在不同的时间尺度上。线性网络提供了严格的理论核心，ReLU网络的分析以条件归约的形式解释了实验现象，为理解泛化与过拟合的时间动力学提供了理论框架。

Original Abstract: Grokking suggests that fitting the training data and learning a simple underlying rule may occur on different time scales. We formalize this phenomenon by separating the fast decay of the classification loss from the slower simplification of the learned representation, and we call the resulting pair of stopping times two training clocks. For deep linear networks, we show that a post-margin gap-growth or one-step tail-contraction condition reduces the cross-entropy loss to level epsilon on a logarithmic time scale. In contrast, when layerwise weight decay is present, the induced regularization on the end-to-end map can be expressed as a Schatten-type penalty; under a sharp late-time Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz tail, this structural energy closes on a polynomial time scale. The two clocks, therefore, separate fitting from representation simplification. We then explain how the same mechanism can appear in ReLU MLPs. In regions where the activation patterns on the training set remain fixed, the network reduces to a linear model in the active coordinates. In a two-layer ReLU embedding model, chain-rule estimates further show that the classifier head can receive larger effective gradients than the embedding block under controlled downstream norms. This supports a two-stage mechanism in which the classifier fits first, while the representation continues to simplify later. We use modular addition as the main experimental setting. The deep linear theory provides the rigorous core of the analysis. But the ReLU results are formulated as conditional reductions that account for empirical behavior without claiming a global proof for nonlinear training dynamics.

中文摘要翻译: Grokking现象表明，拟合训练数据与学习底层简单规则可能发生在不同的时间尺度上。我们通过将分类损失的快速衰减与学习表征的缓慢简化相分离来形式化这一现象，并将由此产生的停时对称为"两个训练时钟"。对于深度线性网络，我们证明了后间隔间隙增长或一步尾收缩条件可将交叉熵损失在对数时间尺度上降至ε水平。相比之下，当存在层间权重衰减时，对端到端映射的诱导正则化可以表示为Schatten型惩罚；在尖锐的晚期Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz尾部条件下，这种结构能量在多项式时间尺度上闭合。因此，两个时钟将拟合过程与表征简化过程相分离。随后我们解释了相同机制如何在ReLU MLP中出现。在训练集上的激活模式保持固定的区域中，网络退化为活跃坐标上的线性模型。在一个两层ReLU嵌入模型中，链式法则估计进一步表明，在受控的下游范数条件下，分类头可以比嵌入块获得更大的有效梯度。这支持了一个两阶段机制：分类器先拟合，而表征随后继续简化。我们以模加法作为主要实验设置。深度线性理论提供了分析的核心严格基础。但ReLU结果以条件归约的形式表述，用于解释实验行为，而不声称对非线性训练动力学提供全局性证明。

</details>


### [170] [TS-ICL: A Flexible Time-Indexed Foundation Model for Time Series via In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05878)
*Etienne Le Naour,Tahar Nabil,Adrien Petralia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TS-ICL，一种基于概率上下文学习的编码器-回归器Transformer模型，统一了时间序列预测与插补任务，在插补任务上达到新最优水平，同时在预测任务上保持竞争力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有时间序列基础模型主要聚焦于预测任务，但真实世界的时间序列数据往往不规则、部分观测，需要模型能够同时处理预测、缺失值插补以及退化的采样条件。

Method: 提出TS-ICL模型，采用概率上下文学习（In-Context Learning）的编码器-回归器Transformer架构；将时间序列任务形式化为时间戳对齐的回归问题；通过新颖的因果数据先验生成合成依赖结构来自然融入协变量信息。

Result: 在插补任务上达到新的最优水平；在单变量和含协变量的基准测试中，与领先的预测基础模型保持竞争力；在部分观测的回看窗口预测任务中表现尤为出色。

Conclusion: TS-ICL成功统一了预测和插补任务，为处理真实世界中不规则观测的时间序列数据提供了有效的通用解决方案。

Original Abstract: Foundation models mark a profound paradigm shift in time series modeling, with task-specific models being superseded by general-purpose zero-shot models. Yet, current approaches primarily focus on forecasting, while real-world time series are often irregularly and partially observed, requiring models that can jointly forecast, impute missing values, and handle degraded sampling conditions. To address these challenges, we introduce TS-ICL, a novel probabilistic In-Context Learning encoder--regressor Transformer that unifies forecasting and imputation. TS-ICL formulates time series tasks as timestamp-aligned regression and naturally incorporates covariates by training on synthetic dependency structures generated from a novel causal data prior. Empirically, TS-ICL achieves a new state-of-the-art in imputation, while remaining competitive with leading forecasting foundation models across both univariate and covariate-aware benchmarks. It shows particularly strong performance in forecasting with partially observed look-back windows.

中文摘要翻译: 基础模型标志着时间序列建模中一个深刻的范式转变，通用零样本模型正在取代特定任务模型。然而，当前的方法主要集中在预测上，而现实世界中的时间序列数据往往是不规则且部分观测的，需要模型能够联合进行预测、缺失值插补以及处理退化的采样条件。为应对这些挑战，我们提出了TS-ICL，一种新颖的基于概率上下文学习的编码器-回归器Transformer，统一了预测与插补任务。TS-ICL将时间序列任务形式化为时间戳对齐的回归问题，并通过在从新颖的因果数据先验生成的合成依赖结构上进行训练，自然地融入协变量。实验结果表明，TS-ICL在插补任务上达到了新的最优水平，同时在单变量和含协变量的基准测试中与领先的预测基础模型保持竞争力。在部分观测的回看窗口预测任务中，它表现出尤为强劲的性能。

</details>


### [171] [When Denser Credit Is Not Enough: Evidence-Calibrated Policy Optimization for Long-Horizon LLM Agent Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05885)
*Yuanfan Li,Qi Zhou,Wenjing Duan,Lu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出证据校准策略优化（ECPO），一种无需评论家的策略优化算法，通过证据校准动作优势和方差门控信用加权来解决长程LLM智能体中稀疏奖励下的信用分配问题，在ALFWorld和WebShop上超越现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于分组的策略优化方法（如GiGPO）虽然通过锚点状态构建了步骤级优势，但在有限rollout下，罕见但幸运的动作可能获得过大的优势估计，导致锚点偏差和后期训练震荡，即密集信用分配在统计上不可靠。

Method: ECPO包含两个核心组件：(1) 证据校准动作优势（Evidence-Calibrated Action Advantage），将rollout按规范动作分组并对低计数估计进行收缩；(2) 方差门控信用加权（Variance-Gated Credit Weighting），抑制由动作内噪声主导的锚点状态。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop上使用Qwen2.5-1.5B/7B进行实验，ECPO持续优于强基线，在Qwen2.5-1.5B上相比GiGPO在ALFWorld/WebShop上分别提升+5.2/+7.3个成功点，且仅增加0.1%的额外优势计算开销。

Conclusion: ECPO通过校准步骤级信用分配，在几乎不增加计算开销的情况下显著提升了长程LLM智能体的策略优化性能，验证了统计可靠性对密集信用分配的重要性。

Original Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents require reinforcement learning methods that can assign credit to intermediate decisions under sparse and delayed rewards. Recent group-based methods such as GiGPO improve over GRPO by constructing step-level advantages at repeated anchor states. However, we show that such dense credit can be statistically unreliable: under limited rollouts, rare but lucky actions may receive overly large advantages, producing divergent anchor bias and late-stage training oscillation. We propose Evidence-Calibrated Policy Optimization (ECPO), a critic-free policy optimization algorithm that calibrates step-level credit before policy updates. ECPO combines Evidence-Calibrated Action Advantage, which groups rollouts by canonical actions and shrinks low-count estimates, with Variance-Gated Credit Weighting, which suppresses anchor states dominated by within-action noise. Experiments on ALFWorld and WebShop with Qwen2.5-1.5B/7B show that ECPO consistently outperforms strong baselines, improving GiGPO by +5.2/+7.3 success points on ALFWorld/WebShop with Qwen2.5-1.5B while adding only 0.1% additional advantage-computation overhead.

中文摘要翻译: 长程LLM智能体需要能够在稀疏和延迟奖励下为中间决策分配信用的强化学习方法。最近基于分组的方法（如GiGPO）通过在重复的锚点状态构建步骤级优势，改进了GRPO。然而，我们表明这种密集信用在统计上可能不可靠：在有限的rollout下，罕见但幸运的动作可能获得过大的优势，产生发散的锚点偏差和后期训练震荡。我们提出了证据校准策略优化（ECPO），一种无需评论家的策略优化算法，在策略更新前校准步骤级信用。ECPO结合了证据校准动作优势（将rollout按规范动作分组并对低计数估计进行收缩）和方差门控信用加权（抑制由动作内噪声主导的锚点状态）。在ALFWorld和WebShop上使用Qwen2.5-1.5B/7B的实验表明，ECPO持续优于强基线，在Qwen2.5-1.5B上相比GiGPO在ALFWorld/WebShop上分别提升+5.2/+7.3个成功点，且仅增加0.1%的额外优势计算开销。

</details>


### [172] [High-Dimensional Theory of LoRA Fine-Tuning in a Solvable Attention Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05899)
*O. Duranthon,F. Boncoraglio,L. Zdeborová*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了注意力模型中低秩适配（LoRA）的高维统计理论，揭示了预训练与微调之间的相互作用，通过可解框架在高温极限下得到测试误差和表示对齐的精确渐近刻画。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 理解LoRA在注意力模型中的理论基础，特别是预训练与微调之间的相互作用机制，以及预训练对LoRA性能的影响。

Method: 构建了一个可解框架：先对单头注意力层进行数据丰富的预训练，再通过秩一LoRA更新在有限数据上微调；在高维极限下用有限个序参量刻画两个阶段的渐近行为。

Result: 预训练对LoRA的影响可概括为有效噪声项；基于此推导出最优预训练过程的处方；发现了测试误差与表示质量之间存在失配的机制；提出了理论在主动微调中的应用。

Conclusion: LoRA微调的性能受预训练影响的程度可由一个有效噪声项统一描述，该理论为最优预训练和主动微调提供了理论指导。

Original Abstract: We develop a high-dimensional statistical theory of low-rank adaptation (LoRA) in attention models, capturing the interplay between pre-training and fine-tuning. We introduce a solvable framework in which a single-head attention layer is first pre-trained on a data-abundant task and subsequently adapted via a rank-one LoRA update on limited data. In the high-dimensional limit, both stages admit a sharp asymptotic characterization in terms of a finite set of order parameters, yielding explicit predictions for test errors and representation alignment. Our analysis shows that the impact of pre-training on LoRA is summarized by an effective noise term, from which we derive prescriptions for the optimal pre-training procedure. We also demonstrate a regime with a mismatch between the value of the test error and representation quality, and propose an application of our theory to active fine-tuning.

中文摘要翻译: 我们发展了注意力模型中低秩适配（LoRA）的高维统计理论，揭示了预训练与微调之间的相互作用。我们引入了一个可解框架：首先在数据丰富的任务上预训练一个单头注意力层，随后通过秩一LoRA更新在有限数据上进行适配。在高维极限下，两个阶段均可通过一组有限的序参量获得精确的渐近刻画，从而给出测试误差和表示对齐的显式预测。我们的分析表明，预训练对LoRA的影响可归纳为一个有效噪声项，由此我们推导出最优预训练过程的处方。我们还展示了测试误差与表示质量之间存在失配的机制，并提出了该理论在主动微调中的应用。

</details>


### [173] [Addressing Imbalance in Multi-Label Data via Label-Specific Distance-based Oversampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05927)
*Bin Liu,Jun Wu,Haoyu Peng,Ao Zhou,Jin Wang,QiaoSong Chen,Grigorios Tsoumakas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于标签特定距离的多标签过采样方法（LSDMLO），通过构建加权的特征空间来识别标签一致的近邻，生成更高质量的合成实例以缓解多标签分类中的类别不平衡问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有过采样方法在全局特征空间中使用欧氏距离检索近邻，忽略了不同特征对不同标签的语义相关性差异，导致近邻间标签不一致，进而引入标签混淆和过拟合问题。

Method: 提出LSDMLO方法，通过加权相关特征空间推导标签特定距离，识别标签一致的近邻；基于边界区域的种子实例生成合成新实例，使其与原始数据的标签分布对齐。

Result: 在多种基分类器下的综合实验验证了LSDMLO优于当前最先进的多标签采样方法。

Conclusion: LSDMLO通过考虑特征与标签之间的语义相关性，有效解决了多标签不平衡数据中的标签不一致问题，生成了更有用且标签正确的合成实例。

Original Abstract: The complex imbalanced label distribution poses a crucial challenge to multi-label classification, as most classifiers are biased towards the majority class and high-frequent labels. Oversampling is an efficient and flexible solution that augments instances to provide a more balanced training dataset for multi-label classifiers. Most existing oversampling methods create synthetic instances in a heuristic way that essentially relies on neighborhood information retrieved using Euclidean distance within the entire feature space. However, they fail to consider the varying semantic relevance of features to different labels, leading to label inconsistency among proximate neighbors and further introducing label confusion and overfitting to synthetic instances. To overcome the above issue, we propose a novel sampling approach called Label-Specific Distance-based Multi-Label Oversampling (LSDMLO) that creates more useful and well-labeled synthetic instances to address the imbalance in multi-label datasets. LSDMLO derives the label-specific distance to identify label-consistent neighbors based on the weighted pertinent feature space, which facilitates selecting seed instances that express more label correlations in boundary areas and generating synthetic instances aligned with the label distribution of original data. The comprehensive experiments verify that the proposed LSDMLO outperforms the state-of-the-art multi-label sampling approaches under various base classifiers.

中文摘要翻译: 复杂的非平衡标签分布对多标签分类构成了关键挑战，因为大多数分类器偏向于多数类和高频标签。过采样是一种高效且灵活的解决方案，通过增加实例为多标签分类器提供更平衡的训练数据集。现有的大多数过采样方法以启发式方式创建合成实例，其本质上依赖于在整个特征空间中使用欧氏距离检索的近邻信息。然而，这些方法未能考虑特征对不同标签的不同语义相关性，导致邻近近邻之间存在标签不一致，进而给合成实例引入标签混淆和过拟合问题。为克服上述问题，我们提出了一种新颖的采样方法，称为基于标签特定距离的多标签过采样（LSDMLO），该方法创建更有用且标签正确的合成实例，以解决多标签数据集中的不平衡问题。LSDMLO基于加权的相关特征空间推导标签特定距离，以识别标签一致的近邻，这有助于选择在边界区域中表达更多标签相关性的种子实例，并生成与原始数据标签分布对齐的合成实例。综合实验验证，所提出的LSDMLO在多种基分类器下均优于当前最先进的多标签采样方法。

</details>


### [174] [Short paper: Models in the dark -- Rectification and erasure under GDPR in ML supply chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05946)
*Henrik Graßhoff,Malte Hansen,Meiko Jensen,Sara Ramezanian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文全面调查了在机器学习模型中实施GDPR规定的更正权和删除权所面临的挑战，特别关注ML供应链中多参与方带来的问题，提出了"黑暗中的模型"这一概念，并分析了由此引发的紧迫挑战。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有工作将更正权和删除权的实施问题从法律或技术角度孤立处理，忽视了模型是在涉及多方参与者的复杂供应链中产生的，因此需要跨学科视角来弥合法律要求与技术实现之间的差距。

Method: 采用跨学科视角，通过对学术文献和数据保护机构指导文件的综合分析，对ML模型中实施更正权和删除权的挑战进行系统性调查。

Result: 研究发现许多GDPR要求在技术上尚无法在实践中实现；ML供应链中产生的问题在研究中也未得到充分解决；为此提出了"黑暗中的模型"概念，即在下游ML链中衍生出的缺乏足够透明度和可追溯性的模型。

Conclusion: 本文通过跨学科视角弥合了法律要求与ML中数据主体权利技术实现之间的差距，为可信人工智能的发展提供支持。

Original Abstract: The rights to rectification and erasure, as established under the General Data Protection Regulation (GDPR), are central to protecting individuals' privacy. However, their effective enforcement in machine learning (ML) systems remains challenging. Existing work has largely addressed these rights from either a legal or a technical perspective in isolation and disregards the fact that models are produced in complex supply chains involving multiple actors across development, distribution, and deployment. This paper presents a holistic survey of challenges in implementing the rights to rectification and erasure in ML models. Drawing on academic literature and guidance from data protection authorities, we find that many GDPR requirements cannot yet be technically met in practice. Our findings further suggest that issues arising in ML supply chains are insufficiently addressed in research. To tackle this gap, we introduce the notion of models in the dark -- derived models created further downstream in an ML chain without sufficient transparency or traceability -- and analyse the urgent challenges posed by this phenomenon. By adopting an interdisciplinary perspective, this work contributes to bridging the gap between legal requirements and the technical implementation of data subject rights in ML, ultimately supporting the development of trustworthy artificial intelligence.

中文摘要翻译: 《通用数据保护条例》（GDPR）所确立的更正权和删除权是保护个人隐私的核心。然而，在机器学习（ML）系统中有效执行这些权利仍然面临挑战。现有工作大多从法律或技术的角度孤立地处理这些权利，忽视了模型是在涉及开发、分发和部署等多个参与者的复杂供应链中产生的。本文对在ML模型中实施更正权和删除权所面临的挑战进行了全面调查。通过借鉴学术文献和数据保护机构的指导意见，我们发现许多GDPR要求在技术上尚无法在实践中实现。我们的研究进一步表明，ML供应链中产生的问题在研究中也未得到充分解决。为弥补这一空白，我们引入了"黑暗中的模型"（models in the dark）这一概念——即在ML链下游衍生的、缺乏足够透明度和可追溯性的模型——并分析了这一现象带来的紧迫挑战。通过采用跨学科视角，本文致力于弥合法律要求与ML中数据主体权利技术实现之间的差距，最终支持可信人工智能的发展。

</details>


### [175] [Dead Directions: Geometric Singular Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05957)
*Tejas Pradeep Shirodkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过“死亡方向”（dead direction）这一原始概念，将奇异学习理论（涉及贝叶斯不变量的Hironaka分解坐标）与信息几何（原始坐标、非退化假设）统一起来。核心发现是：Fisher度量的退化方向对应KL散度的消失阶数，该阶数可在原始参数坐标中通过方向Fisher曲率向奇点的衰减率恢复，无需Hironaka分解。论文将此衰减率推广到多分量交叉、多重性、奇异波动、先验RLCT偏移和温度后验，并进一步将其提升到深度网络（多层K-FAC分解、残差流、层归一化、注意力机制），通过商定理连接到规范群商流形。最后提出DDCAdam优化器，可从单个检查点的前向/后向传播读出Watanabe三元组(λ, m, ν)，无需后验采样。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 奇异学习理论与信息几何各自研究同一参数空间但使用不同词汇：前者在分解坐标下计算贝叶斯不变量，后者在原始坐标下工作但依赖过参数模型常违反的非退化假设。本文旨在桥接这两种理论，提供一个无需Hironaka分解的、基于原始参数坐标的奇异几何处理方法。

Method: (1) 引入“死亡方向”概念：Fisher度量退化的单位方向向量，等价于解析奇点集的切向量并具有确定的KL阶数。(2) 证明KL阶数可通过方向Fisher曲率向奇点的衰减率在原始坐标中恢复。(3) 建立光滑纤维上的选择规则，将衰减率转化为Watanabe的单方向对实对数规范阈值(RLCT)的贡献，并扩展到多分量交叉、多重性m、奇异波动ν、先验RLCT偏移和温度后验。(4) 将衰减率提升到深度网络：多层K-FAC分解将每个Fisher块写作激活侧与梯度侧衰减率的乘积，并应用到残差流、层归一化、注意力等现代网络原语。(5) 通过商定理将衰减率传递到度量不变群作用下的规范商流形Θ/G。(6) 构建符合G等变性的DDCAdam优化器。

Result: (1) 建立了Fisher度量退化方向与KL散度消失阶数之间的精确对应关系。(2) 在原始参数坐标中恢复了Watanabe奇异学习理论的三个核心量(λ, m, ν)。(3) 为深度网络架构（残差网络、层归一化、注意力机制）提供了闭式单方向RLCT预测。(4) 证明了SGD在规范商下具有度量不变性，标准Adam不具备，而DDCAdam具备。(5) 实现了从单个检查点的前向/后向传播直接读出(λ, m, ν)，无需后验采样的计算框架。

Conclusion: 本文通过死亡方向概念成功桥接了奇异学习理论与信息几何，建立了参数坐标下的奇异几何可操作方法，实现了对Watanabe三元组的高效计算，并为深度网络提供了架构级别的闭式预测和轨迹级别的在线诊断工具。

Original Abstract: Singular learning theory and information geometry have studied the same parameter spaces in mostly separate vocabularies: the former computes Bayesian invariants in resolved coordinates, the latter works in original coordinates under a non-degeneracy assumption that overparameterised models routinely violate. We bridge them through one primitive, the dead direction: a unit vector along which the Fisher metric degenerates, equivalently a tangent to the analytic singular set with a definite KL order, set by how fast the KL divergence vanishes. The two readings name the same vector; our central move shows its KL order is recoverable as the decay rate of the directional Fisher curvature approaching the singularity, in original parameter coordinates and without a Hironaka resolution. A selection rule on smooth fibres translates this rate into Watanabe's single-direction contribution to the real log canonical threshold, and we extend the recovery to multi-component crossings, multiplicity $m$, the singular fluctuation $ν$ (universal in the KL order for 1D directions), prior-RLCT shifts, and tempered posteriors. We then lift this rate to a deep network: a multi-layer K-FAC factorisation writes each Fisher block as a product of activation- and gradient-side rates with a duality between them, instantiated at modern-network primitives (residual streams, layer normalisation, attention). A quotient theorem carries the rate to the gauge quotient $Θ/G$ under gradient flow on a $G$-invariant metric; SGD qualifies, standard Adam does not, and we construct a $G$-equivariant Adam-family preconditioner (DDCAdam) that does. The bridge yields a parameter-coordinate handle on singular geometry, closed-form per-architecture predictions, and a trajectory-rate readout of Watanabe's triple $(λ, m, ν)$ from one checkpoint's forward and backward passes, without posterior sampling.

中文摘要翻译: 奇异学习理论与信息几何研究相同的参数空间，但使用的词汇大多不互通：前者在分解坐标下计算贝叶斯不变量，后者在非退化假设下于原始坐标中运作，而过参数化模型通常会违反该假设。我们通过一个原始概念——"死亡方向"——将它们桥接起来：该方向是沿着Fisher度量退化的单位向量，等价于解析奇点集的切向量，并具有确定的KL阶数（由KL散度消失的速度设定）。这两种解读指向同一向量；我们的核心动作表明，其KL阶数可在原始参数坐标中通过方向Fisher曲率向奇点逼近的衰减率来恢复，无需Hironaka分解。一个光滑纤维上的选择规则将该衰减率转化为Watanabe的单方向对实对数规范阈值的贡献，我们将恢复扩展到多分量交叉、多重性m、奇异波动ν（在一维方向上关于KL阶数为普适的）、先验RLCT偏移以及温度后验。随后，我们将该衰减率提升到深度网络：多层K-FAC分解将每个Fisher块写作激活侧与梯度侧衰减率的乘积，二者之间存在对偶性，并在现代网络原语（残差流、层归一化、注意力机制）上实例化。一个商定理将该衰减率传递到规范商流形Θ/G上——在G-不变度量下的梯度流框架中；SGD满足条件，标准Adam不满足，为此我们构造了一个G-等变的Adam族预条件器（DDCAdam）以满足要求。该桥接提供了参数坐标下的奇异几何处理方法、逐架构的闭式预测，以及从单个检查点的前向与反向传播中读取Watanabe三元组(λ, m, ν)的轨迹级读出方案，无需后验采样。

</details>


### [176] [Steering Vectors are an Adversarial Attack Surface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05958)
*Abzal Aidakhmetov,Donato Crisostomi,Tommaso Mencattini,Adrian Robert Minut,Iacopo Masi,Emanuele Rodolà*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一种针对激活引导管道的隐蔽数据投毒攻击：攻击者仅需替换引导数据集中4-6%的令牌，即可使生成的引导向量具备反拒绝方向，从而破解目标模型，同时保持对良性提示的引导效果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 激活引导技术无需微调即可控制大模型行为，且用户会共享数据集和预计算向量。这种开放共享的流水线存在安全隐患，攻击者可能通过数据投毒植入恶意向量。

Method: 攻击者在引导数据集中替换4-6%的令牌，使生成的引导向量与反拒绝方向对齐，从而在不影响良性提示引导效果的前提下劫持模型。

Result: 在两组开源模型族和八种模型-属性组合上，投毒向量的绝对攻击成功率（ASR）达到20-55%，比干净基线高出19-51%。拒止方向正交化防御可恢复约82%的ASR差距，且不损害良性行为。

Conclusion: 激活引导管道存在隐蔽数据投毒攻击风险，但可以通过拒止方向正交化等防御措施有效缓解。

Original Abstract: Activation steering has become a popular way to control Large Language Model (LLM) behavior without fine-tuning. Since the technique is plug-and-play, users share datasets and precomputed vectors to steer model activations. However, we show that a \emph{stealth data poisoning attack} silently compromises this pipeline. By substituting $4{-}6\%$ of tokens in the steering dataset, an attacker can silently align the resulting vector with an anti-refusal direction. This jailbreaks the target model while preserving the intended steering effect on benign prompts. Under this threat model, a malicious actor can distribute an apparently safe bundle containing texts, vectors, and weights, alongside an equivalence certificate that the end-user can verify. We test the attack on two open-weight model families and eight model-attribute combinations, observing that poisoned vectors reach an absolute attack success rate (ASR) of $20{-}55\%$, $+19\%$ to $+51\%$ over a clean reference. Finally, we find that a refusal-direction orthogonalization defense can recover ${\approx}82\%$ of the ASR gap without harming benign behavior.

中文摘要翻译: 激活引导已成为无需微调即可控制大语言模型（LLM）行为的流行技术。由于该技术即插即用，用户会共享数据集和预计算向量以引导模型激活。然而，我们证明了一种隐蔽的数据投毒攻击可以悄无声息地破坏这一流水线。通过替换引导数据集中4-6%的令牌，攻击者可以使生成的引导向量与反拒绝方向对齐。这能在破解目标模型的同时，保持对良性提示的预期引导效果。在此威胁模型下，恶意行为者可以分发看似安全的捆绑包（包含文本、向量和权重），并附带终端用户可验证的等价性证书。我们在两组开源模型族和八种模型-属性组合上测试了该攻击，观察到投毒向量达到20-55%的绝对攻击成功率（ASR），比干净基线高出19-51%。最后，我们发现拒止方向正交化防御可以恢复约82%的ASR差距，且不损害良性行为。

</details>


### [177] [LLM Explainability with Counterfactual Chains and Causal Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05972)
*Nirit Nussbaum-Hoffer,Nitay Calderon,Liat Ein-Dor,Roi Reichart*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用因果图建模LLM推理过程本身，通过四阶段方法（概念发现、状态映射、反事实数据增强、因果发现）构建可解释的因果图，揭示模型内部高层概念的因果组织方式，并在三个任务上验证了其有效性和稳定性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有工作多用LLM恢复外部世界的因果图，而本文旨在建模LLM自身推理过程中的因果结构，让利益相关者透明地看到模型如何感知和组织高层概念以产生预测，从而实现对LLM的概念级可解释性。

Method: 提出四阶段方法：(1) 发现类别判别性、人类可解释的概念；(2) 将每个输入映射为LLM感知的概念状态；(3) 引入MCMC启发的反事实增强过程，通过反事实链扩展稀疏观测数据；(4) 使用σ-CG进行稳定的因果发现，生成信息丰富且可解释的因果图。

Result: 在疾病诊断、情感分析和LLM-as-a-judge分类三个任务上评估了三个LLM，结果表明：所发现的因果图捕捉到了与LLM推理一致的、有意义的依赖关系，且MCMC反事实增强在收敛性和下游效用上表现良好。

Conclusion: 本文为LLM的概念级可解释性提供了基础性方法，证明因果图能够有效揭示LLM内部概念的因果组织方式，为理解模型推理过程提供了透明化工具。

Original Abstract: Causal graphs provide a high-level language for making mechanisms transparent. Recent work uses Large Language Models (LLMs) to recover causal graphs of external-world processes. Instead, in this paper, we use causal graphs to model LLM inference itself, providing stakeholders with a transparent view of how the model perceives and organizes high-level concepts to produce a prediction. We propose a four-phase method for constructing such graphs. Given a target LLM and a set of textual examples, our method discovers class-discriminative, human-interpretable concepts and maps each input to LLM-perceived concept states. We then introduce an MCMC-inspired counterfactual augmentation procedure that expands the sparse observational data through chains of counterfactuals. This enables stable causal discovery with $σ$-CG, yielding informative, interpretable graphs. We apply our method to three LLMs across disease diagnosis, sentiment analysis, and LLM-as-a-judge classification tasks. We evaluate the learned graphs for predictive fidelity and structural stability, and the MCMC-inspired augmentation for convergence and downstream utility. Our results show that the discovered causal graphs capture meaningful dependencies consistent with LLMs' reasoning. Together, this paper provides a foundation for concept-level explainability of LLMs.

中文摘要翻译: 因果图为揭示机制提供了高层语言。现有工作利用大语言模型（LLM）恢复外部世界过程的因果图。相反，本文使用因果图建模LLM推理过程本身，为利益相关者提供模型如何感知和组织高层概念以产生预测的透明视图。我们提出了一种四阶段方法来构建此类图。给定目标LLM和一组文本示例，我们的方法发现类别判别性的、人类可解释的概念，并将每个输入映射为LLM感知的概念状态。然后，我们引入了一种MCMC启发的反事实增强过程，通过反事实链扩展稀疏的观测数据。这使得σ-CG能够实现稳定的因果发现，生成信息丰富且可解释的图。我们将该方法应用于三个LLM，涵盖疾病诊断、情感分析和LLM-as-a-judge分类任务。我们评估了所学图的预测保真度和结构稳定性，以及MCMC启发的增强方法的收敛性和下游效用。结果表明，所发现的因果图捕捉到了与LLM推理一致的、有意义的依赖关系。综上，本文为LLM的概念级可解释性提供了基础。

</details>


### [178] [Compress-Distill: Reasoning Trace Compression for Efficient Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05988)
*Maxime Griot,Paul Steven Scotti,Tanishq Mathew Abraham*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在知识蒸馏前对推理模型的长思维链（CoT）轨迹进行后验压缩。使用两个教师模型生成的约28.3万条正确轨迹，经指令微调模型压缩至原始长度的8.6%-21.0%。实验表明，压缩轨迹可将训练token减少至原始的12%-30%，训练速度提升2.0-7.6倍，推理输出缩短3-19倍，但原始轨迹在所有规模下均保持最高下游准确率。压缩本质上是精度-效率的权衡，而非免费改进。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 推理模型产生冗长的思维链轨迹，这些轨迹在知识蒸馏时成本高昂，且会促使学生模型输出冗长内容。现有蒸馏方法直接使用原始长轨迹，导致训练和推理效率低下，亟需探索如何在不严重损失精度的情况下压缩这些轨迹。

Method: 采用两阶段方法：首先由两个大型教师模型（Qwen3.5-397B-A17B和gpt-oss-120B）生成约28.3万条正确推理轨迹，然后使用两个经过指令微调的压缩模型将这些轨迹压缩至原始字符长度的8.6%-21.0%。通过48次主网格实验和7次Qwen教师截断消融实验进行系统评估。

Result: 压缩轨迹将训练token减少至原始的12%-30%，训练加速2.0-7.6倍，推理输出长度缩短3-19倍（在较短的gpt-oss教师下压缩幅度较小）。原始轨迹在所有规模和教师模型下保持最高下游准确率。长度匹配的截断消融实验表明，模型压缩通常优于或匹配朴素截断，尤其对小规模学生更优。学生模型在压缩条件下可保留原始轨迹高达96%的准确率，并获得高达18倍的每token效率提升。

Conclusion: 推理轨迹压缩提供的是精度-效率的权衡而非免费改进：压缩可大幅提升训练和推理效率并保留大部分准确率，但无法超越原始轨迹的性能上限。在0.8B规模使用LoRA时，压缩轨迹缩小了与原始轨迹的差距但未能超越。

Original Abstract: Reasoning models produce long chain-of-thought traces that are costly to distill and encourage verbose student outputs. We study post-hoc compression of such traces before knowledge distillation. Two teachers, Qwen3.5-397B-A17B and gpt-oss-120B, generate about 283k correct traces each; two instruction-tuned models then compress them to 8.6-21.0% of their original character length. Across a 48-run main grid plus seven Qwen-teacher truncation ablations, compressed traces reduce training tokens to 12-30% of raw, speed up training by 2.0-7.6x, and shorten inference outputs by 3-19x with smaller reductions under the shorter gpt-oss teacher. However, raw traces retain the highest downstream accuracy at every scale and for both teachers. A length-matched raw-trace truncation ablation shows that compression is not merely benefiting from a smaller token budget: model-compressed traces usually beat or match naive truncation, especially for smaller students, while maintaining shorter inference outputs. Overall, reasoning-trace compression offers an accuracy-efficiency trade-off rather than a free improvement: students retain up to 96% of raw-trace accuracy while gaining up to 18x higher per-token efficiency, and at the 0.8B scale under LoRA compressed traces narrow the raw-vs-compressed gap but do not exceed raw.

中文摘要翻译: 推理模型会产生冗长的思维链轨迹，这些轨迹不仅蒸馏成本高昂，还会促使学生输出冗余内容。本文研究在知识蒸馏前对这些轨迹进行事后压缩。两个教师模型——Qwen3.5-397B-A17B和gpt-oss-120B——各生成约28.3万条正确轨迹；随后使用两个经过指令微调的模型将其压缩至原始字符长度的8.6%-21.0%。通过48次主网格实验以及七组Qwen教师截断消融实验发现：压缩轨迹将训练token减少至原始的12%-30%，训练速度提升2.0-7.6倍，推理输出缩短3-19倍（在较短的gpt-oss教师下压缩幅度较小）。然而，在所有规模和教师模型下，原始轨迹均保持最高的下游准确率。长度匹配的原始轨迹截断消融实验表明，压缩并非仅仅受益于更小的token预算：模型压缩后的轨迹通常优于或匹配朴素截断，尤其对于小规模学生，同时保持更短的推理输出。总体而言，推理轨迹压缩提供的是精度-效率的权衡而非免费改进：学生模型可保留原始轨迹高达96%的准确率，同时获得高达18倍的每token效率提升；在0.8B规模下使用LoRA时，压缩轨迹缩小了与原始轨迹的差距，但未能超越。

</details>


### [179] [Diffusion Models for Adaptive Sequential Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06007)
*Haoyang Cao,Minshuo Chen,Yinbin Han,Renyuan Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种序贯前向-后向扩散框架，用于生成具有适应性的时序数据，通过逐步注入和移除噪声并基于历史信息条件化，解决了现有扩散模型在时序生成中无法捕捉时间依赖和信息结构的问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有扩散模型在静态数据生成上表现优异，但直接扩展到时序场景时无法捕捉时间依赖性和信息结构，设计能够以适应性方式（不前瞻未来信息）模拟时序数据的扩散模型仍是一个开放挑战。

Method: 提出序贯前向-后向扩散框架：沿序列方向逐步注入和移除噪声，并基于先前生成的歷史信息进行条件化以确保适应性；引入新的分数匹配目标以实现高效并行训练；在通用框架下推导统计保证，并以ReLU网络为具体实例建立分数近似、分数估计和分布估计的理论结果。

Result: 在合成数据（ARMA模型和高斯过程）上验证了方法有效性，并展示了其在构建均值-方差最优投资组合中的应用效果。

Conclusion: 所提出的序贯扩散框架能够生成适应性的合成时序数据，在理论和实验上均表现良好，为时序数据生成提供了新范式。

Original Abstract: Generating realistic synthetic sequential data is critical in real-world applications across operations research, finance, healthcare, energy systems, and scientific computing, where time-indexed observations are used for prediction, simulation, risk assessment, and data-driven decision-making. While diffusion models have achieved remarkable success in generating static data, their direct extensions to sequential settings often fail to capture temporal dependence and information structure. Designing diffusion models that can simulate sequential data in an adapted manner, and hence without anticipation of future information, therefore remains an open challenge.
  In this work, we propose a sequential forward-backward diffusion framework for adapted time series generation. Our approach progressively injects and removes noise along the sequence, conditioning on the previously generated history to ensure adaptiveness. A novel score-matching objective is introduced for efficient parallel training. We derive rigorous statistical guarantees under a generic framework, then establish score approximation, score estimation, and distribution estimation results with ReLU networks serving as a concrete instance. Empirically, we validate our method on synthetic data, including ARMA models and Gaussian processes, and demonstrate its effectiveness in constructing mean-variance optimal portfolios.

中文摘要翻译: 生成逼真的合成序贯数据在运筹学、金融、医疗健康、能源系统和科学计算等现实应用中至关重要，这些领域使用时间索引观测值进行预测、仿真、风险评估和数据驱动决策。虽然扩散模型在静态数据生成方面取得了显著成功，但其直接扩展到序贯设置时往往无法捕捉时间依赖性和信息结构。因此，设计能够以适应性方式（即不前瞻未来信息）模拟时序数据的扩散模型仍然是一个开放挑战。本文提出了一种用于适应性时间序列生成的序贯前向-后向扩散框架。我们的方法沿序列方向逐步注入和移除噪声，并基于先前生成的历史信息进行条件化以确保适应性。引入了一种新颖的分数匹配目标以实现高效的并行训练。我们在一个通用框架下推导了严格的统计保证，然后以ReLU网络为具体实例，建立了分数近似、分数估计和分布估计的结果。在实验方面，我们在合成数据（包括ARMA模型和高斯过程）上验证了该方法，并展示了其在构建均值-方差最优投资组合中的有效性。

</details>
